### [1] [Degradation of Proton Exchange Membrane Water Electrolyzers in Accelerated Stress Tests of Dynamic Load Cycling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02389)
*Yunyi Zhang,Qingbo Gao,Jiawei Yang,Zhen Zeng,Rui Chen,Tianyou Wang,Zhizhao Che*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本研究通过加速应力测试实验探究了质子交换膜水电解（PEMWE）在波动电源下的退化行为，重点分析动态负载循环中峰值电压和循环频率两个关键参数对催化剂层结构特性与电化学性能之间复杂关系的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PEMWE是消纳间歇性可再生能源的理想技术，但其在波动电力供应条件下的退化机制尚不明确。理解动态负载关键参数（峰值电压和循环频率）对退化行为的影响，有助于设计更可靠的电解槽并优化运行策略。

Method: 实验研究采用加速应力测试（AST）方法，设计了一系列简化的AST协议来模拟可变可再生能源运行相关的动态负载波动特征，系统考察了峰值电压和循环频率两个关键动态负载参数对PEMWE性能退化的影响。

Result: 研究发现峰值电压升高会加速AST初始阶段的退化；低循环频率可通过限制各类电阻上升来缓解退化，而高循环频率主要通过增大传质阻力加剧退化，表明系统组件的退化具有频率敏感性。

Conclusion: 动态负载下的PEMWE退化行为受峰值电压和循环频率共同调控，呈现出频率敏感的退化机制，这为理解和优化PEMWE在可再生能源波动场景下的运行策略提供了实验依据。

Abstract: Proton exchange membrane water electrolysis (PEMWE) is a promising technology for harnessing intermittent renewable energy. This study experimentally investigates the degradation of PEMWE under fluctuating power supply, characterized by dynamic load cycling under accelerated stress test (AST). We focus on the effects of key parameters of dynamic loading, including the peak voltage and cycling frequency, in a set of simplified AST protocols designed to represent selected features of dynamic load fluctuations associated with variable renewable-energy operation. The results unveil the intricate relationship between the structural characteristics of the catalyst layer and the electrochemical performance. An elevated peak voltage accelerates the degradation in the initial phase of the AST. However, a low cycling frequency can mitigate the degradation by limiting the rise in various resistance forms, whereas a higher cycling frequency exacerbates the degradation primarily by increasing mass transport resistance, suggesting a frequency-sensitive deterioration of the system's components.

</details>

### [2] [Vibrational spectroscopy identifies the bond asymmetry of hexagonal diamond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00138)
*Li Zhu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理晶格动力学研究了块体六方金刚石的结构争议问题。发现弛豫后的六方金刚石的层间键比层内键长24 mÅ，该效应随多型体六方化程度变化。通过拉曼光谱与衍射数据的联合分析，排除了两种相互矛盾的现有结构精修结果，最终确定了小正键不对称性（OB−OA = +24±3 mÅ，95%置信区间），并指出1529 cm⁻¹特征峰无法归属于均匀的理想2H金刚石，HRTEM观测仍是一个未解之谜。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 六方金刚石虽已通过多种独立途径合成，但其结构仍存在争议：最近两次结构精修在两种不等价键长之差（仅相差238 mÅ尺度上的差异，即两者符号不一致）上就互不一致，且均偏离2003年的早期精修结果。作者旨在利用第一性原理晶格动力学来判别这些相互竞争的结构模型。

Method: 采用第一性原理晶格动力学方法：对六方金刚石进行结构弛豫（使用两种交换关联泛函以验证一致性），计算声子色散与拉曼光谱；利用明亮的布里渊区中心A1g振动模式作为层间键长的敏感探针（灵敏度约−2100 cm⁻¹ Å⁻¹）；联合拉曼光谱与衍射数据进行对比分析，排除不匹配的结构模型，并通过反演相位纯样品的光谱提取键长不对称性。

Result: 两种泛函均给出层间键比层内键长24 mÅ；两个已报道的精修坐标均无法重现完整实测模式谱型；唯一能匹配孪晶样品三个谱带的结构需要数十GPa的约束应力，且其晶格常数被样品自身衍射数据所排除。拉曼与衍射联合分析选取小幅正键不对称性：对相位纯样品光谱的逆变给出OB−OA=24±3 mÅ（95%区间），两个独立样品给出+33±8和+60±45 mÅ。

Conclusion: 综合拉曼光谱与X射线衍射证据，六方金刚石的真实结构应具有小幅正的键不对称性（层间键略长于层内键），此前两种争议精修结构均不正确。1529 cm⁻¹特征峰无法归因于均匀理想的2H金刚石，其起源以及局部HRTEM观测仍未解决，留作开放问题。

Abstract: Bulk hexagonal diamond has been synthesized by independent routes, but its structure remains contested: the two recent refinements disagree even on the sign of the difference between its two inequivalent bond lengths, 238~mÅ apart, and both depart from an earlier 2003 refinement. Here we test the competing structures with first-principles lattice dynamics. Relaxed hexagonal diamond has an interlayer bond \emph{longer} than the intralayer bonds by 24~mÅ in both functionals, an effect of its eclipsed conformation that scales with polytype hexagonality. The bright zone-center $A_{1g}$ mode gauges the interlayer bond at $\approx\!-2{,}100$~\icm~Å$^{-1}$, and neither refined coordinate reproduces the full pattern of measured modes. The only structure matching the twinned sample's three bands requires tens-of-gigapascals confining stress and lattice constants excluded by its own diffraction. Raman spectroscopy and diffraction jointly select a small positive bond asymmetry: inverting the spectrum of the phase-pure sample gives $\OB-\OA=24\pm3$~mÅ (95\% interval), and two determinations on separate samples give $+33\pm8$ and $+60\pm45$~mÅ. The 1{,}529~\icm{} feature cannot be assigned to homogeneous ideal 2H diamond, and the local HRTEM observation remains an open puzzle.

</details>


### [3] [Uncertainty-guided active learning for surrogate prediction of stream-finishing wear fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00593)
*Anand Kumar,Puli Saikiran,Vineet Dawara,Koushik Viswanathan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一个不确定性引导的代理模型框架，直接从几何信息预测流式精加工中工件的三个侵蚀场（法向冲击速度、切向冲击速度和粒子冲击通量），并结合Finnie磨损模型重建磨损率分布。通过深度集成估计认知不确定性并采用主动学习策略，仅用13%的方向进行DEM模拟即可达到高精度预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式精加工中，工件磨损率强烈依赖于其在旋转磨粒介质中的方位。为确定实现均匀磨损的合适方位，需要评估所有可行方位上的磨损率场。虽然离散元方法(DEM)能精确求解粒子相互作用，但对新几何体模拟数百个可行方位计算成本极高。因此需要一种计算高效的代理模型来替代昂贵的DEM仿真。

Method: 提出不确定性引导的代理模型框架：采用深度集成(deep ensemble)作为代理模型，直接从几何信息预测控制磨损的三个场——每个三角形的法向冲击速度、切向冲击速度和粒子冲击通量。通过集成成员间的分歧估计认知不确定性(epistemic uncertainty)，实现主动学习策略，仅对最不确定的方位选择性地执行DEM仿真来更新训练数据。三个预测场通过Finnie磨损模型组合重建磨损率分布。

Result: 仅使用696个可行方位中的13%进行训练，代理模型在法向冲击速度、切向冲击速度和粒子冲击通量上分别达到0.93、0.89和0.93的Spearman秩相关系数。预测不确定性校准良好，能可靠预测预测误差和重建磨损场的保真度。低不确定性方位的重建磨损场与DEM的Spearman秩相关高达0.97，且随不确定性增加以受控方式退化。

Conclusion: 该不确定性引导的代理模型框架能够以较低的计算成本准确预测流式精加工中的磨损率分布，通过主动学习和不确定性量化实现了计算效率与预测精度之间的良好平衡，为优化工件方位以实现均匀磨损提供了一条可行的途径。

Abstract: In stream finishing, the wear experienced by a workpiece depends strongly on its orientation within the rotating abrasive media. Determining suitable orientations to achieve uniform wear requires evaluating the wear-rate field over all feasible orientations. Although the discrete element method (DEM) accurately resolves particle interactions, simulating hundreds of feasible orientations for a new geometry is computationally expensive. We present an uncertainty-guided surrogate framework that predicts, directly from geometry, the three fields governing erosion: per-triangle normal impact velocity, tangential impact velocity, and particle impact flux. These fields are combined through the Finnie wear model to reconstruct the wear-rate distribution. The surrogate employs a deep ensemble whose disagreement estimates epistemic uncertainty, enabling an active-learning strategy that selectively performs DEM simulations for the most uncertain orientations. Trained using only $13\%$ of the $696$ feasible orientations, the surrogate achieves Spearman rank correlations of $0.93$, $0.89$, and $0.93$ for the normal impact velocity, tangential impact velocity, and particle impact flux, respectively. Moreover, the predicted uncertainty is well calibrated, reliably anticipating prediction error and the fidelity of the reconstructed wear field, which matches DEM with a Spearman rank correlation of up to $0.97$ for low-uncertainty orientations and degrades in a controlled manner as uncertainty increases.

</details>


### [4] [Ultrahigh Intrinsic Hole Mobilities in $M$N$_2$ ($M$= Mo and W) at Room Temperature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00699)
*Zhongjuan Han,Rong-Tian Pang,Wu Xiong,Zhonghao Xia,Jin-Jian Zhou,Jiangang He*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过层级筛选框架（第一性原理计算结合玻尔兹曼输运理论），发现MN₂（M=Mo和W）族极性半导体具有极高的本征空穴迁移率，其中1H-WN₂室温空穴迁移率超过10⁴ cm²V⁻¹s⁻¹。高性能源自极性光学声子和声学声子散射的协同抑制以及自旋-谷锁定导致的谷间散射相空间缩减，这些效应源于反常小的玻恩有效电荷、强共价N-N键及特定轨道杂化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高迁移率p型半导体对先进电子器件至关重要，但目前此类材料稀缺，需要发现和设计具有高本征空穴迁移率的新型半导体材料。

Method: 采用层级筛选框架，将第一性原理计算与玻尔兹曼输运理论相结合，系统评估MN₂（Mo和W）家族材料的空穴迁移率及其散射机制。

Result: 鉴定出MN₂家族为具有极高本征空穴迁移率的极性半导体，1H-WN₂室温空穴迁移率超过10⁴ cm²V⁻¹s⁻¹。机理为极性光学声子与声学声子散射的协同抑制、自旋-谷锁定减少谷间散射，以及反常小的玻恩有效电荷、强共价N-N键和N-2p/W-5d轨道杂化。

Conclusion: 确立了MoN₂和WN₂作为高迁移率极性半导体的有前景材料类别，提出了一种基于晶体结构设计来同时抑制多种电子-声子散射通道的策略，为高迁移率材料设计原则提供了新的修订方向。

Abstract: High-mobility $p$-type semiconductors are essential for advanced electronic devices but remain scarce. Here, using a hierarchical screening framework that combines first-principles calculations with Boltzmann transport theory, we identify $M$N$_2$ ($M$= Mo and W) family as polar semiconductors with exceptionally high intrinsic hole mobilities. In particular, 1H-WN$_2$ exhibits a room-temperature hole mobility exceeding $10^{4}$~$\mathrm{cm^2\,V^{-1}\,s^{-1}}$. This exceptional transport performance arises from the synergistic suppression of polar-optical-phonon and acoustic-phonon scattering, together with a reduced intervalley-scattering phase space induced by spin--valley locking. These effects arise from anomalously small Born effective charges, strong covalent N--N bonds, and orbital hybridization between N-$2p_x$/$2p_y$ and W-$5d_{xy}$/$5d_{x^2-y^2}$ in the N$_2$-dimer-based structure. Our results establish MoN$_2$ and WN$_2$ as a promising class of high-mobility polar semiconductors and introduce a crystal-structure-based strategy for concurrently suppressing multiple electron--phonon scattering channels, thereby revising design principles for high-mobility materials.

</details>


### [5] [Atomic-scale reactivity of the CeO$_2$~(100) surface reconstructions by scanning probe microscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00704)
*Kyungmin Kim,Manuel González Lastre,Estefanía Fernández-Villanueva,Pablo Pou,Hossein Sepehri-Amin,Masayuki Abe,Shigeki Kawai,M. Verónica Ganduglia-Pirovano,Ruben Perez,Oscar Custance*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过结合STM/AFM实验（使用氧末端探针）与第一性原理计算，系统研究了CeO₂(100)表面的原子尺度结构和反应性，揭示了CeO₄-金字塔端接(2×2)和氧端接c(2×2)两种重构的形貌与电子结构特征，并明确区分了STM和AFM在识别表面原子种类与还原态上的各自局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CeO₂(100)表面因其内在极性和低稳定性导致复杂的表面重构，其原子尺度的结构与反应性长期缺乏清晰认识，阻碍了对该重要催化材料表面的深入理解。

Method: 结合扫描隧道显微镜（STM）和原子力显微镜（AFM）实验，使用氧末端探针，辅以第一性原理密度泛函理论（DFT）计算和AFM模拟，对CeO₂(100)薄膜的两种重构表面进行系统表征。

Result: 识别了CeO₄-金字塔端接(2×2)和氧端接c(2×2)两种重构的STM图案；AFM成像与力谱直接鉴别了暴露表面原子种类并发现吸附相关扰动；DFT和AFM模拟再现了主要实验观察，表明氧端接表面的探针相互作用由短程Pauli排斥主导，而CeO₄端接表面受静电贡献显著影响。STM对CeO₄端接表面无法可靠区分氧化与还原态，但对氧端接表面可敏感探测还原态及Ce³⁺/Ce⁴⁺分布。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了连接表面重构、原子位点指认、局部力响应与吸附质敏感性的实验-理论综合框架，为理解复杂氧化物表面提供了方法论基础，并指明了简单探针模型在强波纹极性氧化物表面的适用极限。

Abstract: The atomic-scale structure and reactivity of the CeO$_2$(100) surface remain poorly understood because its intrinsic polarity and low stability lead to complex reconstructions. Here, we combine scanning tunneling microscopy (STM) and atomic force microscopy (AFM) experiments using oxygen-terminated probes with first-principles modeling to investigateCeO$_2$(100) films at the atomic scale. STM revealsthe patterns associated with the CeO$_4$-pyramid-terminated (2$\times$2) and oxygen-terminated c(2$\times$2) reconstructions, the latter forming small patches at step edges. AFM imaging andforce spectroscopy provide direct identification of the exposed surface atomic species and revealfeatures compatible with adsorbate-related perturbations. Density functional theoryand AFM simulations reproduce the main experimental observations and show that the interaction with the probe isgoverned by the short-range Pauli repulsion for the oxygen-terminated surface, while that for the CeO$_4$-terminated surface is markedly affected by electrostatic contributions, highlighting the limits of simple probe models for describing copper-oxide probes on strongly corrugated polar oxide surfaces. For CeO$_4$-terminated surfaces, STM alone is not a robust fingerprint of the local reduction state, as STM simulations of the oxidized and reduced surfaces yield a similar apparent contrast due to the interplay between the vacuum decay of the local density of states and the highly corrugated surface geometry. In contrast, for the oxygen-terminated reconstruction, STM is sensitive to the reduced state and provides information on the distribution of Ce$^{3+}$ and Ce$^{4+}$ ions in the first Ce layer. These results establish an experimental-theoretical framework that connects surface reconstruction, atomic-site assignment, local force response, and adsorbate sensitivity on complex oxide surfaces.

</details>


### [6] [Conditional grain-graph diffusion for property-guided inverse design of polycrystalline microstructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00707)
*Yuheng Zhou,Xiao Shang,Huicong Chen,Yu Zou*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种条件图扩散框架，用于双相Ti-6Al-4V微结构的性能引导逆向设计。通过增强晶粒图神经网络作为正向代理模型预测应力，结合条件扩散模型在指定α相体积分数、弹性模量和屈服应力代理目标下生成候选微结构。生成的候选在四个目标区域及包络外目标下均能接近目标性能，BOR感知排序将BOR一致性提升最高56.4%，有限元验证最大绝对相对误差仅1.0%，且相比随机搜索和进化优化减少了约三个数量级的候选评估次数和约两个数量级的运行时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多晶微结构对材料宏观性能起决定性作用，但传统的微结构逆向设计依赖于大量试错或昂贵的模拟，缺乏高效且能保留晶粒拓扑和晶界信息的生成框架。现有方法难以在复杂性能约束下快速生成满足目标的高质量候选微结构。

Method: (1) 构建增强晶粒图神经网络（GNN），引入晶界边特征、可学习节点/边嵌入和多统计量池化，作为应力预测的正向代理模型；(2) 设计条件图扩散模型，通过反向扩散过程在α相体积分数、弹性模量和屈服应力代理目标的约束下生成候选；(3) 提出BOR感知排序机制，基于Burgers取向关系偏离度对生成的候选进行排序筛选，以提升晶体学一致性；(4) 采用有限元分析对最佳候选进行验证。

Result: 生成的候选在四个目标区域及包络外目标下均能接近目标性能；BOR感知排序将平均BOR一致性提升最高44.9%（包络内）和56.4%（包络外），同时保持性能对齐；五个最佳候选的有限元验证显示平均性能最大绝对相对误差为1.0%；扩散方法每次输入图仅需32次候选评估，相比随机搜索和进化优化的约40,000次大幅减少，运行时间降低约两个数量级。

Conclusion: 条件晶粒图扩散被确立为一种高效的性能引导多晶微结构设计框架，能够在保持材料晶体学一致性的同时，以极低的计算成本生成满足目标性能的微结构候选，为钛合金微结构的逆向设计提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Graph representations compactly encode polycrystalline microstructures while retaining grain topology and grain boundary information. We present a conditional graph diffusion framework for property-guided inverse design of dual-phase Ti-6Al-4V microstructures. An enhanced grain graph neural network (GNN) with grain boundary edge features, learnable node and edge embeddings, and multi-statistic pooling serves as a forward surrogate for stress prediction and candidate evaluation. The conditional diffusion model generates candidates through reverse diffusion under prescribed α-phase volume fraction, elastic modulus, and yield-stress proxy targets. Across four target regimes and independently seeded starting sets, generated candidates consistently approach the prescribed properties, including a target outside the property envelope of the existing microstructures. Local crystallographic consistency is evaluated post-generation from deviations from the Burgers orientation relationship (BOR). BOR-aware ranking increases mean BOR consistency by up to 44.9% and 56.4% for the in- and out-of-envelope targets, respectively, while maintaining property alignment. Finite element validation of the five best candidates in each primary design case yields a maximum absolute relative error of 1.0% in their mean properties. In a representative benchmark, diffusion requires 32 candidate evaluations per input graph, compared with approximately 40,000 for random search and evolutionary optimization, and reduces runtime by approximately two orders of magnitude in the tested implementations. These results establish conditional grain-graph diffusion as an efficient framework for property-guided polycrystalline microstructure design.

</details>


### [7] [A Large-scale Parallel Implementation of Quasi-Four-Component Relativistic Density Functional Theory with Numeric Atom-centered Orbitals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00773)
*Wentao Zhang,Rundong Zhao,Volker Blum*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向分子和周期性固体的全相对论密度泛函理论（DFT）大规模并行实现，采用准四分量（Q4C）方法和数值原子中心轨道基组。通过非均匀实空间积分网格上的域分解方法实现Q4C哈密顿矩阵元的阶N积分，结合分布式内存和计算并行操作，并使用ELPA特征值求解器库处理广义特征值问题。实测可扩展至每晶胞3,383个原子（216,628个基函数），并用掺杂混合有机-无机钙钛矿进行了演示验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: The Tkatchenko-Scheffler (TS) pairwise method to calculate dispersion interactions is a widely used approach to incorporate missing long-range van der Waals contributions in semilocal and hybrid density functional calculations. Despite numerous refinements of the approach to include many-body terms, the original formulation still remains highly relevant as an efficient and robust method, especially for organic and/or insulating materials. In 2018, Fedorov et al. reported updated van der Waals radii to the seminal work published in 2009. The present work examines the accuracy of the TS method with updated van der Waals radii (abbreviated as TS_2018), coupled with the semilocal Perdew-Burke-Ernzerhof density functional, for structural predictions of semiconducting and insulating materials in comparison to the non-local many-body dispersion method and the original TS method (TS_2009). Special attention is paid to materials containing alkali elements, for which the TS_2009 method exhibits a large overbinding, associated with potentially large errors in predicted atomic structures. We also consider a more narrow reformulation (TS_alkali) where only the the alkali atoms are corrected, so the method remains otherwise compatible with TS_2009. The binding energy curves of five alkali dimers are used to assess the TS_2009 and the TS_2018 methods in comparison to the random phase approximation. Using 45 inorganic solid compounds with available experimental reference data, as well as three widely studied, Cs-containing halide perovskites, CsPb$X_3$ ($X$ = Cl, Br, I), we then examine the performance of the TS_2018 and TS_alkali approaches compared to TS_2009 and the beyond-pairwise, nonlocal many-body dispersion method; the latter found to give good results as well.

Abstract: Metallenes have appealing properties, but stabilizing them in a monolayer phase poses challenges for their synthesis. Here, armed with universal machine-learning interatomic potentials, we constructed 1620 metallene sandwich heterostructures containing 6 different sandwich layers and 45 metals. We found that transition-metal dichalcogenides, particularly MoSe2, are highly effective in stabilizing metallenes. Specifically, buckled hexagonal and honeycomb crystal lattices exhibit the greatest stability. Abstract: Complex and high entropy alloys are attracting much attention currently thanks to their mechanical and corrosion resistance properties in harsh environments, in particular needed for carbon-free energy applications. The recent development of artificial intelligence brings a new possibility for their elaboration and adjustment of their properties.

Then we describe how DIADEM - French initiative for Materials and Data science convergence - tackles the development of innovative coatings for carbon-free energy applications (nuclear, high temperature electrolysis, ...) thanks to the development of a nationwide network of synthesis and characterization platforms - the DIADEM discovery hub. We describe in particular DIADEM-2D, an AI-driven Hybrid HiPIMS/Pulsed-DC PVD process using 4 cathodes in confocal combinatorial configuration. We present the high entropy alloy determination using data from the literature for corrosion resistance in molten salt media and nuclear accidental conditions. Abstract: Two-dimensional quantum spin Hall insulators based on atomic monolayers offer a promising route toward dissipationless electronics, yet their practical use is often limited by environmental instability. Encapsulating the system with a graphene capping layer has been shown to be a reliable method to prevent oxidation and degradation. However, the confirmation of a successful encapsulation still relies on ultra-high vacuum techniques, that considerably slow the process. We use excitation-energy and polarization-dependent Raman measurements to enable an unambiguous assignment of the spectral features. Tuning the excitation energy close to the excitonic transition in pristine bismuthene, we observe a strong enhancement of the Raman response and the emergence of additional scattering peaks. In this regime, higher-order phonon features, as well as interfacial modes between bismuthene and the SiC substrate, become visible, suggesting the involvement of resonant scattering processes. Our results establish Raman micro-spectroscopy as a versatile tool for probing graphene-protected quantum materials, providing access to lattice dynamics and interlayer coupling.

Method: 采用第一性原理计算对NdNiO₃进行系统研究，分析氢掺杂后电子占据与极化子形成过程；同时对比研究不同A位离子半径的NdNiO₃与SmNiO₃在吸氢能力和质子扩散行为上的差异。

Conclusion: 研究为实验筛选强关联氧化物作为电解质材料提供了理论指导，并为提升稀土镍酸盐的质子电导率提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Nickel-based perovskites, owing to their spontaneous hydrogen uptake and the dramatic increase in resistivity upon hydrogenation, have emerged as promising candidates for proton-conducting fuel cell electrolytes. However, the mechanism of the hydrogen-induced metal-insulator transition (MIT) in rare-earth nickelates remains under debate, particularly regarding whether the doped electrons occupy Ni e$_g$ states or O 2p ligand hole states. Here, we reveal a comprehensive MIT mechanism using first-principles calculations on NdNiO$_3$: the electrons introduced by hydrogen doping occupy the O 2p ligand hole states of the Ni-O hybridized d$_8$L configuration, promoting electron-polaron formation. The resulting electron polarons, together with proton polarons, weaken the Ni-O hybridization and thereby drive the originally itinerant Ni e$_g$ electrons toward localization. This generates a local d8 (t$_{2g}$$^6$e$_g$$^2$) electronic configuration, leading to a Mott transition. In addition, we also find that compared with NdNiO$_3$, SmNiO$_3$ with a smaller A-site ionic radius more readily absorbs hydrogen but exhibits weaker proton diffusion capability. Hydrogenation promotes proton permeation along the [001] direction via the intraoctahedral transfer, whereas the overall proton diffusivity is reduced. These results provide guidance for experimental screening of strongly correlated oxides as electrolyte materials and offer theoretical insights for enhancing proton conductivity in rare-earth nickelates.

Result: 跨越Morin温度时SHG偏振各向异性从近似六重对称演变为二重对称，与易轴到易面取向转变一致。相反Neel矢量方向和相反面内磁场方向产生可区分的SHG响应。成功成像了微米尺度磁畴、Morin转变中的畴重组，以及磁场和热循环下的畴重构，包括自旋翻转转变后的残余畴变化。

Conclusion: SHG显微术被确立为一种局部探测赤铁矿薄膜中磁对称性、Neel矢量取向和磁畴演化的有效工具，为后续研究交变磁输运和对称灵敏光学响应提供了关键的微观表征手段。

Abstract: Hematite is an antiferromagnetic oxide and candidate altermagnetic insulator whose Néel order reorients from an easy-axis to an easy-plane phase at the Morin transition. Interpreting altermagnetic transport and symmetry sensitive optical responses requires knowledge of the Néel vector orientation relative to the crystal axes and of the domain structure within the probed device. Here, we show that polarization-resolved second-harmonic generation (SHG) microscopy resolves magnetic symmetry and domains in epitaxial (0001)-oriented hematite films. Across the Morin temperature, the SHG polarization anisotropy evolves from an approximately sixfold pattern consistent with the easy-axis orientation to pronounced twofold patterns consistent with the easy-plane orientation. A symmetry analysis based on magnetic-dipole and electric-quadrupole contributions reproduces this evolution. Importantly, the interference between SHG amplitudes that are odd and even under reversal of the magnetic order renders opposite Néel-vector orientations optically distinguishable. Consistently, opposite directions of an applied in-plane magnetic field produce distinct SHG responses in our experiments. Using this magnetic contrast, we image micrometer-scale domains, their reorganization across the Morin transition, and their reconfiguration under magnetic and thermal cycling, including a remanent change after cycling through the spin-flop transition. These results establish SHG microscopy as a local probe of magnetic symmetry, Néel-vector orientation, and domain evolution in hematite films.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: α-MnTe作为一种交错磁材料，在室温附近表现出巨大的反常霍尔电导和由自旋与轨道极化引起的弱铁磁性，但其大AHC值的微观起源尚不清楚，需要从对称性和能带结构角度阐明其物理机制。

Conclusion: α-MnTe的大AHC源于对称性保护的能带节点线，这些节点线与弱铁磁性存在强耦合，使得AHC可通过自旋倾斜实现磁性调谐，并可通过LD-ARPES在布里渊区边界观测到节点线的特征信号。

Abstract: Altermagnetic $α$-MnTe exhibits huge anomalous Hall conductivity (AHC) up to room-temperature together with weak ferromagnetism arising from spin and orbital polarizations. We clarify the origin of the large value of the AHC by identifying two sets of distinct symmetry-enforced nodal lines in the valence bands with Mn character, located at $k_z=0$ and $k_z=\fracπ{c}$, protected by mirror symmetry $M_z$ and glide symmetry $G_z = \{M_z\,|\,0,0,\tfrac{c}{2}\}$, respectively. Both nodal lines are energy-dependent with an approximate C$_6$ symmetry, which is reduced to an exact C$_2$ symmetry due to the presence of the Néel vector. The highest valence band exhibits a Mexican-hat dispersion, whereas the second-highest valence band exhibits an inverted Mexican-hat dispersion, with nodal lines at the crossing between the two bands. Within first-principles accuracy, we demonstrate that these nodal lines give rise to the large AHC observed experimentally and exhibit a strong interplay with the weak ferromagnetism. We further show that even a small spin canting strongly modifies the nodal lines and the AHC, making them both magnetically tunable. By disentangling the altermagnetic and ferromagnetic contributions to the AHC, the altermagnetic contribution dominates at small canting angles, while the ferromagnetic contribution becomes sizeable for larger values. Using linear dichroism in angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy, we show a signature of the nodal line at the border of the Brillouin zone.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: The charge-density wave (CDW) of bulk 1T-TaS2 is built from Star-of-David (SoD) clusters tiling a sqrt{13} x sqrt{13} superlattice, and it melts through a two-step sequence accompanied by order-of-magnitude changes in resistivity. Whether these steps proceed by collapse of the SoD amplitude or by rearrangement of the SoD lattice has remained unresolved, because the relevant dynamics occur on length and time scales beyond the reach of ab initio molecular dynamics. Here we follow the CDW transitions in real time using a machine-learning force field trained on first-principles data, giving access to 1404-atom supercells over 5 ns. The two steps are mechanistically distinct. Above 200 K, SoD clusters translate coherently by transiently dissolving and re-forming about shifted centers, a deformation-formation process that preserves the local SoD amplitude while randomizing the interlayer stacking order and nucleating domain walls. Only near 350 K does the SoD distortion itself collapse. These results provide microscopic support for the recently proposed two-step model of the CDW transition and offer a framework for interpreting light-induced hidden phases and cavity-modified transition temperatures in 1T-TaS2.

### [19] [Learning Compositional Meta-Routing for Agentic Workflows: An Executable Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00106)
*Natan Vidra,Alina Kapanova,Arun Kanhai,Spurthi Setty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个可执行的智能体路由基准测试以及一个预算感知的元路由器，该路由器能从原始任务文本中组合异构操作；在保留测试集上实现100%成功率，较静态工作流降低43%成本，但词汇泛化是主要局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体系统不仅需要决定产生什么答案，还需决定其之前应执行哪些推理和执行操作。现有路由工作大多孤立地选择模型端点、检索深度或工具，缺乏一个能将异构操作从原始任务文本中组合起来的可执行基准与路由方法。

Method: 构建包含216个训练、72个开发、108个保留测试和108个词汇偏移挑战任务的可执行基准（覆盖数据分析、冻结语料库研究和文档处理），结果经机器检查。方法上采用独立的正则化逻辑回归头预测操作概率，从词与字符特征提取信号，在开发集上进行温度缩放，并在路由成本和操作数量预算下进行贪心组合。

Result: 保留测试集上学得策略达100%成功率（静态与固定工作流为93.5%），成本较静态策略低43%；匹配的一步式路由器仅达56.5%。在未触碰的挑战集上，学得成功率降至75.9%并落后于静态路由的93.5%，但成本仍低49%，并超越一步式路由34.3个百分点。

Conclusion: 词汇泛化（而非路由执行）是该方法的主要局限。研究建立了一个可复现的测试平台和有界的概念验证，但并不声称实时LLM性能的证据。

Abstract: Agentic systems must decide not only what answer to produce, but which reasoning and execution operations should precede it. A controller may answer directly, decompose a request, retrieve evidence, execute code, delegate to a specialist, or verify an intermediate result. Existing routing work largely selects model endpoints, retrieval depth, or tools in isolation. We introduce an executable benchmark and a budget-aware meta-router that composes heterogeneous operations from raw task text. The benchmark contains 216 training, 72 development, 108 held-out test, and 108 locked lexical-shift challenge tasks across data analysis, frozen-corpus research, and document processing. Outcomes are machine checked after operations execute. Independent regularized logistic heads predict operation probabilities from word and character features, are temperature-scaled on development data, and are greedily composed under route-cost and action-count budgets. On the held-out test, the learned policy achieves 100% success versus 93.5% for strong static and fixed workflows, with 43% lower cost than the static policy; a matched learned one-shot router reaches 56.5%. On the untouched challenge split, learned success falls to 75.9% and trails static routing at 93.5%, while remaining 49% cheaper and exceeding one-shot routing by 34.3 points. The gap identifies lexical generalization, rather than route execution, as the principal limitation. These results establish a reproducible testbed and a bounded proof of concept, not evidence of live-LLM performance.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出了"智能体贝叶斯优化"（agentic Bayesian optimization）这一新范式，将LLM智能体作为BO循环的核心决策者，同时保留贝叶斯后端提供不确定性感知的优化基础。作者实现了Sara（增强自动研究智能体）和lenz（模块化BoTorch后端），实验表明Sara在无先验知识时能保持SOTA BO的可靠性、优于LLM基线，并能利用自然语言先验超越标准BO，还能在动态场景中实时重构优化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准BO依赖通用统计先验，难以利用来自自然语言、代码和文档的丰富领域先验；现有LLM-based BO方法要么将LLM固定在单一角色（代理模型、采集函数代理或配置接口），要么赋予其过宽的控制权，牺牲了BO系统探索的可靠性。作者希望让LLM智能体作为中央决策者，同时保留贝叶斯后端的不确定性感知机制，以兼顾灵活性和可靠性。

Method: 提出agentic BO范式：LLM智能体作为BO循环的中央决策者，负责配置问题、查询后端、选择并提交评估，并能在运行中修订优化策略（收紧边界、切换采集函数、提出针对性评估、甚至重新框定问题）。具体实现包括：Sara（surrogate-augmented autoresearch agent）和lenz（模块化的BoTorch后端），后者通过结构化接口供智能体检查和修改。

Result: 在合成和真实世界基准上，Sara在无先验知识情况下保持SOTA BO的可靠性，优于LLM基线，并能利用自然语言先验超越标准BO。在动态设置中，Sara能根据需求变化实时重构整个优化问题，这是标准BO此前不具备的能力。

Conclusion: agentic BO将LLM的领域知识和推理能力与BO的不确定性感知优化相结合，既保留了BO的系统探索可靠性，又能利用丰富先验和动态适应能力，是BO范式的一个重要扩展方向。

Abstract: Bayesian optimization (BO) has become the standard tool for sample-efficient optimization and owes its efficiency to uncertainty-aware search driven by generic statistical priors. Richer domain priors can improve BO in principle, but encoding them through tailored kernels or problem structure is difficult and rarely done in practice. LLMs can help sidestep this difficulty by making informal priors from natural language, code, and documentation directly available to the optimizer. However, existing LLM-based BO methods either insert the LLM into a fixed role (surrogate, acquisition proxy, or configuration interface) or hand it broad control, sacrificing the systematic exploration that makes BO reliable. We introduce agentic Bayesian optimization: a paradigm in which an LLM agent is the central decision maker in the BO loop while a Bayesian backend provides the uncertainty-aware optimization substrate. The agent configures the problem, queries the backend, selects and commits evaluations, and can revise the optimization strategy during the run by tightening bounds, switching acquisition functions, proposing targeted evaluations, or even reframing the problem following new instructions or observed evidence. We instantiate this idea in Sara, a surrogate-augmented autoresearch agent, and lenz, a modular BoTorch-based backend that the agent can inspect and modify through a structured interface. Across synthetic and real-world benchmarks, Sara preserves the reliability of state-of-the-art BO without prior knowledge, outperforms LLM-based baselines, and uses natural-language priors to improve beyond standard BO. We further demonstrate the practical value of agentic BO in dynamic settings, where Sara reconfigures the full optimization problem on the fly as requirements change, a capability not previously available in standard BO.

</details>

*Sidhdharth D. Sikka,Suyi Gao,Zehui Lu,Rongjie Lai,Shaoshuai Mou*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统轨迹优化方法在面对成对安全约束随星群规模增长而急剧增加时扩展性差，而基于学习的规划器难以跨星群规模和碎片密度泛化。需要一种既快速又可扩展的规划方案来应对日益拥挤的轨道空间。

Method: 设计了一个置换等变神经算子，将航天器、目标和碎片的分布直接映射为整个星群的碰撞感知轨迹（单次前向传播）；配合批量高斯-牛顿精化过程以强制执行精确的轨道动力学约束；训练采用自监督物理损失与针对自身rollout生成的对抗威胁相结合的方式，无需最优轨迹标签。

Result: 在10个航天器上训练后零样本泛化到1000个航天器星群（含11000+编目碎片），精度与逐智能体最优控制求解器相当；能规避碎碎片盲基线无法应对的最坏情况威胁；并将星群内部邻近度降低数倍。

Conclusion: 物理基础的算子学习为拥挤轨道中的最优控制提供了快速且可扩展的替代方案，实现了从少量航天器到大规模星群的跨规模泛化。

Abstract: Autonomous spacecraft swarms must plan fuel-efficient, collision-free maneuvers in increasingly congested orbits, yet classical trajectory optimization scales poorly as pairwise safety constraints multiply with swarm size, and learning-based planners rarely transfer across swarm sizes or debris densities. Here we introduce a permutation-equivariant neural operator that maps distributions of spacecraft, targets and debris to collision-aware trajectories for an entire swarm in a single forward pass, paired with a batched Gauss-Newton finish that enforces exact orbital dynamics. The operator is trained without optimal-trajectory labels, combining self-supervised physics objectives with adversarial threats generated against its own rollouts. Trained on ten spacecraft, it generalizes zero-shot to swarms of 1,000 amid more than 11,000 catalogued objects, matching a per-agent optimal-control solver's accuracy, evading worst-case threats that a debris-blind baseline cannot, and reducing proximity within the swarm several-fold. Physics-grounded operator learning thus offers a fast, scalable alternative to optimal control for crowded orbits.

</details>


### [22] [Agentic Graph Token Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00542)
*Zhuoyi Peng,Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种"智能体图令牌推理"框架，将图令牌化重新定义为推理过程本身的一部分：模型在每一步自主选择要编码的图视图及粒度，图编码器按需实例化相应的图令牌并将其拼接到运行上下文中，从而实现逐步推理与轨迹依赖的图令牌读取。通过三阶段训练管线（自监督阅读异构图令牌、带图令牌一致性正则化的令牌鲁棒轨迹阶段、偏好优化），模型在七个图领域评估中大幅超越基线，并能零样本迁移到未见领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图令牌的LLM图分析方法采用静态单次快照机制：模型在未见目标任务之前就编码一个预定义的图视图，且从不修订该视图，这浪费了LLM逐步推理的能力，也限制了模型对图的适应性理解。

Method: 三阶段训练管线：(i) 自监督任务训练模型读取异构图的图令牌；(ii) 结合图令牌一致性正则化器的令牌鲁棒轨迹阶段；(iii) 偏好优化，奖励图令牌证据与节点文本证据一致的轨迹。推理时模型在每个步骤自主选择图视图（节点、k跳邻域或簇）和粒度，图编码器按需生成图令牌并拼接到上下文中。

Result: 在横跨七个图领域的评估中，该模型大幅超越广泛基线，并能够在无需任何目标任务微调的情况下零样本迁移到未见领域。

Conclusion: 本工作推动基于LLM的图分析从静态图令牌编码器向图原生智能体范式转变，体现了图令牌化作为推理过程本身一部分的新思路。

Abstract: Graphs model relational data throughout science and industry, from citation networks to product co-purchase graphs. Because the nodes of many such graphs carry rich text, a growing line of work applies large language models (LLMs) to graph analysis. The most graph-native of these methods use graph tokens: a graph encoder compresses a graph view, such as a node, its k-hop neighbourhood, or a cluster, into a short block of continuous tokens that jointly encodes node attributes and topology and is read directly by the model. Existing methods, however, use graph tokens in a static single-shot manner: they encode one predefined graph view before the model has even seen the target and never revise it, leaving the model's step-by-step reasoning ability unused. We introduce agentic graph token reasoning, which recasts graph tokenization as part of the reasoning process itself. At each step, the model chooses which graph view to encode and at what granularity; a graph encoder is invoked on demand to materialise the corresponding graph tokens; and the resulting block is spliced into the running context. The model thus reasons step by step in the graph token space, and the tokens it reads are trajectory-dependent. We realise this with a three-stage training pipeline: (i) self-supervised tasks that teach the model to read heterogeneous graph tokens, (ii) a token-robust trajectory stage with a graph-token consistency regulariser, and (iii) preference optimisation that rewards trajectories in which the graph-token evidence and the node-text evidence agree. Across evaluations spanning seven graph domains, our models outperform a broad set of baselines by a large margin and transfer zero-shot to unseen domains without any per-target fine-tuning. More broadly, this work pushes LLM-based graph analysis from static graph-token encoders towards a graph-native agent paradigm.

</details>

*Viacheslav Barkov,Jonas Schmidinger,Robin Gebbers,Martin Atzmueller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了TabPFN（上下文学习表格基础模型）、CNN、Cubist、随机森林和PLSR在85个土壤光谱回归任务上的表现，发现TabPFN在全校准尺度上均优于所有经典基线，而TabPFN与PLS潜变量结合可获得最佳整体预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可见近红外（vis-NIR）和中红外（MIR）光谱技术可快速低成本预测土壤属性，但高维、强共线性的光谱数据在机器学习建模中难以转化为精确的土壤属性预测。现有研究缺乏对现代基础模型与传统回归方法在土壤光谱建模中的系统比较，需要为不同操作尺度下的校准模型选择提供实证依据。

Method: 基于公开土壤计量学基准数据集（涵盖田块尺度数字土壤制图和全球土壤光谱库）构建85个回归任务，系统比较TabPFN、CNN、Cubist、随机森林和PLSR五种模型；同时评估全光谱直接建模与经PCA、PLS潜变量降维后的特征建模两种策略的交叉组合效果。

Result: TabPFN在所有尺度上均表现最优，包括包含数万个土壤样本的大型光谱库任务；直接应用于全光谱的TabPFN已超越所有经典基线，表明显式降维并非强预测性能的必要条件；PLS被证明是对所有模型都有效的降维策略，TabPFN与PLS潜变量组合取得最佳整体预测性能。

Conclusion: 研究为不同操作尺度下的光谱校准模型选择提供了循证指导，表明PLSR的传统优势与现代表格基础模型（TabPFN）在化学计量学中具有互补性，PLS降维与TabPFN的结合是土壤光谱建模的推荐方案。

Abstract: Visible and near-infrared (vis-NIR) and mid-infrared (MIR) spectroscopy enable rapid, cost-effective prediction of soil properties. Yet, translating high-dimensional, highly collinear spectra into accurate soil property predictions remains challenging, particularly when employing machine learning. We systematically investigated regression models and dimensionality reduction approaches for spectroscopic modeling across 85 regression tasks from open benchmark datasets in pedometrics spanning field-scale digital soil mapping and a global soil spectral library. We compared an in-context learning tabular foundation model (TabPFN), a convolutional neural network (CNN), rule-based regression (Cubist), Random Forest, and partial least squares regression (PLSR) using full spectra as well as features derived from principal component analysis (PCA) and partial least squares (PLS) latent variables. TabPFN consistently delivered the best overall performance across scales, including large spectral library tasks with tens of thousands of soil samples. Notably, TabPFN applied directly to full spectra already surpassed all classical baselines, showing that explicit dimensionality reduction is not strictly required for strong performance. Further improvements were achieved through PLS, which proved to be an effective dimensionality reduction strategy for all models. Combining PLS latent variables with TabPFN yielded the best predictions overall. Our findings provide evidence-based guidance for spectroscopic calibration model selection across operational scales, demonstrating that the long-standing advantages of PLSR and modern tabular foundation models complement each other in chemometrics.

</details>


### [24] [An Embedded RISC-V Evaluation of Kolmogorov--Arnold Networks in Hard-Constrained Recurrent Physics-Informed Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00737)
*Enzo Nicolas Spotorno,Josafat Leal Filho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了硬约束循环物理信息网络（HRPINNs）中KAN与MLP作为残差分支在嵌入式RISC-V平台上的部署性能。结果表明，KAN尽管参数效率更高，但在执行速度（慢4.7-14.5倍）、能量消耗（高4.7-18.7倍）和INT8量化鲁棒性（轨迹发散早43倍）方面均显著劣于MLP，参数效率无法转化为部署成本优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作已定性分析KAN作为HRPINN残差分支在发现精度上何时可与MLP匹配或不如MLP，但尚未回答参数效率能否在真实部署中存活。本文旨在探索KAN的可学习B样条激活在标量嵌入式核心上是否具有实际的部署可行性。

Method: 使用完全相同的训练权重，在无向量扩展的RISC-V RV64GC平台（StarFive VisionFive 2, SiFive U74）上，测量KAN与MLP残差分支在闭环递归积分中的执行延迟、每积分步能量消耗，以及后训练量化下的可靠性（INT8量化）。测试涵盖两对精度匹配的模型和四组参数匹配的规模层级。

Result: 精度匹配的KAN残差分支执行速度慢8.0-13.5倍，每积分步能耗高5.6-11.3倍（最小对为3.7μJ对0.33μJ）；所有参数匹配层级范围分别为4.7-14.5倍和4.7-18.7倍。INT8量化下KAN轨迹发散比匹配MLP早高达43倍，损坏主要归因于权重量化而非输入侧节点间隔映射错误。

Conclusion: KAN报告的参数效率不能转化为标量嵌入式核心上的部署成本优势；除非采用专门的量化协同设计，否则MLP残差分支是嵌入式HRPINN部署更可靠的默认选择。

Abstract: Hard-constrained recurrent physics-informed networks (HRPINNs) embed known dynamics inside a recurrent numerical integrator and restrict a neural branch to learning only the residual dynamics that the first-principles model does not capture. Kolmogorov--Arnold Networks (KANs) have been proposed as parameter-efficient replacements for multilayer perceptrons (MLPs) in such residual branches, but their learnable B-spline activations follow a markedly different execution profile. Building on prior work that characterized when a vanilla B-spline KAN matches or underperforms an MLP as an HRPINN residual branch in discovery accuracy, this paper asks whether that parameter efficiency survives deployment. Using identical trained weights, we measured execution latency, energy per integration step, and dependability under post-training quantization in the closed recurrent loop on a RISC-V RV64GC platform without vector extensions (StarFive VisionFive~2, SiFive U74). For the two accuracy-comparable pairs, the KAN residual branch executed $13.5\times$ and $8.0\times$ slower and consumed $11.3\times$ and $5.6\times$ more energy per integration step (3.7\,$μ$J against 0.33\,$μ$J for the smallest pair); across all four parameter-matched size tiers the ranges are $4.7\times$--$14.5\times$ and $4.7\times$--$18.7\times$. Under INT8 quantization, KAN trajectories diverged up to $43\times$ earlier than matched MLPs; the damage traces to weight quantization, not to input-side knot-interval misassignment. These results indicate that the parameter efficiency reported for KANs does not transfer to deployment cost on scalar embedded cores, and that an MLP residual branch is the more dependable default for embedded HRPINN deployment unless specific quantization co-design is used.

</details>


### [25] [Paris as a 15-Minute City: An Explainable AI Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00815)
*András J. Molnáar,Csaba I. Sidló,Rita Rónai,Domonkos Rózsay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用巴黎大都会区约7万条移动轨迹，结合INSEE人口统计数据和OpenStreetMap兴趣点数据，构建步行与骑行可达性指标，检验"15分钟城市"理念与实际出行行为的关系。结果表明更高的服务设施可达性与更少的私家车出行和更多的主动出行相关，且这一关系在外围区域显著减弱。可解释机器学习方法识别出出行目的、家到工作地距离、服务可达性、车辆拥有量、公共交通订阅等为重要预测因子，并通过XAI方法验证了特征归因的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: "15分钟城市"理念强调短途步行或骑行即可获得日常服务，但其与实际观测出行行为之间的关系一直难以量化。现有研究缺乏基于大规模真实轨迹数据的实证检验，难以评估该理念在不同空间和社会经济背景下的适用性。

Method: 使用NetMob 2025数据挑战中的移动轨迹数据，经停留点分割和数据清洗后获得约7万个出行段；结合INSEE社会经济数据和OpenStreetMap兴趣点构建步行与骑行可达性指标；采用梯度提升树模型建模，并配合可解释机器学习(XAI)方法进行特征归因分析，同时检验不同变量顺序对特征归因结果的影响。

Result: 更高的兴趣点可达性与更少的私人机动化出行和更多的主动出行相关，但在外围大都市区这一关联显著减弱。梯度提升树模型一致识别出出行目的、家到工作地距离、本地服务可达性、车辆拥有量、公共交通订阅和社会经济背景为重要预测因子。短途出行中，高兴趣点密度与更低汽车使用率相关，而车辆拥有和驾照持有与更高汽车使用预测相关；服务稀疏区域中，公共交通订阅与更低的汽车依赖预测相关。

Conclusion: 研究结果与"15分钟城市"的核心假设一致，同时揭示了显著的空间和人口异质性。可解释机器学习方法能够补充传统可达性指标，为城市出行政策提供本地化的假设和实证依据。

Abstract: The 15-minute city promotes access to everyday services within a short walk or bicycle ride, but its relationship with observed mobility remains difficult to quantify. We investigate this relationship in the Paris metropolitan area using mobility trajectories from the NetMob 2025 Data Challenge, enriched with INSEE sociodemographic data and OpenStreetMap points of interest (POIs), yielding approximately 70,000 trip segments after stop-based segmentation and data cleaning.
  We construct walking- and cycling-based indicators of local service availability and examine their associations with trip duration, transport mode, and short-trip car use. Higher POI availability is associated with less private motorized travel and more active mobility, although this relationship is substantially weaker in the outer agglomeration. Gradient-boosted tree models interpreted with explainable machine-learning methods consistently identify trip purpose, home--work distance, local service availability, vehicle ownership, public-transport subscription, and sociodemographic context as important predictors. For short trips, high POI density is associated with lower car use, while car ownership and driving-licence availability are associated with higher predicted car use; where services are sparse, public-transport subscription is associated with lower predicted car dependence. Finally, explainable AI (XAI) methods are used to examine how feature attributions change under alternative assumed variable orderings.
  The results are consistent with central assumptions of the 15-minute city while revealing substantial spatial and demographic heterogeneity. They also demonstrate how explainable machine-learning methods can complement accessibility indicators and identify locally relevant hypotheses for urban-mobility policy.

</details>


### [26] [AdvPlan-Bench: Adversarial Evaluation of Structured Plan-Generation Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00832)
*Alina Kapanova,Arun Kanhai,Natan Vidra,Spurthi Setty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了AdvPlan-Bench，一个用于对抗性评估结构化计划生成智能体的离线基准，包含类型化计划、对抗响应集、选择器诊断和可追踪的前沿指标，通过BLUE-vs-RED优势、Nash-gap诊断和启发式评分准则评估计划质量，并提供了多基线的实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结构化计划生成智能体的评估方式往往将计划视为孤立的"高质量"产物，但在许多现实规划任务中，一个候选方案必须在其他智能体可以搜索响应的对抗环境中被检验。这种评估盲区促使作者设计一个对抗性评估框架。

Method: 将计划表示为带可选分支的类型化动作链；使用合成质量分数；通过BLUE-vs-RED优势和Nash-gap诊断比较对抗计划；用透明启发式评分准则评估定性约束一致性。实验涵盖150个合成场景、五种规划模板，涉及单样本响应、八候选最佳响应策略、离线LLM策略合同基线和两阶段多智能体委员会等对比设置，并进行三元评分的检敏感性研究（600条评分记录）。

Result: 八候选最佳响应策略将BLUE优势从.518降至.486，BLUE胜率从.900降至.820（对比单样本响应）；离线LLM策略合同基线达到.496 BLUE优势与.700胜率；两阶段多智能体委员会达到.509优势与.813胜率；三元评分者间一致性达.978。

Conclusion: AdvPlan-Bench不是可运行的规划器，也不提供真实世界决策质量的证据，而是一个可复现的基准工件，用于研究对抗性计划评估、响应预算敏感性、候选前沿和多智能体批评-修订轨迹。

Abstract: Structured plan-generation agents are often evaluated as if a plan has quality in isolation, yet many realistic planning tasks require asking how a candidate behaves when another agent can search for responses. We introduce AdvPlan-Bench, an offline benchmark for adversarial evaluation of structured plan-generation agents. The contribution is a general evaluation object: a typed plan, an adversarial response set, selector diagnostics, and traceable candidate-frontier metrics. AdvPlan-Bench represents plans as typed action chains with optional branches, assigns synthetic quality scores, compares opposing plans with BLUE-vs-RED advantage and Nash-gap diagnostics, and evaluates qualitative constraint coherence with a transparent heuristic rubric. In 150 synthetic scenarios spanning five planning templates, a sampled best-response policy that draws eight response candidates reduces BLUE advantage from .518 to .486 and BLUE win rate from .900 to .820 relative to a single-sample response. An offline LLM-policy contract baseline reaches .496 BLUE advantage and .700 BLUE win rate, while a two-stage multi-agent council obtains .509 BLUE advantage and .813 BLUE win rate. A three-rater rubric-sensitivity study over 600 rating records yields .978 inter-rater agreement. AdvPlan-Bench is not an operational planner and provides no evidence about real-world decision quality; it is a reproducible benchmark artifact for studying adversarial plan evaluation, response-budget sensitivity, candidate frontiers, and multi-agent critique-and-revision traces.

</details>


### [27] [AOSpec: Action and Observation Co-Speculation for Low-Latency Agent Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00881)
*Hao Mark Chen,Jinnan Guo,Wayne Luk,Hongxiang Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AOSpec是一个无损的推测执行框架，通过协同推测（co-speculation）动作和观察结果来加速大语言模型智能体与环境之间的完整循环。它引入期望值解码（EVD）优化预期时间隐藏而非命中率，并利用联合动作-状态验证（JASV）将长程动作依赖从全链预测重构为目标动作-状态验证，从而在不牺牲串行语义的前提下实现长期重叠，显著降低端到端延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型智能体越来越多地使用有状态工具，但模型生成与环境执行在每个步骤上仍保持串行化。随着解码速度加快，工具执行成为日益增长的瓶颈。现有的仅对动作或仅对观察结果的推测方法无法充分掩盖延迟：价值集中在少数慢速调用上，某些结果只能通过执行才能揭示，更长的前瞻通常需要越来越不可能的动作预测链。

Method: AOSpec提出三个核心组件：(1) 在智能体-环境全循环中进行动作和观察结果的协同推测；(2) 期望值解码（EVD），将观察推测导向具有最大期望延迟收益的结果，优化预期隐藏时间而非命中率；(3) 联合动作-状态验证（JASV），对于仅能通过执行揭示的结果，在隔离的fork中启动延迟关键的目标动作，并在复用前同时验证动作及其来源状态是否与已提交的执行一致。

Result: 在Terminal-Bench服务设置下，涵盖四个harness、五个actor模型和五种服务速度，AOSpec优于所有实际基线，平均端到端延迟降低11.8-32.5%，p99延迟最高降低42.8%。随着解码加速，其增益持续增加，且其观察模型在无需重新训练的情况下可从Terminal-Bench迁移到SWE-bench Verified。

Conclusion: AOSpec通过协同推测和JASV打破前瞻-准确率权衡，实现长程重叠而不牺牲串行语义，为加速智能体-环境循环提供了有效且可迁移的解决方案，在解码速度日益提升的背景下具有重要价值。

Abstract: Large language model agents increasingly act through stateful tools, yet model generation and environment execution remain serialized at every step. As decoding accelerates, tool execution becomes a growing bottleneck. Existing action- or observation-only speculation leaves much of this latency exposed: value is concentrated in a few slow calls, some outcomes emerge only through execution, and longer lookahead typically requires an increasingly unlikely chain of action predictions. We present AOSpec, a lossless framework that co-speculates actions and observations across the full agent-environment loop. Expected Value Decoding (EVD) directs observation speculation toward outcomes with the greatest expected latency benefit, optimizing expected time hidden rather than hit rate. For outcomes only execution can reveal, AOSpec launches latency-critical target actions in isolated forks that contain their effects, while Joint Action-State Verification (JASV) verifies both the action and its origin state against committed execution before reuse. JASV recasts long-horizon action dependency from full-chain prediction into target action-state verification, breaking the lookahead--accuracy tradeoff and unlocking long-range overlap without sacrificing serial semantics. Across Terminal-Bench serving settings spanning four harnesses, five actor models, and five serving speeds, AOSpec outperforms every practical baseline, reducing mean end-to-end latency by 11.8-32.5% and p99 latency by up to 42.8%. Its gains increase as decoding accelerates, and its observation model transfers from Terminal-Bench to SWE-bench Verified without retraining.

</details>


### [28] [Who Belongs in the Eval Set? A Capability-Taxonomy-Driven Pipeline for Curating Regression Eval Sets in Agent-Extensibility Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01004)
*Tezan Sahu,Aritra Das,Pankaj Mittal,Sudipta Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于能力分类学的回归评测集精选流水线，用于平台侧管理智能体（agent）扩展面。该流水线将客户评测集的查询投影到平台拥有的能力分类学中，输出加入/丢弃/替换/人工审查等决策，以在硬查询数量上限约束下最大化能力签名的覆盖广度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平台团队托管智能体扩展面时面临"回归经济学悖论"：每个上线的客户都会发布针对其领域调优的评测集，但平台的回归集必须在发布节奏决定的硬查询数量上限下运行。现有评测框架均为客户侧方案，基准压缩工作也将基准视为固定池而非不断流入的集合，没有已发表的工业流水线解决这一平台侧精选问题。

Method: 流水线由三个组件构成：1）分类器：通过确定性基于规范（specification）的提取与LLM语义推理的混合方式，对每个（查询，能力）对产生判定；2）调用质量（IQ）评分器：评估查询调用每个能力的彻底程度，使共享相同能力签名的新查询仍可被识别为更优测试并替换旧条目；3）整合器：通过基于规则的决策级联比较新查询与回归集在覆盖度和质量上的差异，并由保守的管理器仅建议驱逐。整套机制与分类学无关，适用于任何具有类型化能力分类学的回归评测集精选问题。

Result: 该流水线已应用于Microsoft 365 Copilot中带自定义操作的声明式智能体，能够输出每个查询的加入、丢弃、替换或人工审查决策。核心观念是健康的回归集是能够捕获最大能力签名（查询共同调用的能力组合）跨度（maximal spread of capability signatures）的最小查询集合。

Conclusion: 该方法是分类学无关的，可适用于任何拥有类型化能力分类学的回归评测集精选问题，包括那些会响应流水线所生成证据而演化的分类学。这是首个针对平台侧回归集精选问题的工业流水线。

Abstract: Platform teams hosting agent-extensibility surfaces face a regression-economics paradox: every onboarding customer ships an evaluation set tuned to their domain, but the platform's regression set must live under a hard query-count ceiling bounded by release cadence. To our knowledge, no published industrial pipeline addresses this platform-side curation problem: existing evaluation frameworks are customer-side, and benchmark-compression work treats benchmarks as fixed pools rather than streams of incoming sets. We describe a capability-taxonomy-driven curation pipeline applied to declarative agents with custom actions in Microsoft 365 Copilot. It takes an agent specification and a customer's eval set as input, projects each query into a platform-owned capability taxonomy, and outputs per-query decisions (admit, drop, swap, or human review), under the philosophy that a healthy regression set is the minimal set of queries capturing the maximal spread of capability signatures -- distinct combinations of capabilities a query exercises together. Three components instantiate this: a classifier producing per-(query, capability) verdicts via a hybrid of deterministic specification-based extraction and large-language-model (LLM) semantic inference; an Invocation Quality (IQ) rater scoring how thoroughly a query exercises each capability, so a new query sharing a signature with an existing entry can still be recognized as a better test and displace it; and a consolidator comparing incoming queries against the regression set on coverage and quality through a rule-based decision cascade, backed by a conservative curator that only suggests evictions. The mechanism is taxonomy-agnostic and applies to any regression eval-set curation problem with a typed capability taxonomy, including taxonomies that evolve in response to the very evidence the pipeline surfaces.

</details>


### [29] [When Do Surrogate Updates Improve Decisions? A Local Theory of Trajectory-Wise Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01130)
*Yuyang Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究轨迹训练（trajectory training）中代理损失（surrogate loss）与下游决策效用（decision utility）之间的不匹配问题，从理论上刻画了一步轨迹更新对代理风险和决策风险的联合影响，提出可学习性（learnability）与决策效用（decision utility）两个概念，并给出四个核心定理：一步迁移界、一阶梯度共线性条件、校准间隙对轨迹选择的遗憾界、以及逼近-校准权衡。实验在网格世界和LLM后训练中验证了理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多模型在训练时通过轨迹损失（trajectory loss）更新，但最终评估用的是下游任务奖励（downstream task reward）。这种优化目标与评估目标的不一致（mismatch）意味着代理损失下降不一定带来决策效用的提升。现有理论工作缺乏对这种"轨迹训练-决策评估"迁移过程的系统分析，尤其是跨多步更新的累积效应与可行更新空间中的可达性边界。

Method: 首先固定一个检查点（checkpoint）和受限更新空间（restricted update space），将轨迹诱导的总体代理风险下降定义为"可学习性"，将决策风险下降定义为"决策效用"。在此基础上：(1)推导一步迁移界，将二者差异分解为经过非负校准后的一阶梯度错位项与二阶曲率项，并给出路径式（pathwise）的多步累积推广；(2)证明当代理梯度非零时，对所有可达方向实现普适一阶迁移的充要条件是代理梯度与决策梯度正共线（positively collinear）；(3)证明校准间隙（calibration gap）界定了基于可学习性的轨迹选择的决策遗憾，并提出候选差分（candidate-difference）精细化方法以收紧该保证；(4)建立嵌套更新空间下的逼近-校准权衡（approximation-calibration trade-off）。最后在受控gridworld和LLM后训练任务中验证。

Result: 理论层面给出了一步与多步轨迹迁移的完整刻画（含梯度错位与曲率分解）、普适一阶迁移的充要条件（正共线性）、轨迹选择的决策遗憾界及其候选差分改进，以及嵌套空间下的逼近-校准权衡。实验层面，受控gridworld和LLM后训练实验的结果与理论预测一致。

Conclusion: 本文为轨迹训练与决策评估之间的差距提供了系统的理论框架。核心启示是：仅依赖代理损失下降（可学习性）来选轨迹并不充分，决策效用取决于代理梯度与决策梯度的对齐程度；当二者正共线时才能保证普适迁移。校准间隙与候选差分方法为轨迹选择的决策遗憾提供了可操作的量化保证，逼近-校准权衡则揭示了更新空间选择中的根本矛盾。

Abstract: A broad range of models face the mismatch where they are updated through trajectory losses but are evaluated by downstream task reward. Here, a trajectory is a training instance that induces a surrogate loss whose reduction might not track the model's decision utility update. Theoretically, we ask when one step of trajectory training reduces both population surrogate loss and decision risk, and how transfer accumulates along repeated updates. To formalize this, we first fix a checkpoint and a restricted update space, and define the reductions in population surrogate risk and decision risk induced by a trajectory as its learnability and decision utility, respectively. On this basis, our theory yields four main results. First, a one-step transfer bound separates their discrepancy into first-order gradient misalignment after nonnegative calibration and second-order curvature; and a pathwise extension accumulates the same terms over repeated updates. Second, when the accessible surrogate gradient is nonzero, universal first-order transfer over every accessible direction holds exactly when the accessible surrogate and decision gradients are positively collinear. Third, the calibration gap bounds the decision regret of learnability-based trajectory selection, while a candidate-difference refinement tightens this guarantee by retaining only directions that affect pairwise rankings. Finally, we establish an approximation--calibration trade-off across nested update spaces. Controlled gridworld and LLM post-training experiments yield results consistent with our predictions.

</details>


### [30] [Stop When Memory Suffices: Evidence-Conditioned Progressive Execution for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01285)
*Yidan Lin,Kaixiang Wang,Jiong Lou,Jie Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Router-Mem是一个面向长时程智能体记忆的证据条件渐进执行框架，通过轻量级充足性路由实现在线低延迟与高质量回答的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆系统要么压缩结构化历史以降低成本但可能丢失时间、因果或跨步骤依赖，要么进行深度检索以提高证据覆盖但带来高延迟和推理成本。核心问题是如何在保持低在线延迟的同时实现强答案质量。

Method: Router-Mem首先应用共享的低成本检索前缀获取证据，然后通过轻量级充足性路由器预测上下文是否支持提前终止（单token决策），以证据级监督和原理条件表示蒸馏进行训练。当证据不足时，复用检索命中扩展记忆块并执行更深层分析与聚合。

Result: 在AMA-Bench和BEAM上分别达到55.17%和38.77%的得分，同时相比全量记忆执行将平均推理时间降低27.3%和25.5%。

Conclusion: Router-Mem证明通过证据条件的渐进执行策略，可以在不牺牲答案质量的前提下显著降低在线推理延迟，为长时程智能体记忆提供了一种高效可行的方案。

Abstract: The continued development of LLMs toward persistent and adaptive intelligence increasingly requires long-term memory mechanisms that preserve and reuse information across interactions. Existing memory systems either compress and structure histories for efficient access or perform deep research over broader trajectories. The former lowers online cost but may omit temporal, causal, or cross-step dependencies, while the latter improves evidence coverage at substantial latency and inference cost. This raises a key question: can a memory system achieve strong answer quality while maintaining low online latency? We introduce Router-Mem, an evidence-conditioned progressive execution framework for long-horizon agent memory. Router-Mem first applies a shared low-cost retrieval prefix to obtain evidence. A lightweight sufficiency router then predicts whether the context supports early termination, which enable a single-token decision at inference time. It is trained with evidence-level supervision and rationale-conditioned representation distillation. When evidence is insufficient, Router-Mem reuses retrieval hits to expand memory blocks and perform deeper analysis and aggregation. Experiments on AMA-Bench and BEAM show that Router-Mem achieves 55.17\% and 38.77\% score while reducing average inference time by 27.3\% and 25.5\% compared with full memory execution.

</details>


### [31] [When May a Model Replace the Experiment? Audits, Licenses, and the Price of Trust in Surrogate-Driven Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01378)
*Shuangxiu,Ma,Wenhe,Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过数学分析与三个穷尽真值标注的设计任务验证，确立了机器学习代理模型在筛选候选对象时替代真实评估的安全条件：预测准确率（R²）不能作为信任的锚点，安全必须依靠架构性规则——预测可以用于提议和训练，但所有被认证的结论必须基于真实评估；代理模型可作为oracle的最小判据是秩保持而非精度，且信任需通过选择感知审计来购买。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在化学、材料科学和机器学习的设计活动中，评估候选对象的真实质量需要昂贵的代价（实验、第一性原理模拟或完整训练）。机器学习代理模型日益被用于提出候选、评分候选，甚至将自身预测反馈回搜索中当作真实测量。然而，这种做法何时安全、任何安全证书的最低成本是多少、以及何时替代确实划算，这些问题此前缺乏严格的数学分析和实证验证。

Method: 作者采用数学分析结合三个穷尽真值标注的设计任务进行验证。他们推导出"选择税"（selection tax）的上下界刻画筛选N个候选后过度预测的膨胀程度；证明安全来自架构性规则（预测可无限制用于提议和训练，但认证结论必须基于真实评估）且该规则在无任何代理假设下充分、在存在确定性自我确认失败模式下必要；推导模型可作为oracle的最小判据（秩保持而非精度）；设计选择感知审计以获得最优查询复杂度；最终通过432个代理拟合和6个任务-制度条件的实验验证审计统计量与搜索性能的相关性。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 近乎完美的R²可以兼容最差的选择（说明预测精度不能作为信任的锚点）；(2) 筛选N个候选会使选中候选的过度预测按可量化的"选择税"膨胀，且有匹配的上下界；(3) 确定性的自确认失败模式迫使认证必须基于真实评估；(4) 审计统计量在部署搜索性能上的Spearman秩相关达0.80-0.99，而R²与部署遗憾的秩相关低至0.33；(5) 受审计的筛选将认证oracle成本降低实测25倍。

Conclusion: 预测精度本身不能支撑对代理模型的信任，安全必须来自于架构性分离——预测只能用于提议与训练，认证必须依赖真实评估。信任可以通过选择感知审计来购买，且这种审计在查询复杂度上具有最优性。审计后的代理筛选能够显著降低认证成本（实测25倍），为设计活动中的代理模型使用提供了理论边界和实用指导。

Abstract: Design campaigns in chemistry, materials science, and machine learning share a bottleneck: determining how good a candidate truly is requires an expensive evaluation - an experiment, a first-principles simulation, or a full training run. Machine-learning surrogates that predict these outcomes are increasingly used not only to propose candidates but to grade them, and even to feed their own predictions back into the search as though they were measurements. Through mathematical analysis validated on three exhaustively ground-truthed design tasks, we establish when this practice is safe, what any certificate of safety must cost, and when the substitution provably pays. Predictive accuracy cannot anchor trust: near-perfect R^2 is compatible with worst-possible selections, and screening N candidates inflates the over-prediction at the selected candidate by a quantifiable "selection tax" with matching upper and lower bounds. Safety follows instead from an architectural rule - predictions may propose and train without restriction, but every certified conclusion must rest on true evaluations - which is sufficient with no assumptions on the surrogate, and necessary, since admitting predictions into certification with the standing of measurements opens a deterministic self-confirmation failure mode. We derive the minimal criterion under which a model may act as an oracle (rank preservation, not accuracy), show that trust must be purchased through selection-aware audits that are optimal in query complexity, and prove a dichotomy fixing when audited surrogates cut certified evaluation cost. Across 432 surrogate fits over six task-regime conditions, the audit statistic tracks deployed search performance at Spearman rank correlation 0.80-0.99, while the rank correlation of R^2 with deployed regret falls as low as 0.33; audited screening reduces certified oracle cost by a measured factor of 25.

</details>


### [32] [GraphIR: Architecture-Level Search States for LLM-Guided Neural Architecture Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01633)
*Zhen Liu,Wanqi Zhou,Shuanghao Bai,Yuhan Liu,Jinjun Wang,Jingwen Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GraphIR，一种面向架构感知的中间表示，通过计算骨架、突变表面和有效性包络三个互补视图补充可执行神经程序，解决LLM在代码级神经架构搜索中难以推理张量依赖和兼容约束的问题。在NAS-Dependency基准（120题、六个维度）和六个下游基准上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM可直接在可执行神经网络程序上进行神经架构搜索（NAS），但纯代码级表示缺乏架构状态信息：LLM必须从实现细节中推断张量依赖、可编辑组件和兼容性约束，导致突变（mutation）操作效率低下。

Method: 提出GraphIR，一种架构感知的中间表示，通过三个互补视图组织每个候选架构：1）计算骨架（描述张量流）；2）突变表面（暴露可编辑模块和操作）；3）有效性包络（捕获接口契约、传播形状和下游依赖）。

Result: 构建NAS-Dependency基准（120题、六个依赖推理维度），诊断显示GraphIR在识别精确生产者出现位置、追踪依赖传播、诊断接口和失败风险方面尤为有效。在包括CLRS在内的六个下游基准上取得最佳整体搜索性能，同时保持可比模型大小和良好的端到端NAS效率（集成于OpenEvolve）。

Conclusion: 突变导向的架构状态为可执行神经程序与LLM引导的架构演化之间提供了有效接口，验证了中间表示在LLM驱动NAS中的关键作用。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) enable neural architecture search (NAS) directly over executable neural network programs. However, code-level flexibility does not provide the architecture state needed for effective mutation: LLMs must infer tensor dependencies, editable components, and compatibility constraints from implementation details. To address this representation mismatch, we propose GraphIR, an architecture-aware intermediate representation that supplements executable programs with a mutation-aligned candidate state. GraphIR organizes each candidate through three complementary views: a computation skeleton describing tensor flow, a mutation surface exposing editable modules and operations, and a validity envelope capturing interface contracts, propagated shapes, and downstream dependencies. To evaluate our method, we construct NAS-Dependency, a 120-question benchmark covering six complementary dependency-reasoning dimensions. The diagnostic shows that GraphIR is particularly effective at identifying exact producer occurrences, tracing dependency propagation, and diagnosing interface and failure risks. Across six downstream benchmarks including CLRS, GraphIR achieves the best overall search performance while maintaining comparable model size and favorable end-to-end NAS efficiency when integrated into OpenEvolve. These results show that a mutation-oriented architecture state provides an effective interface between executable neural programs and LLM-guided architecture evolution.

</details>
