*Betty Xiong,Jan-Christian Huetter,Gabriele Scalia,Tommaso Biancalani,Sepideh Maleki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LLM-Guided Retrieval (LGR)框架，利用大语言模型对候选邻居药物进行排名，再通过固定均值聚合器将已测量的相关化合物响应聚合，以预测未测量药物在特定细胞系中的转录组响应。在Tahoe-100M单细胞扰动图谱的多个评估场景下，LGR一致优于药物均值、ChemCPA和基于化学结构的kNN基线，尤其在未见细胞系的泛化场景中优势显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物-细胞系组合的穷尽式实验测序不可行，而跨细胞系预测小分子扰动下的转录组响应对于药物发现至关重要。现有方法在零样本场景下泛化能力有限，因此需要一种能够利用LLM生物学先验知识进行有效检索与聚合的预测框架。

Method: 将分子扰动预测建模为"检索-聚合"任务：1）使用LLM对目标细胞系中已测序的候选药物进行排名（仅限制在目标细胞系中有谱图的药物）；2）选取排名靠前的k个生物相关化合物；3）使用固定的均值聚合器将其观测到的表达差异（expression deltas）平均，生成对未测药物的预测响应。

Result: 在Tahoe-100M单细胞扰动图谱上，LGR在未见药物、未见细胞系和开放世界三种评估状态下均优于药物均值、ChemCPA和基于化学结构的kNN基线。最大提升出现在未见细胞系泛化场景，相关性和误差指标均优于均值基线。此外，LGR在所有设置中均提升了基因调控的方向（符号）准确性，表明即使幅度指标相似，也能更好地恢复生物学上有意义的扰动效应。

Conclusion: 零样本分子扰动预测的关键驱动因素是检索质量而非预测器的复杂度。LLM在作为受约束的检索模块时，能够提供有价值的生物学先验，显著提升未测药物响应的预测性能。

Abstract: Predicting transcriptomic responses to small-molecule perturbations across cell lines is central to drug discovery, but exhaustive profiling of drug-cell combinations is infeasible. We frame molecular perturbation prediction as retrieve-and-aggregate: approximate an unmeasured drug's response in a cell line by aggregating measured responses of a small set of biologically related compounds. We propose LLM-Guided Retrieval (LGR), where a large language model (LLM) ranks candidate neighbor drugs (restricted to those profiled in the target cell line); after which a fixed mean aggregator combines their observed expression deltas to form the prediction. We evaluate on the Tahoe-100M single-cell perturbation atlas under unseen-drug, unseen-cell-line, and open-world regimes. LGR consistently improves over drug mean, ChemCPA, and chemistry-based kNN baselines, with the strongest gains for unseen cell-line generalization, where it achieves higher correlation and lower error than mean baselines. Across settings, LGR improves directional (sign) accuracy of gene regulation, indicating better recovery of biologically meaningful perturbation effects even when magnitude-based metrics are similar. These results suggest that retrieval quality, rather than predictor complexity, is a key driver of zero-shot molecular perturbation prediction, and that LLMs can provide a useful biological prior when used as constrained retrieval modules.

</details>


### [34] [Toward Plasticity-Preserving KL Regularization for Capability Retention in LLM Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01743)
*Li Wang,Xiaodong Lu,Xiaohan Wang,Jiajun Chai,Wei Lin,Tianhao Peng,Guojun Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CoKL（Correctness-Conditioned KL Regularization），一种条件化KL正则化框架，将传统全策略KL约束从整个响应分布收窄到正确性条件的响应分布，以在RL后训练中保护基座模型已有能力的同时最小化对新任务学习的干扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL已成为LLM后训练的核心范式，但优化新目标会退化基座模型已有能力。标准全策略KL正则化约束整个响应分布，可能不必要地限制探索和目标任务学习。作者希望设计更精确的约束，在保留现有能力的同时最小化对新任务学习的干扰。

Method: CoKL将正则化约束从完整输出分布收窄到正确性条件的响应分布（即分别约束正确响应和错误响应的条件分布）。以前向KL散度实例化CoKL，推导出适用于RL后训练的有限组训练目标。在总体层面，CoKL解耦了分配给正确响应的总概率与其正确性条件分布，从而在不直接锚定错误输出或总正确性质量的情况下，正则化参考支持的正确响应之间的相对概率分配。

Result: 理论分析表明全策略前向和反向KL正则化在参考策略不完美时会诱导严格的最优正确性差距（optimal correctness gap），而CoKL避免了该局限。在受控多解环境及跨多个模型规模的持续后训练实验中，CoKL比现有正则化方法在目标任务提升与先验能力保留之间取得了更有利的平衡。

Conclusion: CoKL通过条件化正则化实现了更精确的能力保留，避免了全策略KL正则化的过度约束问题，为LLM RL后训练中的能力保留提供了一种更优的替代方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a central paradigm for large language model (LLM) post-training, but optimization toward new objectives can degrade capabilities already present in the base model. KL regularization is widely used to mitigate such forgetting by constraining policy drift toward a reference model. However, standard full-policy KL regularization constrains the entire response distribution and may unnecessarily restrict exploration and target-task learning. This raises a natural question: can a more precise constraint preserve existing capabilities while minimizing interference with learning new tasks? To this end, we propose \underline{Co}rrectness-Conditioned \underline{KL} Regularization (CoKL), a conditional regularization framework that narrows the preservation constraint from the full output distribution to correctness-conditioned response distributions. We instantiate CoKL with forward KL divergence and derive a practical finite-group training objective for RL-based LLM post-training. At the population level, CoKL decouples the total probability assigned to correct responses from their correctness-conditioned distribution, thereby regularizing the relative probability allocation among reference-supported correct responses without directly anchoring incorrect outputs or total correctness mass. We further show that full-policy forward and reverse KL regularization induce a strict optimal correctness gap when the reference policy is imperfect, whereas CoKL avoids this limitation. Experiments in controlled multi-solution environments and continual post-training settings across multiple model scales demonstrate that CoKL achieves a more favorable balance between target-task improvement and prior-capability retention than existing regularization methods. Our code is available at https://github.com/Lumina04/CoKL.

</details>


### [35] [LAB-Tab: LLM-Augmented Bayesian Network Adaptation for Few-Shot Tabular Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01879)
*Zijian Shen,Taijie Chen,Bin Zhou,Ziyang Jiang,Jintao Ke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LAB-Tab框架，利用LLM增强贝叶斯网络（BN）适配，解决源域丰富的少样本表格数据生成问题。通过LLM提出目标域BN边假设扩展结构空间，再用PPO策略对边进行校准，最后采样生成目标域合成表格。在六个源-目标分布偏移场景中，以10%目标数据预算取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格数据生成在目标域数据稀缺时常用源域丰富数据辅助，但现有少样本生成器要么直接拟合稀疏目标统计量导致过拟合偶然模式，要么复用源域生成器导致保留目标域中不再成立的依赖关系。需要一个能同时利用源域信息并适配目标域分布的方案。

Method: LAB-Tab包含三步：(1) 从源数据拟合BN；(2) 利用LLM提出目标域可能存在的BN边（语义和弱统计证据转化为结构化假设），扩展可编辑边空间；(3) 用PPO策略通过边级动作（保留、削弱、增强、翻转、停用）校准增强BN中的边，奖励函数结合分布对齐、下游效用和目标相关依赖保留。最终从适配后的BN采样生成目标域合成表格。

Result: 在基于三个US Census (ACS)预测任务构建的六个源-目标分布偏移场景中，LAB-Tab在10%目标数据预算下取得最佳性能，在六个单独场景中领先四个，macro Overall分数相对最强基线降低33.8%，同时获得最优的macro JSD、WAPE和UtilityGap，并保持有竞争力的特征-标签保留度。

Conclusion: LLM辅助的BN结构扩展结合强化学习边校准，能有效将源域知识适配到目标域，在少样本场景下优于直接拟合目标统计量或直接复用源生成器的基线方法。

Abstract: Tabular data generation supports analysis and decision-making when target-domain data are scarce, yet collecting complete target samples is often costly. A practical but underexplored setting provides only a few target records together with richer source data from a related domain. Existing few-shot tabular generators often either fit sparse target statistics directly, which can overfit incidental patterns, or reuse source-domain generators, which may preserve dependencies that no longer hold in the target domain. To address this problem, we propose LAB-Tab, an LLM-augmented Bayesian network (BN) adaptation framework for source-aware few-shot tabular generation. LAB-Tab first fits a BN from source data and then uses an LLM to propose plausible target-domain BN edges that are absent from the source BN graph. This step converts semantic and weak statistical evidence into explicit structural hypotheses, thereby expanding the editable edge space beyond the source-fitted graph. Because the proposed edges may be noisy and interact with existing dependencies, a PPO policy calibrates edges in the augmented BN through edge-level actions, including keep, weaken, strengthen, flip, and deactivate. The PPO policy is trained with a reward that combines distributional alignment, downstream utility, and preservation of target-relevant dependencies. The adapted BN is then sampled to synthesize target-domain tables. Across six source--target distribution-shift scenarios built from three US Census (ACS) prediction tasks, LAB-Tab achieves the best performance at the 10% target-data budget, leads four of the six individual scenarios, and reduces the macro Overall score by 33.8% relative to the strongest baseline. It also obtains the best macro JSD, WAPE, and UtilityGap while maintaining competitive feature--label preservation.

</details>


### [36] [Understanding and Correcting Low-Frequency Bias in EEG Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01898)
*Junjie Yu,Zihan Deng,Jianyu Zhang,Junrong Mu,Jiahui An,Wenxiao Ma,Ziling Lu,Yue Wang,Yan Zhu,Kexin Lou,Quanying Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现EEG基础模型在不同数据规模、模型容量和预训练目标下都存在持续的低频偏置，这一偏置源于EEG的1/f^α频谱结构与神经网络偏好学习低频成分的特性，且掩码自编码器中的ℓ2重构目标进一步放大了这一失衡。为此提出FAME框架，通过按预定EEG频段独立标准化重构目标并均衡各频段损失，实现了频谱均衡的监督信号。在OmniEEG-Bench的41个下游任务中，FAME在24个任务上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明增大EEG预训练数据规模或模型容量并不能持续提升下游任务性能，本文旨在揭示阻碍EEG基础模型可迁移性的根本原因，特别是表征学习中的频谱偏置问题。

Method: 提出FAME（Frequency-balanced mAasked autoencoding framework），在EEG预定义的频段内对掩码输入进行时频活动重构。该方法在每个频段内独立标准化重构目标，并对所有频段损失赋予相等权重，从而在EEG频谱上实现均衡的监督信号。作者还通过系统性分析将低频偏置归因于EEG的1/f^α频谱结构与神经网络对低频成分的偏好学习相互作用，以及masked autoencoder中ℓ2重构目标对高功率低频分量的不成比例贡献。

Result: 在OmniEEG-Bench的41个下游任务上，FAME学习到频谱更加均衡的表征，并在其中24个任务上取得了最先进性能，优于现有EEG基础模型。

Conclusion: 研究强调了均衡的频谱监督对于学习可迁移EEG表征的重要性，为设计更有效的EEG基础模型预训练框架提供了重要方向。

Abstract: Increasing EEG pretraining data scale or model capacity does not consistently improve downstream performance. We identify a persistent low-frequency bias in representations learned by diverse EEG foundation models, which remains across dataset scales, model capacities, and pretraining objectives. Our analysis links this bias to the interaction between EEG's $1/f^α$-like spectral structure and neural networks' tendency to preferentially learn low-frequency components. In masked autoencoders, the $\ell_2$ reconstruction objective further amplifies this imbalance: under comparable relative reconstruction errors, high-power low-frequency components contribute disproportionately to the loss. To address this issue, we introduce FAME, a frequency-balanced masked autoencoding framework that reconstructs time--frequency activity in predefined EEG bands from masked EEG inputs. FAME independently standardizes the reconstruction targets within each band and assigns equal weight to all band-specific losses, thereby balancing supervision across the EEG spectrum. Evaluated on 41 downstream tasks in OmniEEG-Bench, FAME learns more spectrally balanced representations and achieves state-of-the-art performance on 24 of them. These results underscore the importance of balanced spectral supervision for learning transferable EEG representations.

</details>


### [37] [RamanPFN: learning from Raman spectral structure with a tabular foundation model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02157)
*Xingyu Pan,Huan Wang,Jinjia Guo,Zhenlin Zhao,Siming Dong,Jixi Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RamanPFN是一个光谱表示框架，通过全局组合分解和局部振动子空间编码两种表示方法，在TabPFN推理前编码谱图依赖关系，在150个任务（74个公开拉曼数据集）上将回归RMSE平均降低19.6%，分类误差进一步降低9.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拉曼光谱数据常存在少量标记光谱和数千个有序波数的问题，传统潜变量化学计量学方法可处理共线小样本但会掩盖精细峰形，深度光谱网络需要任务特定训练，而TabPFN处理极宽输入时会进行特征子采样从而丢失相关波段的联合可见性。

Method: RamanPFN包含两种表示策略：(1) 全局组合分解（Global Compositional Unmixing）——在完整光谱上构建非负坐标，使具有共享潜变量变化的远距离波段占据共同的预测轴；(2) 局部振动子空间编码（Local Vibrational Subspace Encoding）——对连续波数区域用多个正交模式编码，保留峰形、强度和位置独立变化。两种表示分别评估并在预测层面组合。

Result: 在150个任务（来自74个公开拉曼数据集）上，相比直接TabPFN推理，RamanPFN在129个回归目标上平均降低RMSE 19.6%，在21个分类任务上进一步降低分类误差9.0%。

Conclusion: 显式光谱表示可作为高维拉曼测量与可复用表格推理之间的有效接口，确立了光谱表征在连接领域知识和通用推断工具方面的价值。

Abstract: Raman spectroscopy enables non-destructive, label-free molecular characterization across materials science, biomedicine and process monitoring. Predictive Raman datasets often contain few labelled spectra and thousands of ordered wavenumbers, with informative variation within bands and across distant spectral regions. Latent-variable chemometrics accommodates collinear small-sample data but can obscure fine peak morphology, whereas deep spectral networks resolve this structure only after task-specific training. TabPFN avoids task-specific parameter fitting through pretrained in-context inference, but processes very wide inputs as feature-subsampled views that do not preserve joint visibility of related bands. We present RamanPFN, a spectral representation framework that encodes these dependencies before TabPFN inference. Global Compositional Unmixing constructs non-negative coordinates over the complete spectrum so that distant bands with shared latent variation occupy a common predictive axis. Local Vibrational Subspace Encoding represents contiguous wavenumber regions with multiple orthogonal modes that retain independent changes in peak shape, intensity and position. The representations are evaluated separately and combined at the prediction level. Evaluation covered 150 tasks from 74 public Raman datasets. RamanPFN reduced root-mean-square error by 19.6% on average across 129 regression targets relative to direct TabPFN inference and further reduced the remaining classification error by 9.0% across 21 classification tasks. These results establish explicit spectral representation as an effective interface between high-dimensional Raman measurements and reusable tabular inference.

</details>


### [38] [Start Classifying: Categorical Critics for LLM Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02181)
*Zhijian Zhou,Long Li,Xuan Zhang,Zongkai Liu,Yulei Qin,Ke Li,Xing Sun,Xiaoyu Tan,Chao Qu,Yuan Qi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HL-Gauss PPO方法，将PPO中critic的标量MSE回归头替换为基于离散化值域支持的分类预测头，通过平滑HL-Gauss目标进行交叉熵训练，并在数学推理、工具增强数学和Search-R1等多个任务上持续超越PPO和DAPO基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在带可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）中，稀疏二元奖励使得critic的优化和校准尤为关键——较小的值误差会直接扭曲PPO使用的标量优势估计。尽管标量MSE在统计上有效，但作者认为分类式训练目标能否改善critic信号值得研究。

Method: HL-Gauss PPO将标量MSE回归头替换为离散化值域支持上的分类预测头，以平滑的HL-Gauss目标通过交叉熵训练，并将输出解码为标量期望，以便用于标准的GAE和PPO，actor更新保持不变（非分布式的）。控制实验涵盖one-hot、two-hot和伯努利双箱critic以排除输出头大小或二元分类本身的解释。

Result: 在数学推理、工具增强数学和Search-R1任务上，基于Qwen2.5和Qwen3骨干，HL-Gauss PPO一致优于强PPO和DAPO基线。在推理前缀集合上，HL-Gauss改善了Brier分数和校准误差，并产生更对称、更低方差的优势估计。

Conclusion: 分类式价值学习可作为一种有效的PPO critic优化替代方案，适用于RLVR场景。

Abstract: Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) for large language models typically trains its critic by mean-squared-error (MSE) regression on scalar value targets. Although scalar MSE is statistically valid for estimating the conditional expected return, sparse binary rewards in reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) make critic optimization and calibration especially consequential: small value errors directly distort the scalar advantages used by PPO. We study whether a classification-based training objective can improve this critic signal. HL-Gauss PPO replaces the scalar MSE head with a categorical predictor over a discretized value support, trained by cross-entropy against smoothed HL-Gauss targets. Its output is decoded to a scalar expectation for standard GAE and PPO; the actor update is therefore unchanged and is not distributional. Across mathematical reasoning, tool-augmented math, and Search-R1, and on both Qwen2.5 and Qwen3 backbones, HL-Gauss PPO consistently improves over strong PPO and DAPO baselines. Controls with one-hot, two-hot, and Bernoulli two-bin critics show that neither a larger output head nor binary classification alone explains the gains. On a common collection of reasoning prefixes, HL-Gauss improves Brier score and calibration error and yields more symmetric, lower-variance advantages. These results position categorical value learning as an effective optimization surrogate for PPO critics in RLVR.

</details>


### [39] [From fragmented data to actionable design: Physics-calibrated learning for plastic upcycling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02402)
*Jingyang Bai,Zijia Wang,Xiangyi Long,Marcos Millan,Binjian Nie,Mingyue Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了PC-MG-MoE框架，一种物理校准、缺失度门控、负载均衡的混合专家模型，直接从部分观测的塑料废弃物热化学升级实验数据中学习，无需目标插补，实现了跨实验室异质性下的准确预测和可解释的模型行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 塑料废弃物热化学升级实验文献因条件异质和报告不完整而碎片化。完整用例学习仅保留10.99%的实验数据，目标插补又可能引入有偏监督，迫切需要一种能直接从不完整数据中学习的框架。

Method: 开发PC-MG-MoE框架，包含三个关键组件：物理校准（Physics-Calibrated）确保产品分布物理一致；缺失度门控（Missingness-Gated）将结构化的缺失转化为有信息量的学习信号；负载均衡混合专家（Load-Balanced Mixture-of-Experts）适应跨实验室异质性。可直接从部分观测实验学习，无需目标插补。

Result: 在严格的源分组验证下，模型在评估的模型中取得了最低的聚合绝对误差。湿实验提供了外部对照，展示了关键的组分依赖趋势。实现为交互式Web工作流，支持正向筛选、物理约束的逆向设计、定向实验规划和实验室特定适配。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了将碎片化文献数据转化为可实验操作指导的可迁移框架，能减少实验工作量和试错成本，适用于塑料升级及更广泛的热化学系统。

Abstract: Thermochemical upgrading of plastic waste is a key upcycling pathway, yet the experimental literature is fragmented by heterogeneous conditions and incomplete reporting. Complete-case learning would retain only 10.99% of the curated experiments, while target imputation can introduce biased supervision. Here we develop a Physics-Calibrated, Missingness-Gated, and Load-Balanced Mixture-of-Experts (PC-MG-MoE) framework that converts structured missingness into an informative learning signal. PC-MG-MoE learns directly from partially observed experiments without target imputation, reconstructs physically consistent product distributions, accommodates cross-laboratory heterogeneity, and provides interpretable model behaviour rather than black-box prediction alone. Under stringent source-grouped validation, it achieved the lowest aggregate absolute error among the evaluated models, supporting engineering screening under cross-laboratory heterogeneity. Wet-lab experiments provide an external comparison, showing key composition-dependent trends. Implemented as an interactive web-based workflow, PC-MG-MoE enables forward screening, physics-grounded constrained inverse design, targeted experimental planning that supports reduced experimental workload and trial-and-error, and laboratory-specific adaptation with new platform-specific data. This work establishes a transferable framework for converting fragmented literature data into experimentally actionable guidance for model-guided plastic upcycling and broader thermochemical systems.

</details>


### [40] [Why Large Language Models Fail at Tabular Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02412)
*Marta Garnelo,Wojciech M. Czarnecki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过受控实验系统性地检验了LLM在表格数据预测任务上表现不佳的五个假设，发现前四个（噪声处理、CSV格式、数值分词、每次分类的测试点数）均被证伪，而唯一决定性因素是输入维度：随着维度增加，LLM的准确率显著下降，而所有经典基线方法保持平稳或提升。在二维空间中LLM的行为类似基于距离的局部方法，但在高维空间中没有任何经典模型能复现其预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然LLM在众多任务中已成为默认工具，但在表格数据的预测性分析这一最常见的机器学习工作负载上表现不佳。这一差距是表格基础模型这一快速增长领域的创立前提，但通用LLM为何失败的深层原因仍是一个悬而未决的问题。

Method: 将前沿LLM置于最纯粹的推理模式（单次生成、无工具、无智能体脚手架、无微调），对五个假设进行系统性的受控实验验证：(a)无法处理噪声或非线性可分数据；(b)线性化CSV格式掩盖列结构；(c)数值分词的影响；(d)每次查询分类的测试点数量；(e)输入维度。通过对31个基准数据集进行随机线性投影的维度扫描，并对比252个配置的经典模型。

Result: 受控实验证伪了(a)-(d)四个假设。维度是唯一决定性因素：LLM是9种方法中唯一准确率随维度增加而下降的。在二维空间中，LLM的预测与局部距离方法有高达91.6%的网格一致性；但在高维空间中，即使经典模型添加了调参的维度相关噪声，也无法复现LLM的预测行为。

Conclusion: LLM在表格数据上的能力随维度增加而瓦解的方式无法被任何带噪声的经典学习器模拟。这解释了为什么LLM在其他领域能力强大却始终输给50年前的基线方法在表格数据上的表现。模型的内部预测机制仍是一个开放问题，本文未声称识别内部机制。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have become the default tool for a remarkable range of tasks, yet they have had conspicuously little success at one of the most common machine learning workloads: predictive analytics over tabular data. This gap is the founding premise of the fast-growing field of tabular foundation models, but the question of why generic LLMs fail has remained open. We study a frontier LLM in its purest inference regime - a single generation pass over a prompt containing the full training and test data, with no tools, no agentic scaffolding, and no fine-tuning - and systematically evaluate five hypotheses for the failure: (a) an inability to handle noisy or non-linearly-separable data; (b) the linearised CSV format obscuring column structure; (c) the tokenisation of numeric values; (d) the number of test points classified per query; and (e) the dimensionality of the input. Controlled experiments falsify (a)-(d). Dimensionality, in contrast, is decisive: sweeping random linear projections of thirty-one benchmark datasets, the LLM is the only method among nine whose accuracy decreases as dimensionality grows, while every classical baseline stays flat or improves. A behavioural comparison against 252 configured classical models finds that in two dimensions the LLM predicts like a local, distance-based method (up to 91.6% grid agreement), but in higher dimensions no classical model - even when augmented with tuned, dimension-dependent noise - reproduces its predictions. We do not claim to have identified the internal mechanism; our results show, more modestly, that the LLM's capability dissolves with dimension in a way no noise-corrupted classical learner mimics - which explains why LLMs, so capable elsewhere, keep losing to fifty-year-old baselines on tables, while leaving the mechanism of the prediction as an open question.

</details>


### [41] [RoMeRL: Balancing Feedback Coverage and the Memory-Reward Trap in Self-Evolving Agent Memory via Reduced-Order Utility States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02508)
*Yi Yang,Zhennan Chen,Yihong Zhuang,Tiehan Fan,Yinan Chen,Jian Li,Jian Yang,Ying Tai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RoMeRL是一种减少阶数的记忆强化学习方法，用于自进化LLM智能体，通过固定维度的任务记忆状态来解决轨迹索引效用空间增长和记忆奖励陷阱这两个耦合问题，在ALFWorld和LifelongAgentBench上显著提升了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自进化LLM智能体的学习型记忆系统面临两个紧密耦合的挑战：一是轨迹索引的效用随交互历史增长，导致有限的反馈被分散在不断膨胀的状态空间中；二是轨迹级奖励被联合分配给共同检索的记忆，不相关的经验可能收到误导的效用更新，从而陷入记忆奖励陷阱。

Method: RoMeRL通过简阶记忆强化学习表示增长中的轨迹索引效用空间，使用按结果极性和记忆动态因子化的固定维度任务记忆状态。它通过一组固定的语义坐标来纳入新经验，这些坐标的内容随时间更新或替换，从而将反馈集中在有界的效用支撑集上。理论上证明了这种简阶参数化增加了每个效用坐标收到的平均反馈，并刻画了通用坐标转移模型下错误坐标的稳态占用情况。

Result: 在ALFWorld和LifelongAgentBench上，RoMeRL提升了任务性能，将Cold-Q比率降低80.0%，反馈密度提高约6.0倍，维护记忆大小减少84.4%，LLM调用次数减少21.1%。

Conclusion: 简阶效用状态能够支持高效的自进化智能体记忆，同时限制持久性的奖励污染。

Abstract: Learning-based memory systems for self-evolving LLM agents face two tightly coupled challenges. First, trajectory-indexed utilities grow with the interaction history, thereby dispersing limited feedback over an ever-expanding state space. Second, because trajectory-level rewards are jointly assigned to co-retrieved memories, irrelevant experiences may receive misleading utility updates and consequently enter the memory-reward trap. To address these challenges, we introduce Reduced-Order Memory Reinforcement Learning (RoMeRL), which represents the growing trajectory-indexed utility space using a fixed-dimensional per-task memory state factorized by outcome polarity and memory dynamics. RoMeRL incorporates new experiences through a fixed set of semantic coordinates whose contents are updated or replaced over time, thereby concentrating feedback over a bounded utility support. Theoretically, we show that this reduced-order parameterization increases the average feedback received by each utility coordinate and characterize the steady-state occupancy of erroneous coordinates under a generic coordinate-transition model. Empirically, across ALFWorld and LifelongAgentBench, RoMeRL improves task performance, reduces the Cold-Q ratio by 80.0%, increases feedback density by approximately 6.0 times, reduces the maintained memory size by 84.4%, and cuts LLM calls by 21.1%. These results show that reduced-order utility states support efficient self-evolving agent memory while limiting persistent reward contamination. Code is available at: https://github.com/YOUNG-fnxm/RoMeRL

</details>


### [42] [Structured Memory for Edge Language Models: Persistent Context and Corpus Retrieval via O(1) SSM State Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02560)
*Anusha Madan Gopal,Aras Pirbadian,Kristofor D. Carlson,M Anthony Lewis,Jonathan Tapson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PRECOG是一种针对状态空间模型（SSM）的检索增强生成机制，利用SSM固定大小、位置无关的循环隐藏状态作为文档的完整摘要，预先离线编码语料库为隐藏状态，查询时直接注入最佳匹配状态，将预填充成本从O(L_context)降至O(1)。在同系统中还提出SMC（结构化记忆整合）分层持久记忆机制。在TENNs-LLM（1.2B参数gated-SSM模型，192KB隐藏状态）上，PRECOG匹配传统RAG答案质量，预填充延迟从约27秒降至<6毫秒（约4500倍加速），实现从不可用到交互式的跨越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RAG在Transformer架构下面临两个高昂成本：预填充成本与检索上下文长度成正比（O(L_context)），且KV缓存随生成token数线性增长。虽然SSM天然避免了第二个成本，但第一个成本（预填充）仍然存在。作者希望利用SSM独特的固定大小、位置无关的隐藏状态特性，彻底消除预填充成本，使检索增强生成在边缘硬件上达到交互式速度。

Method: PRECOG利用SSM的核心特性——固定大小的循环隐藏状态是模型已读内容的完整摘要——将文档语料库离线预编码为SSM隐藏状态，查询时直接注入最佳匹配的隐藏状态，完全跳过上下文重新摄入。同时提出SMC（结构化记忆整合）：一种分层持久记忆系统，包含认知领域聚类、可调保真度-存储旋钮、O(1)会话初始化，将短期情景记忆整合为长期语义记忆，并在查询时将两者与检索到的语料库状态融合。

Result: 在TENNs-LLM（1.2B参数gated-SSM语言模型，192KB隐藏状态）上验证：PRECOG匹配传统上下文RAG的答案质量，预填充延迟从约27秒降至<6毫秒，实现约4500倍加速，将边缘硬件上的RAG从不可用状态提升至交互式水平。

Conclusion: PRECOG展示了SSM架构在检索增强生成中的独特优势：通过隐藏状态注入实现O(1)预填充，从根本上消除了RAG的上下文摄入瓶颈。该机制在Transformer的KV缓存上架构性不可行，因为KV缓存位置纠缠且随上下文线性增长。这项工作标志着SSM不仅在生成阶段，也在检索阶段展现了对Transformer的架构性替代潜力。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) imposes a prefill cost proportional to retrieved context length, and -- with Transformer backbones -- a KV-cache that grows with each generated token. State-Space Models (SSMs) avoid the second cost by construction; we eliminate the first, collapsing prefill from $O(L_{context})$ to $O(1)$ per query. We introduce PRECOG (Pre-Computed Context Injection), a retrieval mechanism that exploits a property unique to SSMs: the fixed-size, position-agnostic recurrent hidden state is a complete summary of everything the model has read. PRECOG pre-encodes document corpora offline as SSM hidden states and injects the best-matching state directly at query time, bypassing in-context re-ingestion entirely. The same state-injection mechanism enables SMC (Structured Memory Consolidation): a hierarchical persistent memory with cognitive-domain clustering, an adjustable fidelity-vs-storage dial, and $O(1)$ session initialization, which consolidates short-term episodic states into long-term semantic memory and fuses both with retrieved corpus states at query time. We demonstrate the system on TENNs-LLM, a 1.2B-parameter gated-SSM language model with a 192 KB hidden state. PRECOG matches in-context RAG answer quality, reducing prefill latency from $\sim$27 s to $<$6 ms on edge hardware -- a $\sim$4500$\times$ speedup that crosses the threshold from unusable to interactive. The mechanism is architecturally impossible for Transformer KV-caches, which are position-entangled and grow linearly with context length.

</details>


### [43] [GradCuit: Credit-Assigned Gradient Flow Enables Robust and Interpretable Test-Time Latent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02585)
*Zhaoxin Yu,Qi Shen,Hengli Li,Zhaowei Zhang,Song-Chun Zhu,Chi Zhang,Zilong Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GradCuit是一种通过在选定的Transformer层中插入可优化潜在状态、直接将整个续写的奖励加权梯度分配给潜在状态进行测试时推理优化的方法，在五个指令微调骨干模型、三个推理基准和两种答案格式上平均准确率达64.5%，优于思维链提示6.6个百分点、强于最强对比方法2.4个百分点，并表现出更强的鲁棒性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于优化的潜在推理方法在测试时优化实例特定的连续状态以改进LLM输出，但现有方法通常通过解码token将潜在状态连接到推理轨迹，导致序列级信用分配是间接的，难以理解潜在更新如何塑造后续推理。作者希望解决这一信用分配不直接、不透明的问题。

Method: GradCuit在选定的Transformer层中、在提示的隐藏表示与生成续写之间插入可优化的潜在状态。利用因果自注意力机制，使每个续写token的对数概率都有一条经由剩余Transformer块到达每个前置潜在状态的可微路径，从而将来自整个续写的奖励加权梯度直接分配给潜在状态，无需通过解码token进行间接信用分配。

Result: 在五个指令微调骨干模型、三个推理基准和两种答案格式上，GradCuit平均准确率达64.5%，比思维链提示高出6.6个百分点，比最强竞争方法高出2.4个百分点。在七个学习率设置下，其准确率标准差从LatentSeek的1.53降至0.82，且随机游走变体仍与LatentSeek竞争力相当。token级梯度归因显示潜在影响集中在推理连接token上，层分析表明中早期Transformer层是最有效的优化空间。

Conclusion: 通过直接从结果反馈优化内部推理，GradCuit开辟了一条稳健且可解释的测试时扩展新轴——LLM适应其推理方式，而不仅仅是重新生成、采样或重新排序输出。

Abstract: Optimization-based latent reasoning improves large language model outputs by optimizing instance-specific continuous states at test time while keeping model parameters frozen. Existing methods, however, typically connect these states to the reasoning trajectory through decoded tokens, making sequence-level credit assignment indirect and obscuring how latent updates shape subsequent reasoning. We introduce GradCuit (gradient through circuit), which inserts optimizable latent states at a selected Transformer layer between the hidden representations of the prompt and the generated continuation. Causal self-attention provides every continuation-token log-probability with a differentiable path to every preceding latent state through the remaining Transformer blocks, enabling reward-weighted gradients from the entire continuation to be assigned directly to the latents. Across five instruction-tuned backbones, three reasoning benchmarks, and two answer formats, GradCuit achieves an average accuracy of 64.5%, outperforming chain-of-thought prompting by 6.6 percentage points and the strongest competing method by 2.4 points. GradCuit also demonstrates greater robustness: across seven learning-rate settings, it consistently outperforms LatentSeek while reducing the standard deviation of accuracy from 1.53 to 0.82, and even its random-walk variant remains competitive with LatentSeek. For interpretability, token-level gradient attribution reveals that latent influence concentrates on reasoning-connector tokens, while layer analysis identifies early-to-middle Transformer layers as the most effective optimization space. By directly optimizing internal reasoning from outcome feedback, GradCuit opens a new axis of robust and interpretable test-time scaling, where LLMs adapt how they reason rather than merely regenerate, sample, or rerank outputs.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [44] [AutoFOAM: The Self-Refining Autonomous OpenFOAM Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00003)
*Arun Govind Neelan,A Seshaditya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoFOAM是一个基于大型语言模型的自进化智能体，能够仅通过自然语言指令创建、评估、运行并演进出自己的OpenFOAM仿真，通过7阶段的进化循环和三种防退化机制显著降低CFD求解器的使用门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 使用开源CFD求解器（如OpenFOAM）需要大量专业知识和技能，且配置文件设置耗时费力。论文旨在通过生成式AI降低这一负担，使CFD工作流程更易用、更高效。

Method: 基于Qwen-coder 2.5-14B预训练模型，在252个文本提示上进行微调，覆盖7个OpenFOAM求解器、13个参数化网格模板和y+感知数值策略。核心算法是一个由7阶段组成的进化循环；为防止模型在重复自训练中退化，引入三种互补的抗坍缩流：RAG增强重试上下文、外科手术式字典级补丁、以及提示多样性改写。

Result: AutoFOAM能够自主创建、评估、运行并演变OpenFOAM仿真，将生成式AI与严谨的流体仿真相结合，加速快速原型设计并实现高级CFD工作流的民主化。

Conclusion: 通过引入自进化LLM智能体并采用多重防退化机制，AutoFOAM成功弥合了生成式AI与CFD之间的鸿沟，为CFD工程师提供了更高效的自动化工具。

Abstract: Computational Fluid Dynamics (CFD) plays an important role in modern engineering, but using open-source solvers such as OpenFOAM requires considerable knowledge and skills, as well as time-consuming configuration file setup. To reduce this burden, we propose AutoFOAM - a self-evolving large language model (LLM) agent that creates, evaluates, runs, and evolves its own OpenFOAM simulations based solely on natural-language instructions. Our model is pre-trained on the Qwen-coder 2.5-14B, which is then fine-tuned on 252 text prompts targeting 7 OpenFOAM solvers, 13 parametrized mesh templates, and a y plus-aware numerical policy. The crucial element of the algorithm is a sophisticated evolution loop composed of 7 stages. To prevent model degeneration under repeated self-training, the agent employs three complementary anti-collapse streams: RAG-augmented retry context, surgical dictionary-level patching, and prompt-diversity paraphrasing. By bridging generative artificial intelligence with rigorous fluid simulations, AutoFOAM accelerates rapid prototyping and democratizes advanced CFD workflows.

</details>


### [45] [Enhancing LLMs with Context-Specific Knowledge for Mitigating Misinformation in SMEs: A RAG-based Modeling and Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00006)
*Md. Samiul Islam,Iqbal H. Sarker,Chadni Islam,Ahmad Mohsin,Ahmed Ibrahim,Helge Janicke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了VectorRAG和GraphRAG两种增强检索生成方法，用于缓解中小企业在使用大语言模型（LLM）时产生的幻觉和错误信息问题。实验在LLaMA、Mistral和Qwen等多个先进LLM上进行，结果表明RAG增强的LLM能显著提升响应质量，减少幻觉，支持更可靠、可信和上下文感知的中小企业决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM正被中小企业广泛应用于问答和决策支持，但幻觉导致的错误信息会削弱用户对其可靠性和可信度的信心。因此需要有效的方法来缓解这一问题。

Method: 提出VectorRAG（基于向量检索的增强生成）和GraphRAG（基于图结构的增强生成）两种建模方法，结合外部知识源注入LLM生成过程，并在多个SOTA LLM（LLaMA、Mistral、Qwen）上进行系统性评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，RAG增强的LLM在有用响应生成、幻觉风险降低、上下文相关性和人类可解释性等多个维度上均显著优于未增强的基线模型，有效提高了响应质量和决策支持能力。

Conclusion: RAG方法（尤其是VectorRAG和GraphRAG）是缓解LLM幻觉的有效途径，能够显著提升LLM在中小企业环境中的可靠性、可信度和上下文感知能力，从而更好地支持业务决策。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs), a part of artificial intelligence (AI), are increasingly being adopted by Small and Medium Enterprises (SMEs) to enhance question-answering capabilities and support business decision-making processes. However, hallucinations in LLM-generated outputs can serve as a source of misinformation, reducing user confidence in their reliability and trustworthiness within SMEs. Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has emerged as a promising approach to address this challenge by incorporating external knowledge sources into the modeling process. In this paper, we present VectorRAG and GraphRAG modeling approaches to mitigate hallucinations and misinformation risks and evaluate their effectiveness in SME environments. Our experimental evaluation is conducted on multiple state-of-the-art LLMs, including LLaMA, Mistral, and Qwen, to assess performance in terms of useful response generation, risk of hallucination, contextual relevance, as well as human-interpretation. The results demonstrate that RAG-enhanced LLMs can significantly improve response quality by reducing hallucinations and misinformation, thereby supporting more reliable, trustworthy, and context-aware decision-making in SME environments.

</details>


### [46] [CoT-Core: Accelerating LLM Evaluation via CoT-Aware Coreset Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00014)
*Qihua Pan,Zhenheng Tang,Peijie Dong,Xiang Liu,Huacan Wang,Bo Li,Xiaowen Chu*

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM持续开发过程中的评估计算开销巨大；现有核心集选择方法要么依赖大量历史日志导致"冷启动"瓶颈（如IRT方法），要么仅基于表面词汇相似性而忽略了任务背后的推理流形，无法准确反映问题的逻辑等价性。

Method: CoT-Core框架：(1) 利用LLM为每个问题生成零样本Chain-of-Thought推理轨迹；(2) 将推理路径投影到潜空间；(3) 基于内在逻辑等价性而非表面文本相似度对问题进行聚类，从而选择代表性核心问题子集用于评估。

Result: 在GSM8K、MMLU、MMLU-Pro和GPQA上的大量实验表明，CoT-Core大幅降低评估成本同时保持高保真度评分估计；同时揭示了推理感知剪枝的边界条件，发现其有效性受任务复杂度内在约束。

Conclusion: CoT-Core通过利用推理轨迹进行逻辑等价聚类，成功实现了训练自由的低成本高保真LLM评估；但其效能受任务复杂度门控，即推理感知剪枝并非在所有任务上均适用，需要根据任务复杂度选择合适策略。

Abstract: Evaluating Large Language Models (LLMs) incurs prohibitive computational overhead during continuous development processes. While coreset selection accelerates evaluation, existing methods either suffer from a severe ``cold start'' bottleneck requiring massive historical logs (e.g., Item Response Theory) or exhibit a surface lexical bias that misses the underlying reasoning manifold of tasks. We propose CoT-Core, a novel training-free core question selection framework. Recognizing that lexically disparate questions can share equivalent underlying logic, CoT-Core prompts LLMs to unroll zero-shot Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning trajectories. Projecting these paths into a latent space effectively clusters questions by intrinsic logical equivalence rather than superficial text similarity. Extensive experiments on GSM8K, MMLU, MMLU-Pro, and GPQA demonstrate that CoT-Core drastically reduces evaluation costs while maintaining high-fidelity score estimation, and delineate the boundary conditions of reasoning-aware pruning, revealing that its efficacy is intrinsically gated by task complexity.

</details>

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于检索增强生成(RAG)的管道，利用从Text2Zinc种子描述和LLM生成的专家角色合成得到的500个优化问题数据集（存储在Chroma向量数据库中），来提升大语言模型在优化建模方面的表现。在Qwen 3 30B Instruct模型上，NL4OPT准确率从40%提升到72%，MAMO Easy从40%提升到56%，MAMO Complex从32%提升到56%，验证了语义验证的合成示例能显著提升求解准确性和结构完整性，且该方案可作为微调的有效替代。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 优化建模和约束建模需要深厚的领域专业知识和建模形式语言熟练度，而当前大语言模型在生成优化公式时经常产生结构不一致或不完整的输出，尤其是在组合优化场景下。作者希望探索一种无需昂贵微调训练的替代路径，即通过检索增强生成来提升LLM的优化建模性能。

Result: 在三个基准测试平台上，管道均取得了显著提升：NL4OPT准确率从40%升至72%，MAMO Easy从40%升至56%，MAMO Complex从32%升至56%。结果显示使用语义验证的合成示例显著提高了求解准确性和结构完整性。

Conclusion: 合成数据集生成与检索增强相结合是微调的有效替代方案，表明领域特定的合成语料库配合检索增强可作为在实际决策支持场景中部署基于LLM的优化工具的实用途径，无需昂贵的模型重训练。

Abstract: Both optimization modeling and constraint modeling are non-trivial problems requiring deep domain expertise and proficiency in modeling formalism languages. Despite their importance across logistics, healthcare, and supply chain management, current large language models regularly produce structurally inconsistent or incomplete optimization formulations, particularly in combinatorial settings. This paper evaluates whether a Retrieval-Augmented Generation pipeline built on a curated synthetic dataset can meaningfully improve LLM optimization modeling performance. A total of 500 optimization problems were synthesized using seed descriptions from the Text2Zinc dataset and professional personas created using an LLM, specified in JSON and associated with validated Python solver scripts. These problems were encoded in a Chroma vector database. For each inference problem, semantically similar problems were retrieved and used as contextual guidance for a LangChain LLM agent. Three benchmark testbeds were used to evaluate the proposed pipeline under the Qwen 3 30B Instruct model. Accuracy rose from 40% to 72% on NL4OPT, 40% to 56% on MAMO Easy, and 32% to 56% on MAMO Complex. The use of semantically validated synthetic examples greatly improves both solution accuracy and structure. The combination of synthetic dataset generation with retrieval augmentation provides an effective alternative to fine-tuning, suggesting that domain-specific synthetic corpora paired with retrieval augmentation can serve as a practical pathway for deploying LLM-based optimization tools in real-world decision-support contexts without costly model retraining.

</details>


### [48] [Memory Reward Inflation in Self-Improving LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00017)
*Mohammad Asadolahi,Amir Amini,Samira Talebi,Amirfarhad Farhadi,Azadeh Zamanifar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于外部记忆的LLM智能体自我改进范式中的评分偏差问题，发现"回波缺口"（Echo Gap）这一失败模式：LLM对错误记忆给予虚高的奖励分数，导致智能体越自信地复用错误。作者形式化了"错误独立性假设"（EIA）作为纠正奖励膨胀的必要条件，证明其与真值追踪和误差去相关共同决定可恢复的收益，并提出无答案的奖励去膨胀算法LUCID，在BIRD文本到SQL基准上将执行准确率提升至56.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 记忆型自我改进智能体依赖LLM评分作为代理奖励来筛选和检索过往经验，但在部署环境中缺乏真实标签，LLM自评分存在系统性偏差，可能为错误记忆赋予膨胀奖励，导致错误通过记忆累积而非平均消散，阻碍智能体的持续改进能力。

Method: 首先形式化"回波缺口"失败模式，分析代理奖励替代真值奖励时奖励膨胀的机理；进而提出"错误独立性假设"（EIA），证明其为修正奖励膨胀的必要条件——可用信号须同时追踪真值且与记忆偏差去相关；最后提出无答案需求的奖励去膨胀算法LUCID，在BIRD文本到SQL基准上进行实证验证。

Result: LUCID算法在BIRD基准上达到56.9%执行准确率，优于Memento式自评分智能体（54.0%，平均提升2.9个百分点）和无记忆同构架构智能体（52.4%）。同时证明奖励膨胀不仅出现在按评分排序检索时，也出现在纯相似度检索（部署实际使用的模式）下。

Conclusion: 回波缺口是记忆型自我改进智能体的系统性失败模式，EIA是纠正评分膨胀的必要条件，LUCID作为免答案的去膨胀算法能在真实部署场景中带来一致且稳定的端到端性能增益。

Abstract: Self-improving LLM agents increasingly learn from experience without updating any weights. Each episode is stored in an external memory, scored, and retrieved for similar future tasks to shape later behavior. Viewed through a reward lens, the stored score is a proxy reward for an implicit, non-parametric policy. Each retrieved episode then becomes a policy-improvement step whose reliability hinges on how that score is produced. In deployment, ground-truth labels are unavailable, so the stored reward is at best an LLM assessment. This substitution creates a failure mode, the *Echo Gap*, across the memory-based self-improving agents and model families studied. Incorrect episodes receive inflated rewards; thus, the agent preferentially reuses the very mistakes it has most confident in. Because the error compounds through memory rather than averaging out and the confirming judge's errors remain correlated with the original self-grading bias, so it cannot identify which memories are overvalued. The missing property is formalized as the *Error-Independence Assumption* (EIA), which we prove is a *necessary* condition for correcting the inflation, not merely a description of a good verifier: a usable signal must track truth *and* decorrelate its error from the memory bias, and the recoverable payoff is a closed-form function of exactly those two quantities. We further show the inflation compounds not only when retrieval ranks by the stored score but also under plain similarity retrieval which is the regime the deployed agent uses. Finally, the answer-free de-inflation algorithm LUCID delivers a consistent end-to-end gain on the BIRD text-to-SQL benchmark. It raises execution accuracy to $56.9\%$, above both a Memento-style self-graded agent ($54.0\%$, a $+2.9$-point mean gain across seeds) and a memory-less agent of identical architecture ($52.4\%$).

</details>


### [49] [Request-Level Energy Attribution for Batched LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00026)
*Qi Luo,Kunlin Li,Ziwen Wang,Dongsheng Wang,Yun Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出JouleShare框架，用于批处理LLM服务中请求级能耗的公平归因。通过离线回放请求子集计算精确Shapley能耗作为真实基准，并用轻量校准模型JCalib在服务时预测Shapley份额。实验表明token比例归因与精确Shapley偏差高达0.458（归一化L1），而JCalib将其降至0.116-0.177。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 批处理LLM服务的能耗遥测是聚合级别的，但可持续性报告、费用回收和工作负载分析需要请求级能耗。现有基准只提供模型级、阶段级或token级能耗，缺乏经测量的请求级真实基准，导致实际使用的归因规则与公平分配之间的偏差程度未知。

Method: JouleShare包含两个组件：（1）离线harness通过vLLM回放请求子集，整合GPU功率遥测，计算每个请求的精确Shapley能耗作为真实基准；（2）轻量校准模型JCalib从廉价请求特征学习预测Shapley份额，用于服务时实时归因。实验覆盖16个模型/工作负载组合，在静态批处理和连续批处理下对比token比例归因、JCalib和独立测量基线。

Result: 在16个模型/工作负载运行中，token比例归因与精确Shapley的平均归一化L1偏差为0.440（静态批处理）和0.458（连续批处理），该偏差在三种数据中心GPU上可复现。JCalib将误差降至0.116（静态）和0.177（连续），优于在线不可用的独立测量基线，且保持批量能耗效率。采样Shapley可将测量参考扩展到更大组规模，但差距仍然存在，单一离线校准仍是最准确的可用规则。

Conclusion: token归因在批处理执行下不是边际能耗的可靠代理；通过测量的Shapley真实基准可以校准低成本请求特征，实现更公平的能耗归因。

Abstract: Batched LLM serving improves throughput but complicates energy accounting. GPU power telemetry is aggregate, whereas sustainability reporting, chargeback, and workload analysis often require request-level energy charges. Existing inference-energy benchmarks report model-, phase-, or token-level energy, and recent carbon-accounting work motivates Shapley fairness conceptually. Neither provides measured request-level ground truth, so how far the accounting rules used in practice deviate from a fair allocation has remained unknown. We present JouleShare, an attribution framework with two components. An offline harness establishes this ground truth by replaying request subsets under vLLM with a reproducible protocol, integrating GPU power telemetry, and computing exact Shapley energy for each request. A lightweight calibration model, JCalib, then learns to predict Shapley shares from cheap request features for use at serving time. Across 16 model/workload runs, token-proportional attribution differs from exact Shapley by 0.440 normalized L1 on average under static batching and by 0.458 under continuous batching, a gap that reproduces across three data-center GPUs. JCalib reduces this error to 0.116 under static batching and 0.177 under continuous batching, below even a standalone-measurement baseline that is unavailable online, while preserving exact batch-energy efficiency. Sampled Shapley extends the measured reference to larger group sizes, where the gap persists and a single offline calibration remains the most accurate deployable rule. The results show that token attribution is not a reliable proxy for marginal energy under batched execution, and that measured Shapley ground truth can calibrate low-cost request features toward fairer attribution.

</details>


### [50] [SIRIN: A Unified Toolkit for Detecting Contextual Hallucinations in Retrieval-Augmented and Memory-Grounded LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00033)
*Julia Belikova,Rauf Parchiev,Mikhail Filimonov,Konstantin Polev,Andrey Savchenko,Maksim Makarenko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SIRIN是一个统一的可交互工具包和Web UI，用于检测检索增强、代理和基于记忆的LLM系统中的上下文幻觉，统一了表示探测、不确定性估计和裁判式验证三种检测范式，并支持预生成查询可回答性评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG、代理和记忆增强LLM系统中存在上下文幻觉问题（生成流畅但缺乏证据支持的回复），现有的检测方法分散且缺乏统一接口，需要统一的检测工具和界面来综合不同检测范式并支持用户实时分析。

Method: SIRIN采用统一架构，整合三类检测器范式（表示探测、不确定性估计、裁判式验证），同时支持预生成查询可回答性判断；提供响应级和片段级细粒度检验，支持白盒和黑盒设置；通过Web UI支持用户输入上下文-查询-答案三元组的实时分析，包含幻觉分数、不支持片段高亮和多检测器并排比较，插件式设计便于扩展新检测器。

Result: 在幻觉检测、查询可回答性以及长期记忆系统中的忠实度门控三个任务上展示了SIRIN的有效性，源代码已公开。

Conclusion: SIRIN为LLM系统中的上下文幻觉检测提供了统一、可交互、可扩展的工具集和评估管道，覆盖多种检测范式与粒度，兼顾白盒与黑盒场景。

Abstract: SIRIN (Semantic Inconsistency Recognition and Inspection Nexus) is a unified toolkit and interactive web UI for detecting contextual hallucinations (fluent, plausible responses unsupported by the provided evidence) in retrieval-augmented, agentic, and memory-grounded LLM systems. SIRIN unifies three detector paradigms (representation probing, uncertainty estimation, and judge-style verification) and the complementary task of pre-generation query answerability under one interface, configuration system, and evaluation pipeline, supporting response- and span-level inspection in both white-box and black-box settings. The web UI enables live analysis of user-supplied context-query-answer triples through hallucination scores, unsupported-span highlighting, and side-by-side detector comparison, with a lightweight plug-in design for adding new detectors. We demonstrate SIRIN on hallucination detection, query answerability, and as a faithfulness gate within long-term memory systems.
  The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/sb-ai-lab/SIRIN.

</details>


### [51] [RAG-TESTER: Automated End-to-End Testing of Retrieval-Augmented Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00054)
*Ange Maiztegi,Jon Ayerdi,Miren Illarramendi,Aitor Arrieta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RagTester，一种面向RAG系统的自动化端到端测试方法，通过生成检索文档、测试输入和期望输出，并使用LLM作为评审器来评估答案。该系统针对复杂段落、不支持的查询和文档覆盖率标准生成测试，在24种配置（8个LLM×6个嵌入模型）下进行了72,000次测试执行，检测出21,633个失败，比基线多6.6%，并在20/24配置中表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统在LLM中引入了外部和领域特定知识，但其可靠性受生成模型、嵌入模型、检索机制和提示构建策略之间交互作用的制约。目前缺乏自动化、端到端的测试方法来系统评估RAG系统各组件协同工作的可靠性，导致部署前难以发现潜在失败。

Method: RagTester采用端到端自动化测试框架：(1) 生成检索文档、测试输入和期望输出；(2) 执行测试；(3) 使用LLM作为judge评估生成的答案。其测试生成策略针对三个目标：复杂段落理解、不支持查询（超出检索上下文的查询）以及文档覆盖率标准。在8个LLM和6个嵌入模型组成的24个兼容配置上进行了大规模实验。

Result: 在72,000次测试执行中，RagTester检测出21,633个失败，比基线测试输入生成器多检测出6.6%的失败，并在24个配置中于20个配置上表现优于基线。检测到的失败类型包括：不准确的检索、不支持的答案、对检索上下文利用不完整，以及难以解释复杂段落。

Conclusion: 基于覆盖率导向的测试生成方法能有效暴露RAG系统中检索与生成组件之间的交互性失败，可为RAG配置部署前的评估提供有力支持，帮助识别不同LLM与嵌入模型组合的可靠性差异。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) enables Large Language Models (LLMs) to use external and domain-specific knowledge, but its reliability depends on the interaction between the generative model, embedding model, retrieval mechanism, and prompt construction strategy. We present RagTester, an automated end-to-end testing approach for RAG systems. RagTester generates retrieval documents, test inputs, and expected outputs; executes the tests; and evaluates the resulting answers using an LLM as a judge. Its test-generation strategy targets complex passages, unsupported queries, and document-coverage criteria. We evaluate RagTester using eight LLMs and six embedding models, yielding 24 compatible configurations, and compare it with a baseline test-input generator. Across 72,000 test executions, RagTester detected 21,633 failures, 6.6% more than the baseline, and outperformed it in 20 of the 24 configurations. The detected failures include inaccurate retrieval, unsupported answers, incomplete use of retrieved context, and difficulties interpreting complex passages. These results show that coverage-oriented test generation can effectively expose failures caused by the interaction between retrieval and generation components and support the assessment of RAG configurations before deployment.

</details>


### [52] [Agentic Coding in the Wild: Characterizing GitHub Copilot Traces at Production Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00101)
*Banruo Liu,Haoran Qiu,Íñigo Goiri,Rodrigo Fonseca,Ricardo Bianchini,Esha Choukse*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次在生产规模上对AI编码智能体（如GitHub Copilot等）的工作负载进行表征分析，基于2026年6月3.2M用户、13M会话、761M次LLM调用和95T token的追踪数据，揭示了与聊天机器人截然不同的工作负载特性，并设计了轻量级空闲时间预测器以实现高效的资源编排。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码智能体将多步LLM推理与工具执行交织在一起，形成了一种不同于传统聊天机器人的工作负载，但现有LLM服务系统基于聊天工作负载假设设计，缺乏对智能体工作负载的深入理解。需要生产规模的表征分析来为智能体原生基础设施提供实证基础。

Method: 对2026年6月从GitHub Copilot采样获取的生产追踪数据（涵盖3.2M用户、13M会话、761M次LLM调用、95T token）进行大规模统计分析，识别会话结构、KV缓存命中模式、token消耗分布、时间跨度、工具调用等关键工作负载特征，并基于用户空闲期模式设计了一个轻量级空闲时间预测器。

Result: 发现智能体编码会话由稀疏的用户发起轮次组成，每轮展开为自治智能体循环；轮内KV缓存命中率平均90%，但跨轮边界降至55%，且在模型切换或上下文压缩后会被大幅失效；工作负载呈现多样化和长尾分布特征；智能体周转时间快速但轮边界处用户空闲期长达分钟级，设计的空闲时间预测器捕获了86-90%的总空闲时间。

Conclusion: 这些发现挑战了当前LLM服务系统的核心假设，为设计智能体原生（agent-native）基础设施提供了实证基础，特别是在KV缓存管理、资源编排和空闲感知调度等方面具有重要系统设计意义。

Abstract: AI coding agents like GitHub Copilot, Claude Code, and Codex interleave multi-step LLM inference with tool execution, creating a workload different from chatbots. We present the first production-scale characterization of this workload using sampled GitHub Copilot traces from June 2026, comprising 3.2M users, 13M sessions, 761M LLM calls, and 95T tokens.
  Our analysis reveals distinctive workload properties with important systems implications. For example, agentic coding sessions consist of sparse user-initiated turns, each unfolding into an autonomous agent loop of LLM calls almost always coupled with tool execution. This structure yields KV cache hit rates averaging 90% within a turn, but falling to 55\% across turn boundaries and drastically invalidated after events like model switches or context compaction. Diverse workflows and user behaviors are observed with variable and long-tailed token consumption, time span, and tool calls. We highlight the difference between quick agentic turnaround times and the minutes-long user idle periods at turn boundaries, and design a lightweight idle-time predictor that captures 86-90\% of total idle time, enabling proactive decisions for efficient resource orchestration. These findings challenge assumptions underlying current LLM-serving systems and provide an empirical foundation for agent-native infrastructure.

</details>


### [53] [Can LLM Agents Price Competitively? A Dynamic Multi-Attribute Auction Benchmark for Agentic Commerce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00102)
*Shimaa Ahmed,Yiwei Cai,Mohsen Minaei,Rahul Rachuri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍Bazaar基准，一个在真实市场动态条件（隐藏客户偏好、实时竞争、需求冲击）下评估LLM代理定价能力的开放密封竞价多属性拍卖基准。评测11个前沿LLM后发现：客户获取领先者（如Gemini 3.1 Pro）与利润领先者（如Opus 4.6）不一致，需求冲击下排名再次变化，且最强代理的利润也不及事后最优的三分之一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体商务（Agentic Commerce）正从概念走向实际部署，支付网络、零售商和AI平台已开始让代理代表商家和消费者进行交易。然而，LLM代理能否在客户偏好隐藏、竞争对手实时适应、需求突发变化的真实市场中胜任定价，此前缺乏系统性测试。现有基准无法捕捉这些动态博弈特征，因此需要一个既能反映真实市场动态、又能精确评估的基准平台。

Method: 提出Bazaar基准，一个动态密封竞价的多属性拍卖环境。其核心设计在于：尽管市场环境具有动态性（隐藏偏好、实时竞争、需求冲击），但客户效用基于闭式（closed-form）函数建模，从而支持精确评估。对不同提供商（四大提供商）的11个前沿LLM代理进行系统评测。

Result: 评测发现：（1）客户获取领先代理（Gemini 3.1 Pro）与利润领先代理（Opus 4.6）并不一致；（2）需求冲击下排名再次发生转变，冲击前学习最快的代理通常在冲击后修订信念最慢，而Gemini 3.1 Pro恢复最快但利润并非领先；（3）即使最强代理的利润也不到事后最优利润的三分之一，表明当前LLM在智能体商务上虽有进展但仍有显著提升空间。

Conclusion: 当前前沿LLM在智能体商务定价任务上展示了初步能力，但存在显著的性能差距：不同目标的优化（获客vs利润）导致代理排名不一致，冲击下的适应能力与前期学习速度存在权衡，整体利润距离事后最优仍有约三分之二的差距。该基准为未来LLM定价代理能力的系统评估与改进提供了标准框架。

Abstract: Agentic commerce is moving from concept to deployed infrastructure: payment networks, retailers, and AI platforms are setting the stage for agents to transact on behalf of merchants and consumers. Yet whether the LLMs behind these agents can price competently in real markets, where customer preferences are hidden, competitors adapt in real time, and demand can shift without warning, has not been systematically tested. We introduce Bazaar, a dynamic sealed-bid benchmark for multi-attribute auction under these conditions. Despite its dynamics, the benchmark is grounded in closed-form customer utilities, enabling exact evaluation. Across 11 frontier LLMs from four providers, the leading agents on customer acquisition (e.g. Gemini 3.1 Pro) are often not the leading agents on profit (e.g. Opus 4.6). The ranking shifts again under demand shocks: agents that learned fastest pre-shock are typically the slowest to revise their beliefs afterwards, while Gemini 3.1 Pro recovers fastest despite not leading on profit. However, even the strongest agent captures less than a third of hindsight-optimal profit, suggesting current LLMs are progressing in agentic commerce but leave substantial headroom.

</details>


### [54] [AgentStream: How Well Do Self-Evolving LLM Agents Perform Under Streaming Tasks?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00155)
*Dong Yan,Jian Liang,Dapeng Hu,Ran He,Nicholas Jing Yuan,Qi Zhang,Tieniu Tan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AgentStream框架，通过将智能体基准组织为可配置的任务流，在测试时实例化隔离、顺序和交错三种流式场景，系统地评估自进化智能体在真实流式环境中的表现。研究组合评估了三种前沿基础模型上的五种自进化方法，发现自进化可靠性随流式场景而变化、其收益受模型能力门控且与模型强度非单调相关，没有任何单一方法在所有模型和场景中占优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型智能体自进化研究主要采用独立评估方式，忽视了真实场景中智能体需要适应多样化、复杂任务流的实际情况，导致自进化智能体在流式设置下的行为特征尚不明确。

Method: 提出AgentStream统一框架：将智能体基准组织为可配置任务流，实例化隔离(Isolated)、顺序(Sequential)和交错(Interleaved)三种流式场景，逐步改变流的范围和领域构成；在此基础上组合评估五种代表性自进化方法（基于三种前沿基础模型），解耦模型能力、方法架构和流式场景对自进化结果的联合影响。

Result: 实验表明：自进化的可靠性随流式场景不同而变化；自进化的收益受模型能力门控，且与模型强度呈非单调关系；没有任何单一方法能在所有模型和场景中占优。这些发现为跨模型和场景选择自进化方法提供了具体指导。

Conclusion: 自进化智能体应在真实任务流而非隔离的单任务设置下进行评估，研究者应关注流式场景对自进化行为的影响。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents can self-evolve by continually improving from their own accumulated experience. However, existing studies predominantly adopt independent evaluation. Consequently, the behavior of self-evolving agents in realistic streaming settings, where agents adapt to diverse and complex task streams, remains poorly understood. To address this gap, we introduce AgentStream, a unified framework that evaluates self-evolving agents spanning diverse evolution components by organizing agentic benchmarks into a configurable task stream and instantiating the \texttt{Isolated}, \texttt{Sequential}, and \texttt{Interleaved} streaming scenarios at test time, which progressively vary the scope and domain composition of the stream. Over these scenarios, we combinatorially evaluate five representative self-evolving methods across three frontier foundation models, disentangling how model capability, method architecture, and streaming scenario jointly shape self-evolution. Our results show that self-evolution reliability varies across streaming scenarios, the benefit of self-evolution is gated by model capability and non-monotonic in model strength, and no single method dominates across models and scenarios. These findings offer concrete guidance for selecting self-evolving methods across models and streaming scenarios. Overall, we advocate that self-evolving agents should be evaluated under realistic task streams rather than isolated single-task settings.

</details>


### [55] [TRACE-TS: Attribution-Grounded and Traceable Sensor-Language Reasoning for Human Activity Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00200)
*Sparsh Rastogi,Tanmay Kumar,Baiyu Chen,Jatin Bedi,Zechen Li,Flora D. Salim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TRACE-TS是一个面向穿戴式传感器时间序列的可追溯推理框架，通过专家分类器的归因机制识别显著时空区域，构建带显式证据来源的DAG推理轨迹，并训练紧凑语言模型通过门控交叉注意力生成这些轨迹。在推理时联合输出活动预测与推理轨迹，无需额外归因计算。引入SNM指标诊断推理保真度。在7个可穿戴基准上达到最佳平均准确率(84.43%)和F1(81.24%)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于语言模型的可穿戴传感器解释方法虽然能生成自然语言解释，但其推理与底层信号弱关联，导致生成的解释流畅但无法验证。已有方法将传感器信号简化为活动标签，丢失了丰富的细粒度运动模式信息，亟需一种能够将推理过程与信号证据真正锚定的方法。

Method: TRACE-TS包含三个核心组件：(1) 从专家分类器获取归因信息，识别显著的时空传感器区域；(2) 利用这些区域构建带有显式证据来源的DAG推理轨迹；(3) 训练紧凑语言模型，通过门控交叉注意力机制与传感器记忆token交互来生成这些轨迹。在推理阶段，模型联合输出活动预测和推理轨迹，无需归因计算或教师指导。同时提出SNM(语义节点匹配)指标，作为LLM-as-judge评估工具，在观察、推理和综合三个层面诊断推理保真度。

Result: 在7个可穿戴基准数据集上，TRACE-TS在所有评估方法中达到最佳平均准确率84.43%和F1 81.24%，比最优的LLM基线在F1上提升17.96%。SNM指标能有效定位普通NLG指标遗漏的幻觉观察和断裂的证据链。

Conclusion: TRACE-TS证明了将信号归因与语言模型推理轨迹结合可以有效提升可穿戴传感器数据的可解释推理质量，同时保持紧凑模型的高效推理能力。SNM指标为时间序列推理评估提供了更细粒度的诊断方案。代码已开源。

Abstract: Wearable sensors capture fine-grained motion patterns that support rich behavioral understanding, yet most existing methods reduce these signals to activity labels. Recent LM-based approaches generate natural-language explanations for sensor data, but their reasoning is weakly grounded in the underlying signal, leading to fluent yet unverifiable explanations. We introduce TRACE-TS (Traceable Reasoning with Attribution-Grounded Evidence), a framework for structured and signal-grounded reasoning over wearable time series. TRACE-TS uses attribution from an expert classifier to identify salient spatio-temporal sensor regions, uses them to construct DAG reasoning traces with explicit evidence provenance, and trains a compact language model to generate these traces through gated cross-attention over sensor memory tokens. At inference, the adapted model jointly outputs the activity prediction and its reasoning trace, without requiring attribution computation or teacher guidance. We introduce Semantic Node Match(SNM), an LLM-as-judge metric that diagnoses reasoning fidelity at the observation, inference, and synthesis levels, localizing hallucinated observations and broken evidence chains missed by standard NLG metrics. Across seven wearable benchmarks, TRACE-TS achieves the best average accuracy and F1 among all evaluated methods (84.43%/81.24%), and outperforms the best LLM-based baseline by 17.96% in F1. Our code is available at https://github.com/SparshRastogi/TRACE-TS.

</details>


### [56] [Personalizing Large Language Model Agents with Small Policy Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00215)
*Dian Jin,Zhi Zhang,Huichao Li,Yihe Pan,Rundong Huang,Doudou Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FABLE（因子化自适应Bandit执行层），一种轻量级策略层，用于在冻结的大型语言模型（LLM）智能体之上实现个性化执行决策，通过贝叶斯上下文汤普森采样在线学习每个用户的离散执行策略，无需微调底层模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体虽能检索记忆、调用工具、提出澄清问题、变换响应风格，但将这些执行决策适配到个体用户仍很困难。为每个用户微调单独的LLM成本高昂或对专有系统不可行，而提示和记忆主要只是向智能体暴露用户信息，并未根据反馈适配其执行决策。需要一个轻量级方案来从标量反馈中在线学习用户的执行偏好。

Method: FABLE将记忆、信息获取（如提问）和响应决策进行因子化分解，使反馈只更新相关选择；在探索前通过外部指定的可行集过滤动作；利用贝叶斯上下文汤普森采样学习相对固定默认与成本评分的用户特定残差偏好。在线性残差奖励模型下，经过校准的变体对最优可行动作具有期望遗憾界保证；同时提供任意有效的虚假提升控制。

Result: 在个性化推理、受控反馈和可执行工具使用评估中，FABLE在多个偏好敏感行为上优于纯规则控制，并在端到端任务性能上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: FABLE证明了在不修改底层冻结LLM的前提下，通过在线学习因子化的执行策略能够有效地实现个性化，且具备理论遗憾保证和安全性控制，为黑盒专有LLM系统的个性化提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents can retrieve memory, call tools, ask clarifying questions, and vary response style, yet adapting these execution decisions to an individual user remains difficult. Fine-tuning a separate LLM is costly or impossible for proprietary systems, while prompts and memory primarily expose user information to the agent rather than adapt its execution decisions from feedback. We formulate personalization of a frozen agent as online learning of a per-user execution policy from scalar feedback observed only for the executed action. We propose FABLE (Factorized Adaptive Bandit Layer for Execution), a lightweight policy layer outside a potentially black-box host agent. FABLE factorizes memory, information-acquisition, and response decisions so feedback updates related choices; filters actions through an externally specified feasible set before exploration; and learns user-specific residual preferences relative to a fixed default-and-cost score via Bayesian contextual Thompson sampling. Under a linear residual-reward model, a calibrated variant inherits an expected-regret bound against the best feasible action. We also characterize preferences unidentifiable under persistent feasibility constraints and provide anytime-valid false-promotion control. Across personalized-reasoning, controlled-feedback, and executable tool-use evaluations, FABLE improves several preference-sensitive behaviors relative to rule-only control while remaining competitive on end-to-end task performance.

</details>


### [57] [More Debate, Same Evidence: Structural Limits of Homogeneous Multi-Agent Groundedness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00243)
*Yuelyu Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文检验了多智能体LLM评审小组通过交换批评来提高判断质量的假设，在接地性验证（groundedness verification）任务上，使用同质三智能体小组对六个公开基准进行评估。结果显示，相对于固定单智能体参照，小组的系统级准确率差异从+8.5到-4.4个百分点不等：两个数据集有可靠提升，一个数据集有可靠下降，三个数据集统计上不显著。由于参照和小组使用不同模型变体，这些差异反映的是完整系统间的差异，而非孤立地衡量“辩论”这一因果效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM评审小组被普遍认为通过交换批评能提升判断质量，但这一假设缺乏严格的实证检验。作者旨在系统性地验证“多智能体辩论能否改善接地性验证（判断声明是否被证据支持）的性能”这一核心假设。

Method: 采用同质三智能体评审小组，在六个公开的事实验证和幻觉检测基准上评估，与固定的单智能体参照系统进行对比，报告系统级准确率差异并做统计显著性检验。

Result: 系统级准确率差异范围为+8.5至-4.4个百分点：两个数据集展示可靠增益，一个展示可靠损失，三个统计上无定论。多智能体小组并非总是优于单智能体，效果因数据集而异。

Conclusion: 多智能体评审小组在接地性验证任务上并不总是优于单智能体，效果高度依赖于具体数据集。同时提醒注意：由于参照与小组使用不同模型变体，研究观察到的是完整系统的差异，不能简单归因于辩论/批评交换的因果效应。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) judges are increasingly organized as multi-agent panels under the assumption that exchanging critiques improves judgment quality. We test this assumption for \emph{groundedness verification}, where a judge must determine whether a claim is supported by the supplied evidence. We evaluate a homogeneous three-agent panel on six public fact-verification and hallucination-detection benchmarks. Relative to a fixed single-agent reference, the panel's system-level accuracy difference ranges from $+8.5$ to $-4.4$ percentage points: two datasets show reliable gains, one shows a reliable loss, and three are statistically inconclusive. Because the reference and panel use different model variants, these differences characterize the complete systems rather than isolate a causal debate effect.

</details>


### [58] [CrystalMem: Elastic Memory for Self-Evolving LLM Agents via Knowledge Crystallization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00303)
*Beining Wu,Jun Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CrystalMem，一种弹性记忆侧车，通过四级保真度状态降级、优势加权影响力排序和验证式重结晶机制，解决LLM智能体在内存挤压-恢复循环中能力无法还原（记忆滞后）的问题，在所有评估设置中均超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体内存系统假设字节预算只增不减，但云平台会随负载和成本动态调整配额。研究发现经历"挤压-恢复"循环后，智能体能力无法回到挤压前水平（记忆滞后），其原因在于删除和单向压缩丢弃了后续重建所需材料，且任何仅保留/丢弃条目的策略都存在残余赤字下限。

Method: CrystalMem作为弹性内存侧车：1) 在晶化能量调度下将条目跨四级保真状态逐级降级；2) 结合依赖耦合，按优势加权影响力排序降级顺序；3) 在显式计算和字节上限下，通过验证式重结晶恢复能力。

Result: 在7个环境、17种方法、6个骨干模型上，以及多租户服务和物理边缘-云部署场景中，CrystalMem在所有设置中达到最高的恢复能力。在50%字节预算下，CrystalMem在全部环境中匹配了最强预算受限基线在完整配额下的表现；在相同预算下平均领先+4.6个百分点。

Conclusion: CrystalMem解决了内存弹性恢复问题，证明了通过分级保真度管理和依赖感知的重建策略，可以消除记忆滞后效应，使有限字节预算下的能力恢复达到甚至超越完整资源配额下的基线水平。

Abstract: Memory for self-evolving large language model (LLM) agents is often provisioned as if its byte budget only grows. Cloud platforms, however, adjust quotas with load and cost, and we show that capability does not follow the budget back up: after a squeeze-and-recover cycle, the agent settles below its pre-squeeze level, a gap we call memory hysteresis. The cause is structural. Deletion and one-way compression discard the material needed for later rebuilding, and we prove that any policy that only keeps or drops entries carries a residual-deficit floor. We propose CrystalMem (Crystallized Memory), an elastic memory sidecar that demotes entries across four fidelity states under a crystallization-energy schedule, orders demotions by advantage-weighted influence with dependency coupling, and recovers capability through verified recrystallization under explicit compute and byte caps. Across seven environments, seventeen methods, and six backbones, with multi-tenant serving and a physical edge-cloud deployment, CrystalMem achieves the highest restored capability in every setting and closes the loop left open by every baseline. From a 50% byte budget, CrystalMem matches the strongest budgeted baseline at full provision on every environment; at equal budgets, it leads by +4.6 pp on average.

</details>


### [59] [Learning to Coordinate Symbolic Tools: LLM Agents for Verified Sum-of-Squares Certificates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00326)
*Bohan Chen,Shivam N. Patel,Richard Hoffmann,Sam Looi,Tony Yue Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于工具调用（tool calling）的LLM智能体在加权和平方（weighted SOS）分解任务中的应用。作者构建了一个结合代数任务训练、符号工具（SymPy）和验证器反馈（GRPO）的智能体系统，在合成数据上实现了78.96%的加权SOS验证成功率，相比基线模型提升显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具调用使LLM能够在问题求解中调用外部计算，这在AI数学领域尤其有用。加权SOS分解是证明多项式非负性的可机器验证途径，但候选分解的寻找需要处理非唯一的重新分组和多种符号变换的协调，这对LLM而言是一项具有挑战性的组合任务，值得系统研究。

Method: 作者构建了一个三阶段训练管线：（1）构造包含八个辅助多项式任务和加权SOS分解的135万个合成示例；（2）在直接代数问题和模拟符号轨迹上进行监督微调（SFT），SFT语料不含原生工具调用消息；（3）使用带有任务特定符号奖励的组相对策略优化（GRPO）进一步训练。评估时智能体使用原生SymPy调用进行展开、收集、重排和因式分解，所有最终SOS答案均通过精确展开和系数比较进行验证。

Result: 在保留的同生成器合成问题上，完整的SFT+GRPO+工具系统是四种评估配置中最强的，加权SOS达到78.96%的验证成功率（基线模型使用相同工具为44.73%），九个多项式任务的宏平均准确率达91.75%。

Conclusion: 该工作在受控设定下为结合领域技能训练、可执行工具和验证器反馈提供了一项案例研究，表明对于输出可精确验证的任务，这种组合方法能显著提升LLM智能体的性能，可为其他具有可检查输出的工具调用智能体设计提供参考。

Abstract: Tool calling allows large language models (LLMs) to invoke external computation during problem solving, a useful capability in various fields including AI for mathematics. We study this setting through weighted sum-of-squares (SOS) decomposition, a machine-checkable route to proving polynomial nonnegativity and hence polynomial inequalities. A candidate decomposition can be checked exactly, but finding one requires choosing among non-unique regroupings and coordinating multiple symbolic transformations. We develop an agent that combines algebraic task training, symbolic tools, and verifier-grounded optimization for this task. Rather than training only on the composite SOS task, we construct 1.35 million synthetic examples covering eight supporting polynomial tasks together with weighted-SOS decomposition. We first apply supervised fine-tuning (SFT) to direct algebra problems and simulated symbolic traces, and then use Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with task-specific symbolic rewards. The SFT corpus contains no native tool-calling messages; at evaluation, the agent uses native SymPy calls for expansion, collection, reordering, and factorization. Every final SOS answer is checked by exact expansion and coefficient comparison. On held-out, same-generator synthetic problems, the full SFT+GRPO+tools system is the strongest of four evaluated configurations, reaching 78.96% verified success on weighted SOS, compared with 44.73% for the base model with the same tools, and 91.75% macro accuracy across nine polynomial tasks. Within this controlled setting, our work provides a case study of combining domain-specific skill training, executable tools, and verifier feedback, and may inform the design of tool-calling agents in other domains with exactly checkable outputs.

</details>


### [60] [SymboUQ: Symbolic Uncertainty Quantification for Spatial Reasoning in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00417)
*Dahai Yu,Lin Jiang,Rongchao Xu,Guang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SymboUQ是一个符号化不确定性量化框架，通过区分可符号化性与语义确定性来估计LLM空间推理轨迹的最终答案可靠性，在五个基准上相比最强基线提升了约8%的AUROC并降低了7%的类平衡Brier损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLM）虽然能生成流畅的空间推理轨迹，但其中间关系可能无法支撑最终结论，仅依赖token级置信度不足以可靠估计最终答案的置信度。现有形式化验证器虽能提供更强的语义证据，但适用性有限——解析出的声明不一定能得到明确的语义判定（如未知或不可评估结果）。

Method: SymboUQ框架包含三个核心组件：(i) Layout Auditor：执行有序空间声明并提取可行性、冲突和修复证据；(ii) 无标签的Determinacy Profile：刻画有效的可执行覆盖范围；(iii) Determinacy-Aware Reliability Composer：根据验证器适用性，整合基于约束、表示和解码的评分。

Result: 在五个空间推理基准和四个冻结LLM骨干模型上的广泛实验表明，SymboUQ相比最强基线在AUROC上实现了约8%的相对提升，在类平衡Brier损失上实现了约7%的相对降低。

Conclusion: 通过区分"可符号化性"（声明能否在验证器的形式语言中表示）和"语义确定性"（执行是否产生蕴含或矛盾的判定结果），SymboUQ能够更可靠地估计LLM空间推理的最终答案可靠性，弥补了token级置信度与形式验证器之间的适用性鸿沟。

Abstract: Although large language models (LLMs) can produce fluent spatial reasoning traces, their intermediate relations may fail to support the final conclusion, making token-level confidence insufficient for final-answer reliability estimation. Existing formal verifiers provide stronger semantic evidence, but their applicability is partial: a parsed claim need not yield a definite semantic verdict. To address this issue, we introduce SymboUQ, a symbolic uncertainty quantification framework that estimates final-answer reliability from reasoning traces by distinguishing symbolizability, whether a claim can be represented in the verifier's formal language, from semantic determinacy, whether its execution yields an entailed or contradicted verdict rather than an unknown or not-evaluable outcome. SymboUQ comprises (i) a Layout Auditor that executes ordered spatial claims and extracts feasibility, conflict, and repair evidence; (ii) a label-free Determinacy Profile that characterizes effective executable coverage; and (iii) a Determinacy-Aware Reliability Composer that integrates constraint-based, representation-based, and decoding-based scores according to verifier applicability. Extensive experiments on five spatial reasoning benchmarks with four frozen LLM backbones show that SymboUQ achieves approximately an 8% relative improvement in AUROC and a 7% relative reduction in class-balanced Brier loss over the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [61] [TrAC: Trace-Conditioned Answer Consistency for Efficient Uncertainty Quantification in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00422)
*Dahai Yu,Lin Jiang,Rongchao Xu,Guang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了TrAC框架，结合主动和被动的不确定性信号，基于单条完整推理轨迹进行答案一致性重提取和困惑度分析，在数学推理基准上优于八样本自一致性方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的响应级不确定性估计方法存在局限：被动单轨迹方法仅使用token级置信度信号，基于采样的方法需要生成多条完整轨迹成本高，主动前缀方法只研究部分轨迹。没有任何方法从已完成推理轨迹中主动重新提取答案来衡量其一致性和支持度。作者旨在填补这一空白。

Method: TrAC包含两个核心组件：(1) 主动组件PCE（前缀条件提取），以完整推理轨迹为条件重新生成短答案，同时表征其与原始答案的一致性及token级概率支持；(2) 被动组件TUP（轨迹不确定性画像），无需额外解码即可总结原始生成过程中的token级不确定性演化。最后通过轻量级头将两种表征整合为响应正确性分数。

Result: 在五个数学推理基准和三个LLM家族上，TrAC相比八样本自一致性方法将宏观AUROC提升1.8%，AURC降低3.4%，同时仅需一条完整推理轨迹和一个简短的缓存答案探针。当已有八个样本时，结合重提取与样本共识可进一步提升宏观AUROC 4.3%，降低AURC 8.3%。

Conclusion: TrAC证明主动重提取答案以验证一致性是一种有效的计算高效的响应级不确定性估计方法，能以比采样更低成本达到或超越多采样方法的性能。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate fluent reasoning traces that nevertheless lead to incorrect answers, making response-level uncertainty estimation important for abstention, human review, and adaptive compute allocation. Existing approaches generally fall into three categories: passive single-trace methods use token-level confidence signals, sampling-based methods compare multiple complete traces at higher generation cost, and active prefix-based methods probe partial traces to study answer stabilization or preference transitions. However, none actively re-elicits an answer from a completed reasoning trace to measure its consistency with and support for the original answer. To address this gap, we introduce Trace-Conditioned Answer Consistency (TrAC), a correctness-supervised uncertainty quantification framework that combines active and passive signals anchored to one completed reasoning trace. Its active component, Prefix-Conditioned Elicitation (PCE), re-elicits a short answer conditioned on the completed trace and represents both its consistency with the original answer and its token-level probabilistic support. Its passive component, Trace Uncertainty Profile (TUP), summarizes how token-level uncertainty evolves throughout the original generation without additional decoding. A lightweight head then integrates the two representations into a response-correctness score. Across five mathematical reasoning benchmarks and three LLM families, TrAC improves macro AUROC by 1.8% and reduces AURC by 3.4% relative to eight-sample self-consistency, while using one complete reasoning trace and a short cached answer probe. When eight samples are already available, augmenting sample consensus with re-elicitation further improves macro AUROC by 4.3% and reduces AURC by 8.3%, without additional full-trace generation.

</details>


### [62] [Diagnose Before You Compress: Prediction-Independent Bottleneck Witness Refinement for LLM Serving Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00423)
*Liming Liu,Chao Hu,Mingfei Lu,Cong Tan,Yiwei Ge,Chijin Zhou,Yongjun Xie,Runzhe Wang,Xiaohai Shi,Heyuan Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Bottleneck-Preserving Witnessing (BPW)框架，用于生成紧凑且诊断可靠的LLM服务回放套件。BPW通过三阶段流程（候选提名、覆盖优先序列构建、瓶颈真实验证）确保每个瓶颈组件都保留证据，而非仅依赖工作负载代表性。在BurstGPT、ServeGen和Mooncake数据集上，BPW以紧凑的工作负载集实现验证门槛，并在Mean prefix Macro-F1和WBRC-AUC上分别相对提升2.3%和16.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中的LLM服务产生数百万多样化请求，导致全轨迹回放成本极高。现有trace缩减方法主要保留工作负载分布或代表性请求，但暴露瓶颈的工作负载可能稀有且不具有代表性。此外，单一组件的证据无法弥补其他组件的证据缺失，而使用预测瓶颈作为真值会产生循环评估。因此需要为每个瓶颈组件保留证据，而非仅依赖工作负载代表性。

Method: BPW包含三个阶段：(1) Workload Candidate Nomination：使用响应盲的工作负载特征和封闭式服务端测量，识别可能暴露调度器、预填充、解码或KV缓存瓶颈的工作负载；(2) Coverage-Priority Sequence Construction：将多组件方案组织为可复用超边，优先考虑薄弱和未覆盖的维度；(3) Bottleneck Truth Verification：仅从直接的目标系统测量中推导预测无关的标签，确定满足每个组件的直接双见证要求的最早前缀。

Result: 在BurstGPT、ServeGen和Mooncake上，BPW以紧凑的工作负载集达到验证门槛，优于16种策略，Mean prefix Macro-F1相对提升2.3%，WBRC-AUC相对提升16.3%。阶段消融和敏感性分析确认了三阶段各自的独特贡献和局部稳定性。

Conclusion: 通过对每个瓶颈组件保留直接证据而非仅依赖代表性，BPW能够生成既紧凑又诊断可靠的LLM服务回放套件，解决现有方法因稀有瓶颈负载和循环评估导致的问题。代码已开源。

Abstract: Production LLM serving generates millions of diverse requests, making full-trace replay across serving configurations increasingly expensive. Existing trace reduction methods mainly preserve workload distributions or representative requests, but bottleneck-revealing workloads may be rare and non-representative. Moreover, evidence for one component cannot compensate for missing evidence in another, while using predicted bottlenecks as target truth creates circular evaluation. These limitations make it necessary to preserve evidence for every bottleneck component rather than rely on workload representativeness alone. We propose Bottleneck-Preserving Witnessing (BPW), a quality-constrained framework for compact and diagnostically reliable LLM serving replay suites. BPW first performs Workload Candidate Nomination using response-blind workload features and closed source-side measurements. This stage identifies workloads that may expose scheduler, prefill, decode, or KV-cache bottlenecks. Coverage-Priority Sequence Construction then organizes multi-component proposals as reusable hyperedges and prioritizes weak and uncovered dimensions. Finally, Bottleneck Truth Verification derives prediction-independent labels solely from direct target-system measurements. The verified results determine the earliest prefix satisfying the direct two-witness requirement for every component. Experiments on BurstGPT, ServeGen, and Mooncake show that BPW reaches the verified gate with a compact workload set and outperforms 16 policies, achieving relative improvements of 2.3% and 16.3% in Mean prefix Macro-F1 and WBRC-AUC, respectively. Stage-resolved and sensitivity analyses confirm the distinct contributions and local stability of its three stages. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/llmllmllm/BPW

</details>


### [63] [F-WANDA: Fisher-Reweighted Post-Training Pruning for Sustainable Deployment of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00481)
*Himanshu Mishra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: F-WANDA是一种对WANDA进行即插即改的后训练剪枝方法，通过在单次额外反向传播中收集预激活的Fisher信息，将每行保留预算按Fisher信息比例跨输出神经元重新分配，在零额外校准数据或微调的情况下，在LLAMA-2-7B上实现更优的困惑度和MMLU性能，同时仅消耗SPARSEGPT三分之一的剪枝时间与能耗，处于质量-剪枝成本权衡的帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的单次后训练剪枝方法在质量与计算成本之间存在权衡：WANDA计算高效但剪枝质量有限，SPARSEGPT质量较好但计算成本高昂。作者旨在寻找一种兼顾质量与效率的剪枝策略，实现可持续的LLM压缩。

Method: F-WANDA是WANDA的即插即改版本。其核心创新在于：将每行的保留预算按照预激活的经验Fisher信息比例跨输出神经元重新分配。Fisher信号通过在与WANDA相同的校准语料上进行单次额外的反向传播即可获取，且不更新任何权重。

Result: 在LLAMA-2-7B上50%非结构化稀疏度下，F-WANDA达到WikiText-2困惑度6.85，匹配WANDA的流畅度，5-shot MMLU比WANDA提升+1.6个百分点、比SPARSEGPT提升+1.1个百分点，且剪枝墙钟时间和能耗仅为SPARSEGPT的三分之一。

Conclusion: F-WANDA在无需额外校准数据或微调的前提下实现了质量与剪枝成本的最优权衡，将自身置于可持续LLM压缩的质量-成本帕累托前沿上。

Abstract: One-shot post-training pruning is the most energy-frugal compression strategy for largelanguage models (LLMs), yet existing approaches trade either quality (WANDA) or compute cost (SPARSEGPT). We introduce F-WANDA, a drop-in modification of WANDA that reallocates the per-row keep budget across output neurons in proportion to the empirical Fisher information of the pre-activation. The Fisher signal is collected in a single additional backward pass over the same calibration corpus WANDA already uses; no weights are updated. On LLAMA-2-7B at 50 % unstructured sparsity, F-WANDA attains WikiText-2 perplexity of 6.85, matches WANDA fluency, and improves 5-shot MMLU by +1.6 pp over WANDA and +1.1 pp over SPARSEGPT, while incurring only one-third of SPARSEGPT pruning wall-clock and energy. The headline trade-off is achieved without extra calibration data or fine-tuning, placing F-WANDA on the Pareto frontier of quality versus pruning cost for sustainable LLM compression.

</details>


### [64] [The Bayesian Reflex: A Predictive Coding Engine for Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00492)
*Sourabh Bhattacharya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"贝叶斯反射"框架，通过预测编码的三个支柱（分层生成模型的信念维护、基于预测误差最小化的序贯贝叶斯更新、基于主动推断的不确定性驱动行动）直接实现大脑启发的计算框架，并借助椭圆分解、递归高斯过程和导数感知的贝叶斯优化等近期突破，实现数学上严谨、可扩展的持续性学习、感知与决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测编码是皮层计算的强大理论，但相应的人工智能可扩展算法实现一直缺失。作者旨在弥合神经科学理论与实际AI算法之间的鸿沟，构建一个可直接实例化预测编码的计算框架。

Method: 提出贝叶斯反射框架，包含三大支柱：（1）通过分层生成模型进行信念维护；（2）通过预测误差最小化实现序贯贝叶斯更新；（3）通过主动推断实现不确定性驱动的行动。利用椭圆分解实现精确i.i.d.采样、递归高斯过程支持深度分层推断、导数感知贝叶斯优化作为关键算法组件。

Result: 该框架在多个应用场景中展示了通用性，包括气候模型评估和素数发现等，证明了其作为真正自适应人工智能蓝图的可行性。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种数学上严谨、可扩展且受大脑启发的持续学习、感知与决策方法，为构建真正自适应的人工智能提供了蓝图。

Abstract: Predictive coding offers a powerful theory of cortical computation, but corresponding scalable algorithmic implementations for artificial intelligence have remained elusive. This paper introduces the Bayesian reflex, a computational framework that directly instantiates predictive coding through three pillars: belief maintenance via hierarchical generative models, sequential Bayesian updating via prediction-error minimization, and uncertainty-driven action via active inference. We show that recent breakthroughs---ellipsoidal decomposition for exact $i.i.d.$ sampling, recursive Gaussian processes for deep hierarchical inference, and derivative-aware Bayesian optimization---provide the missing algorithmic ingredients. The resulting framework enables mathematically principled, scalable, and brain-inspired continual learning, perception, and decision-making. We illustrate its versatility through applications ranging from climate model evaluation to prime number discovery, offering a blueprint for truly adaptive artificial intelligence.

</details>


### [65] [BayesSeg: A Bayesian Optimization Framework for State Segmentation of Electricity Consumption Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00513)
*Zhenya Zhang,Wendi Zhu,Ping Wang,Hongmei Cheng,Shuguang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出BayesSeg，一个将时间序列分割、多维评估与自动参数优化统一整合的NILM（非侵入式负荷监测）框架。它采用双稳态判据实现无监督分段，结合事件级F1分数与归一化互信息构建复合评估指标，并利用贝叶斯优化（TPE代理模型）实现高效的全局参数寻优，在SustDataED2数据集上以约100次评估达到网格搜索最优值0.35%以内的偏差，且优化延迟从约5300秒降至1秒以下（加速超5700倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NILM中的自适应用电时序分割方法面临三大挑战：启发式参数调参依赖人工经验、分割结果对边界位置敏感、传统逐点评估指标的判别能力趋于饱和，难以精准刻画分割质量。因此需要一种统一框架，能够同时解决分割、评估与参数优化三个环节的问题。

Method: BayesSeg采用三层统一架构：(1) 分割层：基于前序子序列的尾值与均值构建双稳态判据，结合顺序提取与补集解析策略，实现稳态段和过渡段的无监督精确划分；(2) 评估层：将分割结果映射为二元状态序列，构建事件级F1分数（event_F1，通过容差匹配量化开关事件的精确率与召回率）与归一化互信息（NMI，刻画全局结构一致性）的复合指标；(3) 优化层：以复合分数为目标函数，采用TPE代理模型的贝叶斯优化进行全局参数空间高效搜索。

Result: 在SustDataED2数据集上，贝叶斯优化仅需约100次目标函数评估即可定位到与穷举网格搜索最优值偏差在0.35%以内的参数区域；框架达到加权复合分数0.7149、事件级F1分数0.9340；优化延迟从约5300秒降至1秒以下，加速比超过5700倍。

Conclusion: BayesSeg实现了分割配置的完全自动化，有效克服了边界敏感性和点式指标判别力不足的问题，为NILM及相关领域的时间序列分析提供了一种可扩展且高效的一体化解决方案。

Abstract: In Non-Intrusive Load Monitoring (NILM), adaptive segmentation of electricity consumption time series is critical for appliance recognition. However, prevailing methods face challenges including heuristic parameter tuning, boundary sensitivity, and metric saturation. This paper proposes BayesSeg, a unified framework integrating time-series segmentation, multidimensional evaluation, and automatic parameter optimization. The segmentation layer employs a dual steady-state criterion based on the tail value and mean of preceding subsequences, combined with a sequential extraction and complement-set parsing strategy, to achieve precise unsupervised partitioning of steady-state and transition-state segments. The evaluation layer maps segmentation results to binary state sequences and formulates a composite metric integrating an event-level F1 score (event_F1) with Normalized Mutual Information (NMI). The event_F1 quantifies switching-event precision and recall via tolerance matching, while NMI captures global structural consistency, jointly overcoming the boundary sensitivity and limited discriminability of point-wise metrics. In the optimization layer, the composite score serves as the objective function for Bayesian optimization, which constructs a TPE surrogate model for efficient global parameter-space exploration.
