In an end-to-end evaluation, automatically predicted and persisted authority labels reduce the observed unauthorized-action rate from 16.9% to 0.0%, while benign task success remains essentially unchanged. These findings show that memory-driven adaptation must preserve not only what was
  learned, but also the authority under which it may be reused.

</details>


### [97] [GABench: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Evaluating LLM Agents on Graph Analysis Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01684)
*Jiarui Tan,Zhongjian Zhang,YaBo Guo,Jiawei Liu,Yujie Xing,Muhan Zhang,Cheng Yang,Chuan Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GABench，一个全面的智能体图分析基准，涵盖三种图类型、四类图分析任务，提供84个可执行工具，并构建了10,400个具有可验证真值标签的任务。实验发现现有LLM智能体在复杂图分析任务上表现不佳，harness的选择显著影响性能，且图分析性能更多取决于工具调用的质量而非数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图分析基准存在两方面局限：其一，对图任务和图类型的覆盖有限，难以全面评估LLM智能体；其二，大多数基准将图分析形式化为基于文本的问答任务，图信息直接包含在提示中，限制了端到端智能体能力的评估。此外，LLM智能体通常需要harness支持，而现有工作对此缺乏系统性研究。

Method: GABench包含三个关键组成部分：(1) 三种图类型和四类图分析任务（图检索、图理论、图机器学习、图开放性问答）；(2) 84个可执行工具用于访问图数据和执行多样化图操作；(3) 基于这些工具构建的智能体图分析任务生成流水线，生成10,400个具有可验证真值标签的任务。

Result: 使用GABench评估多个前沿LLM和智能体harness后，发现三个关键结果：(1) 现有LLM智能体在复杂图分析任务上仍表现不佳；(2) harness的选择对性能有显著影响，但现有harness在复杂图任务上仍然受限；(3) 图分析性能更依赖工具调用的质量而非数量。

Conclusion: GABench为LLM智能体的端到端图分析能力提供了全面且可验证的评估框架，实验发现为开发更强大的LLM图分析智能体以及改进harness设计提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly capable of planning, using tools, and interacting with external environments. They are typically supported by harnesses, which manage state and coordinate multi-step execution. Graph analysis provides a promising setting for evaluating their agentic capabilities, because it requires agents to access data and execute operations in a graph environment. However, existing graph benchmarks for LLMs provide limited coverage of graph tasks and graph types, making it difficult to comprehensively evaluate LLM agents. Moreover, they typically formulate graph analysis as text-based question answering, where graph information is directly provided in the prompt, limiting the evaluation of end-to-end agentic capabilities. To address these limitations, we introduce GABench, a comprehensive benchmark for agentic graph analysis. GABench spans three graph types and covers four graph analysis task categories: graph retrieval, graph theory, graph machine learning, and graph open-ended question answering. GABench also provides 84 executable tools for accessing graph data and performing diverse graph operations. Building on these tools, we develop an agentic graph analysis task generation pipeline and construct 10,400 tasks with verifiable ground truth.Using GABench, we evaluate a range of frontier LLMs and agent harnesses. Our experiments reveal three key findings: (1) Existing LLM agents still struggle with complex graph analysis tasks. (2) Harness choice significantly affects performance, yet existing harnesses remain limited on complex graph tasks. (3) Graph analysis depends more on tool-call quality than quantity. Our findings provide practical insights into the development and evaluation of LLM agents for graph analysis.

</details>


### [98] [Beyond Single-Use Tokens: Durable Authorization State for Replay-Resistant LLM Agent Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01710)
*Jinghan Xu,Longze Fan,Zeyuan Wang,Xinjin Li,Hankai Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了"语义重放"（semantic replay）问题：在工具型LLM智能体频繁重规划、重试、委托任务和崩溃恢复的过程中，一个用户授权可能会在多个新鲜token标识符下被反复请求和执行多次，超出了基于token标识符的单次使用限制。作者引入了CapLease，一个授权消费层，通过绑定认证用户确认为规范化动作、并强制事务化的Issue-Prepare-Commit转换来防止这种语义重放，实验表明仅凭标识符本地token消耗无法防止新鲜重发，除非发行方保留关于授权动作、确认事件和剩余执行预算的单调持久状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体代理频繁进行重规划、重试、委派和崩溃恢复等行为，导致单一用户授权可能被多次请求和执行（每次使用全新的token标识符）。传统的token单次使用机制只限制token标识符本身的复用，无法阻止在新鲜token下重新执行相同授权语义，这构成了一个被忽视的授权执行超预算漏洞。

Method: 提出了CapLease授权-消费层，遵循提案级和权威级防御策略，将认证的用户确认绑定到规范化动作（canonical action），并强制执行事务化的Issue-Prepare-Commit状态转换，依赖发行方维护的单调持久状态来追踪授权动作、确认事件和剩余执行预算。

Result: 在LLM智能体的重规划、重试、委托、并发、确认重放和崩溃恢复等场景中，仅使用标识符本地token会发生新的语义重发，而CapLease和同等有状态的Server Ledger能够阻止重复准入；配合幂等汇点（idempotent sink）时，还能防止重复的外部副作用。

Conclusion: 系统需求的核心在于可持久化的授权状态（durable authorization state），而非token表示形式本身；防止重放攻击的关键是发行方对授权动作、确认与预算保留单调持久状态。

Abstract: Tool-using large language model agents frequently replan, retry failed operations, delegate tasks, and resume after crashes. These behaviors can cause one user authorization to be requested and executed multiple times under freshly issued token identifiers, even when each individual token is single-use. We call this failure semantic replay: exceeding the execution budget of a token-independent authorization instance rather than merely reusing an old token identifier. We show that identifier-local token consumption cannot prevent fresh reissuance unless the issuer retains monotonic durable state over the authorized action, confirmation event, and remaining execution budget. We introduce CapLease, an authorization-consumption layer that follows proposal- and authority-level defenses, binds an authenticated user confirmation to a canonical action, and enforces transactional Issue-Prepare-Commit transitions. Across LLM-agent replanning, retry, delegation, concurrency, confirmation-replay, and crash-recovery scenarios, identifier-local tokens permit fresh semantic reissuance, whereas CapLease and an equally stateful Server Ledger prevent duplicate admission and, with an idempotent sink, duplicate external effects. Our results identify durable authorization state, rather than token representation alone, as the systems requirement for replay-resistant agent execution.

</details>


### [99] [Constructing Executable Analytical Knowledge Representations for Meta-Analysis Synthesis Using an Agentic Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01711)
*Lingbo Li,Anuradha Mathrani,Teo Susnjak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出可执行分析知识表示（EAKR），一种将结构化证据转化为可执行元分析的机器可操作知识表示框架，并基于大型语言模型与确定性服务构建了代理系统MetaSynDec。实验结果表明，EAKR能够支持形式化验证、可追溯性、统计执行，并在方法学一致性上显著优于直接LLM生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化证据本身并不包含执行统计分析所需的分析知识，包括证据分配、分析对比、结果与时间点对齐、效应量公式化以及方法学可接受性等决策。现有自动化方法将这些决策嵌入模型输出、生成代码或工作流痕迹中，缺乏独立可验证的知识表示，导致分析决策不可追溯、不可验证。

Method: 引入可执行分析知识表示（EAKR），该表示涵盖证据、关系、数值输入、约束、溯源及未决问题六个维度。将EAKR操作化于MetaSynDec——一个代理框架，其中大语言模型提出结构化更新，确定性服务执行基于模式（schema）和契约（contract）的验证与执行。

Result: 在58个综合单元中，MetaSynDec构建了全部EAKR，其中57个进入统计执行；38/56（67.9%）达到完整对象保真度，42/56（75.0%）实现精确证据集一致，平均Jaccard相似度为0.909；55个单元中54个（98.2%）的置信区间与已发布结果重叠。在参考综合结构一致性上（57/58 vs 23/58，p<0.001）及23个共同完成单元的精确参考公式一致性上（23/23 vs 1/23，p<0.001），MetaSynDec均显著优于直接LLM生成。

Conclusion: EAKR为知识驱动科学分析提供了可行性证据：通过将分析知识显式化为独立可验证的机器可操作表示，并与确定性验证和执行服务结合，能够实现元分析的形式化验证、完整溯源、统计执行，以及相比直接LLM生成显著提升的方法学一致性。

Abstract: Meta-analysis synthesis highlights a fundamental challenge in knowledge-based scientific analysis: structured evidence does not by itself represent the analytical knowledge required for executable computation. Decisions about evidence assignment, analytical contrasts, outcome and time-point alignment, effect-size formulation, and methodological admissibility must be explicit before statistical execution. Existing automated approaches often embed these decisions in model outputs, generated code, or workflow traces rather than representing them as independently verifiable knowledge. We introduce the Executable Analytical Knowledge Representation (EAKR), a machine-actionable representation of the knowledge required to transform structured evidence into executable meta-analysis. An EAKR represents evidence, relations, numerical inputs, constraints, provenance, and unresolved issues. We operationalise EAKR in MetaSynDec, an agentic harness in which large language models propose structured updates and deterministic services govern schema- and contract-based validation and execution. Across 58 synthesis units, MetaSynDec constructed all EAKRs, with 57 proceeding to statistical execution. Of 56 units with sufficient information to define a reference analysis object, 38 (67.9%) achieved complete object fidelity and 42 (75.0%) exact evidence-set agreement, with a mean Jaccard similarity of 0.909. Generated and published confidence intervals overlapped in 54 of 55 units (98.2%). MetaSynDec outperformed direct LLM generation in reference synthesis-structure agreement (57/58 versus 23/58; p<0.001) and among 23 jointly completed units, exact reference-formulation agreement (23/23 versus 1/23; p<0.001). These findings provide feasibility evidence that EAKR supports formal validation, traceability, statistical execution, and improved methodological agreement relative to direct LLM generation.

</details>


### [100] [LaCache: Robust Semantic Caching for LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01718)
*Jiacheng Liang,Yuhui Wang,Tanqiu Jiang,Ting Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LaCache是一种新型语义缓存方案，通过同时检查查询及其前k个推测解码令牌的缓存命中来防御缓存碰撞攻击，在提供形式化安全保证的同时提升缓存检索的相关性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的语义缓存在LLM服务中被广泛采用以提升响应速度和降低开销，但已有的缓存方案本质上容易受到缓存碰撞攻击——攻击者通过注入精心构造的查询来污染缓存，从而破坏后续合法请求的响应。

Method: 核心洞见是：攻击者虽能完全控制对抗性查询，但对响应控制力有限，因为响应必须同时满足多个语义约束。LaCache不仅检查查询的缓存命中，还额外检查其前k个推测解码令牌的缓存命中。这一设计在不改变缓存架构的前提下，通过对响应侧引入额外约束来阻断攻击者的碰撞能力。

Result: 作者形式化证明了无法同时构造出既能引发恶意响应又能与良性查询碰撞的对抗性查询，从而提供了对缓存碰撞攻击的可靠防御。同时，增强的索引为缓存检索提供了额外的语义上下文，提升了响应相关性。在多种LLM和基准上的实证评估验证了安全保证和效率提升。

Conclusion: LaCache通过"查询+推测令牌"双重校验的简洁设计，同时解决了语义缓存的安全性和相关性问题，为鲁棒语义缓存指明了有前景的方向。

Abstract: Semantic caching, which reuses responses to semantically similar requests via their embeddings, has seen growing adoption in LLM serving, offering faster responses and reduced costs. Yet existing schemes are fundamentally vulnerable to cache-collision attacks, wherein an adversary pollutes the cache by injecting crafted queries, corrupting responses to subsequent legitimate requests. We present LaCache, a novel semantic caching scheme that addresses this vulnerability through a conceptually simple yet principled redesign. The key insight is that while the adversary has full control over the adversarial query, it has far less control over its response, which must simultaneously satisfy multiple semantic constraints. Rather than checking only the cache hit of a query, LaCache additionally checks the cache hit of its first k (speculatively) decoded tokens. This design yields two concrete benefits. First, it provides formally guaranteed resilience against cache-collision attacks: we prove that it is impossible to craft adversarial queries that simultaneously elicit malicious responses and collide with benign queries. Second, the enriched index supplies additional semantic context for cache retrieval, improving response relevance. Empirical evaluation across diverse LLMs and benchmarks validates both LaCache's security guarantees and efficiency gains, pointing to a promising direction for robust semantic caching.

</details>


### [101] [CoEvo-Mem: Co-Evolving Retrieval Policy and Memory Bank for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01739)
*Bowen Ye,Yongchao Xu,Zhijian Li,Xiang Yin,Junkai Ma,Wenzhao Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CoEvo-Mem是一个闭环框架，用于协同进化长期LLM智能体的检索策略和记忆库。通过让检索结果作为耦合接口，路由决策的信用分配与轨迹条件反馈共同驱动记忆更新，并采用交替优化策略缓解耦合带来的非平稳性问题，在七个基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期LLM智能体方法通常将记忆检索（如迭代查询改写或自适应检索策略）和记忆演化（如结构更新）分开优化，忽视了二者之间的根本反馈回路：检索决定了哪些记忆获得使用信号，而更新后的记忆库又会重塑未来的检索结果。

Method: CoEvo-Mem包含以下核心组件：1) 冻结的LLM为每个查询生成路径特定的查询改写和路由先验；2) 轻量级残差路由器在线纠正路由先验；3) 检索上下文作为两个学习过程的耦合接口——任务结果将信用分配给路由决策，轨迹条件反馈则更新记忆值和图关系；4) 通过交替优化（固定记忆库更新路由器、固定检索策略演化记忆库）来缓解耦合导致的非平稳性。

Result: 在七个多样化基准上，CoEvo-Mem取得了最先进的性能，验证了检索-记忆协同进化的重要性。

Conclusion: 检索策略与记忆库的协同进化（而非单独优化）是提升长期LLM智能体在跨任务、跨会话记忆累积场景下性能的关键。CoEvo-Mem通过闭环反馈和交替优化框架，有效实现了这一协同进化目标。

Abstract: As memories accumulate across tasks and sessions, the performance of long-term LLM agents depends jointly on query-specific retrieval and continual memory refinement. However, existing methods typically optimize either memory access, through iterative query refinement or adaptive retrieval policies, or memory evolution such as structural update. This separation overlooks a fundamental feedback loop: retrieval determines which memories receive usage signals, while updated memory bank reshape future retrieval. We propose \textbf{CoEvo-Mem}, a closed-loop framework for co-evolving the retrieval policy and memory bank. For each query, a frozen LLM generates route-specific query rewrites and a routing prior, which a lightweight residual router corrects online. The retrieved context serves as the coupling interface between the two learning processes: task outcomes assign credit to routing decisions, while trajectory-conditioned feedback updates memory values and graph relations. These updates alter how memories are ranked and selected for subsequent queries, thereby closing the feedback loop. To mitigate coupling induced non-stationarity, CoEvo-Mem alternates between updating the router with the memory bank fixed and evolving the memory bank with the retrieval policy fixed. Across seven diverse benchmarks, \textbf{CoEvo-Mem} achieves state-of-the-art performance, demonstrating the importance of retrieval-memory coevolution.

</details>


### [102] [MemSIF: From Structured Interactions to Dual-Track Fact Memory for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01742)
*YuFei Luo,Xiucheng Xu,Zhen Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MemSIF，一种结构化交互到事实的记忆框架，通过缓解长期交互中的两种错位模式（时间-结构错位TSM和延迟效用显现DUM）来提升LLM智能体的长期记忆能力。实验在LoCoMo和LongMemEval-S两个基准上、跨五个骨干LLM验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期记忆对LLM智能体在长时间跨度的交互中至关重要，但现有记忆系统存在两类持续性的错位问题：一是时间邻近性与主题/事件相关性不总是对齐（TSM），二是写入时的显著性无法可靠预测未来的查询效用（DUM）。这两个问题限制了记忆系统的检索效率和使用价值。

Method: MemSIF包含两个核心组件：1）结构化交互记忆（Structured Interaction Memory），将原始交互组织为保持局部主题一致性的Topical Segments和维持跨时间事件连续性的Event Trajectories；2）双轨事实记忆（Dual-Track Fact Memory），包含CoreFact记忆（写入时稳定整合schema引导的信息）和ActiveFact记忆（按需形成事实，并提升由多个历史来源支持且被反复查询需求重用的事实）。

Result: 在LoCoMo和LongMemEval-S两个基准上，跨五种骨干LLM的实验中，MemSIF在所有场景下均取得最高的Total ACC，相比最强基线在LoCoMo上提升2.29%-8.79%，在LongMemEval-S上提升2.87%-6.15%。

Conclusion: 实验结果证明将结构化交互记忆与双轨事实记忆相结合能有效缓解TSM和DUM问题，从而提升LLM智能体的长期记忆能力。

Abstract: Long-term memory is critical for LLM agents operating over long-horizon interactions. However, several persistent limitations of existing memory systems can be traced to two recurring misalignment patterns in long-term interaction settings: Temporal-Structural Misalignment (TSM) and Delayed Utility Manifestation (DUM). TSM arises when temporal proximity does not reliably align with topical or event-level relatedness, whereas DUM arises when write-time salience does not reliably predict future query utility. To mitigate these misalignment patterns, we propose MemSIF (Memory with Structured Interactions and Facts), a structured interaction-to-fact memory framework. Structured Interaction Memory organizes raw interactions into Topical Segments that preserve local topical coherence and Event Trajectories that maintain cross-time event continuity. Dual-Track Fact Memory uses two complementary tracks: CoreFact memory consolidates stable, schema-guided information at write time, whereas ActiveFact memory forms facts on demand and promotes those supported by multiple historical sources and recurring query demand for reuse. Experiments on LoCoMo and LongMemEval-S across five backbone LLMs show that MemSIF achieves the highest Total ACC in all settings, outperforming the strongest baseline by 2.29%-8.79% on LoCoMo and 2.87%-6.15% on LongMemEval-S. These results support the effectiveness of combining Structured Interaction Memory with Dual-Track Fact Memory to mitigate TSM and DUM. Code is available at https://github.com/luoyufeihaha/MemSIF.

</details>


### [103] [Leveraging AI for fine-grained food safety risk forecasting in sparse data conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01767)
*Dongqi Wang,Weiwei Chen,Han Zhou,Weihua Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于Transformer的框架，通过整合超过1100万条食品检查记录与统计年鉴中的社会经济环境数据，预测城市级别的细粒度食品安全风险。采用三阶段预训练设计，结合Wilson区间部分监督和半监督标签精化，在2022年数据上显著优于基线，并通过浙江省市场监管局现场实验表明其能提高检出率和检查资源配置效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 食品安全是关键的公共卫生挑战，尤其在检查资源有限且区域采样数据稀疏的情况下，传统的反应式监管模式难以实现早期、细粒度的风险识别，亟需更主动的数据驱动监管方案。

Method: 提出一个Transformer架构框架：(1) 统一整合1100万+检查记录与来自统计年鉴的人口、经济、环境等多源辅助指标；(2) 采用三阶段预训练设计，利用Wilson区间捕捉安全性和风险排序的部分监督信号；(3) 结合半监督标签精化策略，在本地样本量不足时有效利用历史记录。

Result: 在2022年数据的实验中显著优于基线方法；与浙江省市场监管局合作的现场实验表明，该方法相比人工制定的检查计划能提高检出率和检查资源配置效率；此外，观察到监管决策中存在基于阈值的启发式规则，暗示额外培训或决策支持界面可进一步提升AI风险评分的影响。

Conclusion: 本研究表明，将大规模公共检查数据、Wilson区间置信度建模与先进深度学习进行严格整合，能够促进食品安全威胁的更早期、更细粒度的识别，减少对反应式措施的依赖，推动全球食品供应链的主动式、数据驱动监管。

Abstract: Ensuring food safety represents a critical public health challenge, particularly when inspection resources are limited and regional sampling data are sparse. This study proposes a Transformer-based framework capable of forecasting fine-grained, city-level food safety risks by unifying over 11 million inspection records with supplemental demographic, economic, and environmental indicators extracted from the Statistical Yearbook. A three-stage pretraining design leverages partial supervision from the Wilson interval (capturing both safety and risk rankings), together with semi-supervised label refinement, to effectively utilize historical records even when local sample sizes are insufficient. Experimental evaluations on data from 2022 show that the proposed approach outperforms baselines significantly. A subsequent field experiment in collaboration with the Zhejiang Provincial Administration for Market Regulation further demonstrates improved detection rates and more efficient allocation of inspection resources compared to a manually developed plan. Observations of regulatory decision-making reveal a threshold-based heuristic employed by inspectors, hinting that additional training or decision-support interfaces could further enhance the impact of AI-generated risk scores. Overall, these findings underscore that a rigorous integration of large-scale public inspection data, Wilson interval-based confidence modeling, and advanced deep learning can facilitate earlier and more granular identification of food safety threats. By reducing reliance on reactive measures alone, the proposed framework has the potential to advance proactive, data-driven oversight of the global food supply.

</details>


### [104] [FRAMES: Guarded and Dual-Objective Skill Evolution for Agents in Policy-Governed Enterprise Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01772)
*Xuhui Wang,Ruoqi Shu,Chen Dan,Tianhua Xu,Mengxi Luo,Yanming Mai,Bo Wan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FRAMES是一个闭环框架，从现有资产冷启动可部署技能，通过共识驱动的变异、基于Pareto的准确率与成本选择以及反回归保证来演进技能，同时保持可审计性。在内部生产系统和tau-bench上均取得最优的准确率-成本权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理在执行受策略约束的企业工作流（如文档审计）时，面临三大挑战：运营反馈稀疏且无标签、单条规则编辑可能导致无关案例回归、以及必须在不增加推理成本和保持可审计性的前提下提升准确率。

Method: FRAMES采用闭环框架，包含：1）从现有资产冷启动初始技能；2）通过共识驱动的变异产生新候选技能；3）基于Pareto选择在准确率和成本之间进行多目标优化；4）引入反回归保证防止性能回退；5）整个过程保持可审计性。

Result: 在内部生产系统部署中，FRAMES在所有基线中取得了最优的准确率-成本权衡，并且在tau-bench基准上复现了相同的性能提升。

Conclusion: FRAMES有效解决了LLM代理在策略约束企业工作流中技能改进的难题，证明在不牺牲推理成本和可审计性的前提下，能够通过闭环演进持续提升代理准确率，且结果具有跨场景的泛化性。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly run policy-bound enterprise workflows such as document auditing, where they must apply rules consistently, ground every value, and stay auditable. Improving these agents is hard: operational feedback is sparse and unlabeled, edits to one rule can regress unrelated cases, and accuracy must improve without inflating inference cost or losing auditability. We present FRAMES, a closed-loop framework that cold-starts deployable skills from existing assets and then evolves them through consensus-based mutation, Pareto selection over accuracy and cost, and an anti-regression guarantee, all while preserving auditability. Deployed on our internal production system, FRAMES attains the best accuracy-cost trade-off among baselines, with the same gains reproduced on tau-bench.

</details>


### [105] [CockpitHAT: Dependency-Graph-Driven Hierarchical Attribution for Embodied Multi-Agent Cockpits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01805)
*Wei Wang,Shuanghe Liu,Zhu Zhuo,Jiaqi Zhong,Xiaozhao Zhao,Xiaojie Zuo,Jie Su*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CockpitHAT，一个分层归因框架，用于解决LLM多智能体系统中的"正确性崩塌"（Correctness Collapse）问题——即高层任务准确率高但过程级错误严重。框架采用依赖距离阈值替代位置窗口、通过具身适配器整合多通道证据、并在置信度加权分析师共识中施加安全提升。同时发布CockpitBench基准（212条标注故障轨迹），在多个基准上超越现有的文本哨兵SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM多智能体系统中存在"正确性崩塌"现象，模块级准确率高但过程级故障严重，尤其在汽车座舱等安全关键场景中，词法上正确的语句可能触发危险物理操作。现有归因方法仅依赖文本痕迹，无法捕获依赖结构、多通道证据和安全性评估，导致故障诊断不充分。

Method: 1) 以交互DAG的依赖距离阈值替代传统位置窗口，构建分层归因结构；2) 通过具身适配器（embodied adapter）整合多通道证据（对话、车辆状态、环境、记忆）；3) 采用置信度加权分析师共识，并对高风险故障施加安全提升（safety-uplift）以加强风险校准。

Result: 在Who&When基准上，CockpitHAT在Hand-Crafted和Algorithm-Generated两个split上分别达到77.9%/37.8%和86.5%/46.0%的agent-level/step-exact准确率，比文本SOTA ECHO高出至多17.6/16.7个百分点。在CockpitBench上达到78.3% agent-level和38.2% step-exact准确率。

Conclusion: 依赖感知、多通道、风险校准的归因范式为真实世界具身LLM多智能体系统的可靠故障诊断提供了有效方案。

Abstract: LLM multi-agent systems suffer from Correctness Collapse, where high task-level accuracy conceals severe process-level failures. This is especially hazardous in safety-critical embodied settings such as automotive cockpits, where lexically correct utterances may trigger dangerous physical operations. Existing attribution methods rely on text traces alone, missing dependency structure, multi-channel evidence, and safety-aware evaluation. We introduce CockpitHAT, a hierarchical attribution framework that replaces positional windows with dependency-distance thresholds from interaction DAGs, integrates multi-channel evidence via an embodied adapter, and applies a safety-uplift to high-risk failures during confidence-weighted analyst consensus. We further release CockpitBench, a benchmark of 212 annotated failure traces spanning dialogue, vehicle-state, environmental, and memory channels, each labeled with ISO 26262 ASIL severity via three-expert consensus. On the public Who&When benchmark, CockpitHAT achieves agent-level / step-exact accuracies of 77.9% / 37.8% on the Hand-Crafted split and 86.5% / 46.0% on the Algorithm-Generated split, surpassing the text-only SOTA ECHO by up to 17.6 / 16.7 points. On CockpitBench, it attains 78.3% agent-level and 38.2% step-exact accuracy. These results establish dependency-aware, multi-channel, risk-calibrated attribution as an effective paradigm for reliable failure diagnosis in real-world embodied LLM multi-agent systems.

</details>


### [106] [SearchMaster: Grounded and Regulated Self-Play for Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01822)
*Wentao Tan,Qiong Cao,Jiaqi Wang,Nan Duan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SearchMaster是一个自博弈框架，通过让单个LLM在本地搜索环境中自行生成、求解和验证搜索任务来训练搜索代理。它通过三个关键控制（证据链生成器ECG、搜索深度奖励SDR、过度打开惩罚OOP）解决自生成任务中的误导性信号问题，在六个深度搜索基准上将Qwen3.5-9B的平均准确率从38.19%提升到51.52%，无需人工标注或专家示范。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练基于LLM的搜索代理需要高质量的搜索数据（真实的多跳检索任务和有效的工具使用轨迹），但现有管线依赖人工编写任务、专家示范或更强的教师模型，成本高昂且难以规模化。作者希望突破这些外部依赖，实现模型自我训练。

Method: SearchMaster采用自博弈框架，核心包含三个控制机制：(1) Evidence-Chain Generator (ECG) 通过显式的跨文档证据链约束任务生成，减少伪多跳问题；(2) Search-Depth Reward (SDR) 用成功轨迹的搜索深度而非成功率来评估任务难度，确保保留的任务具有真正的搜索密集性；(3) Over-Opening Penalty (OOP) 通过惩罚过度打开文档来规范工具使用，避免长而浅的浏览。之后用GRPO对验证过的Proposer和Solver轨迹进行联合优化。

Result: 在六个深度搜索基准上，SearchMaster将Qwen3.5-9B骨干模型的平均准确率从38.19%提升至51.52%，其中在BrowseComp-Plus上获得30.1个百分点的提升。

Conclusion: SearchMaster证明了有约束和调节的自博弈能有效生成搜索代理训练数据，无需人工标注的QA对或专家示范，为搜索代理的自我训练提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Training LLM-based search agents requires high-quality search data: tasks that demand genuine multi-hop retrieval and trajectories that use search tools effectively. Existing pipelines often depend on human-written tasks, expert demonstrations, or stronger teacher models. We present SearchMaster, a self-play framework that trains a single LLM from search tasks it generates, solves, and verifies in a local search environment. The key challenge is that self-generated tasks and rollouts can yield misleading signals: pseudo multi-hop questions, success-rate difficulty estimates that ignore search depth, and rollouts with excessive opening but little targeted evidence acquisition. SearchMaster addresses these failure modes with three controls. An Evidence-Chain Generator (ECG) grounds task generation in explicit cross-document evidence chains to reduce pseudo multi-hop questions. A Search-Depth Reward (SDR) scores task difficulty by the search depth of successful rollouts rather than success rate alone, keeping retained tasks search-intensive. An Over-Opening Penalty (OOP) regulates tool use by discouraging excessive document opening, avoiding long but shallow browsing. Verified Proposer and Solver rollouts are then jointly optimized with GRPO. Across six deep-search benchmarks, SearchMaster improves a Qwen3.5-9B backbone from 38.19% to 51.52% average accuracy, with a 30.1-point gain on BrowseComp-Plus. These results show that grounded and regulated self-play can provide effective search-agent training data without human-labeled QA pairs or expert demonstrations. The code is available at https://github.com/WentaoTan/SearchMaster.

</details>


### [107] [PCSD: Persistent Consistency for Self-Distillation in Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01837)
*Chunji Lv,Yangguang Wei,Junlin Liu,Yang Gao,Ming Liu,Xinming Wang,Jinyang Wu,Guoren Wang,Changsheng Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出持久一致性自蒸馏（PCSD）方法，通过基于信号局部持久性的token级蒸馏权重，结合自适应窗口与指数衰减聚合，为大型语言模型智能体的强化学习提供密集且可靠的token级监督信号，在ALFWorld和WebShop任务上显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型智能体在复杂交互任务中的强化学习常受稀疏奖励困扰——一条长多轮轨迹可能仅获得单一的结果级信号。已有的OPSD方法虽可从特权教师处获得密集token级监督，但教师并非在每个位置都可靠。现有方法要么依赖孤立的token级差异（对噪声敏感），要么分配共享的step级权重（忽略位置差异），亟需更稳健的蒸馏权重设计。

Method: PCSD从教师偏好信号的局部持久性中推导token级蒸馏权重。具体包括：1）结合自适应窗口与指数衰减聚合来捕捉持久的相对教师支持；2）应用趋势感知调制以衰减局部下降的支持；3）通过sigmoid门控产生连续权重。蒸馏目标与GRPO联合优化，融合密集教师指导与稀疏环境反馈。

Result: 在不依赖推理期技巧的情况下，PCSD在两种骨干网络上均取得ALFWorld Overall最优结果，比GRPO高15.6和13.3个点，比SDAR高6.2和5.5个点；在WebShop上保持竞争力，并在未见过的ALFWorld分割上比GRPO提升15.8个点。

Conclusion: PCSD通过捕捉信号的持久性而非孤立的token差异来生成蒸馏权重，有效缓解了教师信号不可靠和稀疏奖励问题，为LLM智能体的强化学习提供了一种更稳健的密集监督方式。

Abstract: Large language model agents have shown strong potential in complex interactive tasks, yet their reinforcement learning (RL) is often hindered by sparse rewards, as a long multi-turn trajectory may receive only a single outcome-level signal. On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) provides dense token-level supervision from a privileged teacher, but the teacher may not be reliable at every position. Existing methods commonly rely on isolated token-level discrepancies, which can be sensitive to noise, or assign a shared step-level weight that may overlook positional variation. We propose Persistent Consistency Self-Distillation (PCSD), which derives token-level distillation weights from the local persistence of teacher-favoring signals. PCSD combines adaptive windows with exponentially decayed aggregation to capture persistent relative teacher support, applies trend-aware modulation to attenuate locally declining support, and produces continuous weights through sigmoid gating. The resulting objective is jointly optimized with GRPO, combining dense teacher guidance with sparse environmental feedback. Without inference-time skills, PCSD achieves the best ALFWorld Overall results among all baselines on both backbones, exceeding GRPO by 15.6 and 13.3 points and SDAR by 6.2 and 5.5 points, while remaining competitive on WebShop and gaining 15.8 points over GRPO on unseen ALFWorld split.

</details>


### [108] [FOCUS: FP4 Optimization via Coupled-Relaxation and Dual-Granularity Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01847)
*Xianglong Yan,Hong Liu,Chengzhu Bao,Tianao Zhang,Guanghua Yu,Jianchen Zhu,Yulun Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FOCUS提出了一种面向FP4量化的后训练量化框架，通过耦合松弛缩放（CRS）和双粒度缩放（DGS）实现了端到端的尺度学习，在MXFP4和NVFP4格式下均达到最先进的FP4精度，且不引入额外的推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLM）部署成本高昂，FP4量化虽然借助现代加速器的原生硬件支持提供了有吸引力的解决方案，但保持精度仍困难重重。现有方法将量化和反量化尺度紧密耦合，迫使两者均需遵循硬件要求的离散低精度格式（如MXFP4中的E8M0），然而量化尺度从不存储、无需遵循此约束，存在显著的未开发优化空间。

Method: 提出FOCUS框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 耦合松弛缩放（CRS）：用一个可学习的全精度系数松弛量化与反量化尺度之间的严格耦合，在不破坏硬件合规性的前提下实现更有效的优化；(2) 双粒度缩放（DGS）：以更细的子块粒度细化量化尺度，使模型能更精准地适应局部权重分布。

Result: 在多个LLM家族和基准测试上的实验表明，FOCUS在MXFP4和NVFP4两种格式下均取得了最先进的FP4精度，且不引入任何额外的推理开销。

Conclusion: FOCUS通过解耦量化和反量化尺度的约束并引入双粒度缩放，有效解决了FP4量化中的精度瓶颈，实现硬件合规与模型精度的兼顾，为低比特量化LLM部署提供了新的优化方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) achieve remarkable performance but are expensive to deploy due to their enormous size. FP4 quantization, with formats such as MXFP4 and NVFP4, offers an appealing solution with native hardware support on modern accelerators. However, maintaining accuracy under FP4 precision remains difficult. A key bottleneck lies in scale optimization: existing methods tightly couple the quantization and dequantization scales, forcing both to conform to the discrete low-precision format required by hardware, such as E8M0 in MXFP4. Yet the quantization scale is never stored and need not obey this constraint, suggesting a significant untapped optimization space. In this work, we propose FOCUS, a post-training quantization framework with end-to-end scale learning for FP4 Optimization via Coupled-Relaxation and Dual-Granularity Scaling. Coupled-Relaxation Scaling (CRS) relaxes the tight coupling between quantization and dequantization scales with a learnable full-precision coefficient, enabling more effective optimization without breaking hardware compliance. Dual-Granularity Scaling (DGS) further refines the quantization scale at a finer sub-block granularity, allowing more precise adaptation to local weight distributions. Experiments across multiple LLM families and benchmarks show that FOCUS achieves state-of-the-art FP4 accuracy under both MXFP4 and NVFP4 formats, while introducing no additional inference overhead. Code and quantized models will be released at https://github.com/tencent/AngelSlim.

</details>


### [109] [ReasonCast: Towards Explainable Time Series Forecasting with Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01875)
*Seunghan Lee,Jun Seo,Jaehoon Lee,Junhyeok Kang,Sangjun Han,Sungdong Yoo,Minjae Kim,Tae Yoon Lim,Dongwan Kang,Hwanil Choi,Soonyoung Lee,Wonbin Ahn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出任务融合的时间序列模型，将数值预测与可解释的文本推理整合在一次自回归生成中。作者构建了ReasonTS-Bench基准（涵盖五种基本时间序列模式）和ReasonCast微调框架，实验表明ReasonCast在预测准确性和因果推理可验证性上均优于现有LLM和专用TS模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列模型通常专精于单一任务——理解（返回文本答案）或生成（返回数值预测）。即使最近的统一模型也只是在同一架构中以任务分离的路径处理两类输出，无法在单个连贯响应中同时输出预测及其原因解释。本文旨在弥合这一差距，将预测（生成）与自解释（理解）整合为任务融合的单一框架。

Method: 作者提出：(1) ReasonTS-Bench基准，识别时间序列背后的五种基本模式并支持两任务的联合评估；(2) ReasonCast微调配方，可对任意LLM进行微调，使其通过单一自回归过程同时生成推理链和预测结果。该方法融合了数值时间序列预测与可解释的文本推理。

Result: 广泛实验表明，ReasonCast在预测准确性上优于LLM和时间序列专用模型，同时产出的推理具有可验证性和因果性。

Conclusion: 任务融合的时间序列建模是可行的且有价值的：单一模型可在一次自回归推理中同时完成数值预测与自解释推理，兼顾准确性与可解释性，为统一的TS理解和生成开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Most time series (TS) models are specialized for a single task, either understanding (i.e., returning text answers about a TS) or generation (i.e., returning a numeric forecast). Only recently have unified models begun to handle the two within a single architecture. Even these models, however, produce the two outputs as task-separated paths and cannot predict a series and explain why that prediction arises within a single coherent response. In this paper, we argue for a task-fused model that jointly produces 1) prediction (generation) and 2) selfexplanation (understanding), thereby integrating 1) numerical TS forecasting and 2) interpretable text reasoning within a single response. To enable the systematic study of this capability, we present both a benchmark and a recipe that jointly address the two tasks. The benchmark, ReasonTS-Bench, identifies five fundamental patterns underlying TS and enables the joint evaluation of both tasks. ReasonCast, our recipe for finetuning any LLM to perform both tasks jointly, yields a model that generates a reasoning chain and a forecast together in a single autoregressive pass. Extensive experiments show that ReasonCast outperforms both LLMs and TS models on prediction accuracy while producing verifiable, causal reasoning. Code is available at: https://github.com/seunghan96/reasoncast.

</details>


### [110] [Long-Horizon Autonomous Architecture Research with a Language-Model Agent: A Behavioural Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01995)
*Aon Safdar,Mohamed Saadeldin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了单个通用大语言模型作为唯一研究者，在长期神经架构设计任务中自主进行约100次连续实验的过程。研究发现生产率呈现"快速提升—饱和停滞—恢复"的阶段性结构，早期单一假设贡献了大部分精度提升；贪婪增量式假设主要源于工作流设计而非模型能力；智能体能够独立重新发现已有结论，并在纯通道注意力这一陌生领域推翻标准设计选择。结论认为工作流设计与智能体能力同等重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索单一通用大语言模型能否在长时间跨度内独立承担完整科研流程（从假设提出、实验实施到结果评估与记录），并考察其成功与失败的决定因素——是智能体能力还是工作流设计？

Method: 以单一LLM作为唯一研究者，配备源码与实验管理、实验跟踪、文献访问和持久记忆等科研权限工具，在三个递进阶段（逐步扩展工具面或问题规模）中自主执行约100个连续实验。智能体将非标准Vision Transformer从弱基线逐步优化，在小型基准上达到更强的效率模型，在ImageNet-1K上达到可用但未达SOTA的模型，并记录完整行为轨迹。

Result: 四个主要发现：(i)生产率呈现明显的阶段结构：早期快速提升、多打十多个假设的饱和墙、以及恢复期，恢复由扩展动作面而非改变底层模型触发；(ii)单一早期假设贡献了大部分精度提升，后续改进呈现长尾分布；(iii)贪婪增量式假设偏好主要是工作流诱导的——commit-or-discard评估规则与贪婪爬山同构，余下部分反映大胆失败后的风险厌恶和对熟悉文献的锚定；(iv)智能体独立重新发现既有结论，并在纯通道注意力这一陌生领域推翻标准设计。

Conclusion: 工作流设计在本研究中对科研产出的影响至少与智能体能力同等重要。提出未来研究方向：多样化搜索、预算型"登月"假设、显式分支（fork）、以及基于范式感知的重新验证。

Abstract: We study what happens when a single general-purpose large language model acts as the sole researcher on a long-horizon neural architecture design problem. The agent receives a scientific question, an initial hypothesis and motivation, a compute budget, and research affordances (source and experiment management, experiment tracking, literature access, and persistent memory), then autonomously proposes, implements, evaluates, and records experiments over an extended period. The study comprises three phases, separated by human-declared transitions, that progressively expand the agent's tool surface or problem scale. Across approximately 100 sequential experiments, the agent improves a non-standard Vision Transformer from a weak baseline to a stronger, efficient model on small benchmarks and a usable but sub-SOTA model on ImageNet-1K, while producing a dense behavioural trace. We report four findings.(i)Productivity exhibits a clear phase structure: rapid early gains, a multi-dozen-hypothesis saturation wall, and recovery, with recovery triggered by expanding the action surface rather than changing the underlying model.(ii)A single early hypothesis contributes more to accuracy gain, with later improvements long-tailed.(iii)The preference for greedy, incremental hypotheses is largely workflow-induced: a commit-or-discard evaluation rule is isomorphic to greedy hill-climbing; the remainder reflects risk aversion after bold failures and anchoring on familiar literature. (iv)The agent independently rediscovers established results and, in the unfamiliar regime of pure channel attention, overturns a standard design choice. We conclude that workflow design was at least as influential as agent capability in this study and propose diversified search, budgeted moonshot hypotheses, explicit forks, and regime-aware re-validation as testable directions for future autonomous research.

</details>


### [111] [Evolving in the Agent Jungle via History-Informed Opponent Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02005)
*Zhaofeng Zhang,Linhan Xia,Rui Liu,Yihao Wang,Binrui Shen,Shengxin Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出OASE（对手感知的选择性演化）方法，用于在多智能体动态环境中实现代理的策略自适应。OASE通过以对手策略的历史快照为锚定条件，对候选技能与现有技能进行配对比较，仅当估计收益增益超过接受阈值时才采纳候选技能，从而避免在环境持续演化时盲目更新策略。实验表明，OASE在首价拍卖和私有成本古诺竞争中相比Reflexion基线取得更低的最终均衡距离，同时接受更少的技能修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常通过修订技能库来实现行为适应，但在多智能体环境中对手会同时更新策略，导致环境本身持续演化。针对静态环境设计的技能修订方法在此类场景中相当于针对过时的参照进行更新，无法有效适应动态环境。

Method: OASE方法在由历史对手策略快照锚定的相同条件下，对候选技能（candidate skill）与现有技能（incumbent）进行配对比较（paired comparisons），只有当候选技能估计的收益增益超过接受阈值（acceptance threshold）时才予以采纳，从而实现有选择性而非盲目性的策略演化。

Result: 在首价拍卖（first-price auctions）和私有成本古诺竞争（private-cost Cournot competition）两个决策场景中，相比于Reflexion风格基线，OASE在两种环境中均获得了更低的最终均衡距离，同时接受了显著更少的技能修正，从而抑制了缺乏足够收益支撑的策略变化。

Conclusion: OASE以证据锚定的选择机制取代盲目更新，使代理即使在对手持续演化的条件下也能稳定且高效地适应，为多智能体动态环境中的LLM代理策略适应提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Learning to adapt strategies through interaction is a key step toward more general and autonomous LLM agents. Existing approaches typically achieve behavioral adaptation by revising skill libraries. However, in multi-agent environments, opponents may simultaneously update their strategies, causing the environment itself to evolve continuously. Applying skill-revision methods designed for static environments in such settings therefore amounts to updating against an obsolete reference. To address this challenge, we introduce OASE (Opponent-Aware Selective Evolution), which identifies and adopts genuinely beneficial skill revisions in dynamic multi-agent environments. Specifically, OASE conducts paired comparisons between a candidate skill and the incumbent under identical conditions anchored by historical snapshots of opponent strategies, and adopts the candidate only when its estimated payoff gain exceeds an acceptance threshold. We evaluate OASE in two decision-making scenarios: first-price auctions and private-cost Cournot competition. Experimental results show that, compared with a Reflexion-style baseline, OASE achieves a lower final equilibrium distance in both environments while accepting substantially fewer skill revisions, thereby suppressing strategy changes that lack sufficient payoff support. OASE therefore replaces blind updating with evidence-anchored selection, allowing agents to adapt stably and efficiently even as opponents continuously evolve.

</details>


### [112] [Before Reasoning Fails: Pre-Evidence Procedural Failures in Agentic RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02011)
*Daeyoung Roh,Donghee Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于智能体的检索增强生成（RAG）系统中一种特定的失败模式：智能体检索到候选片段但未仔细阅读便直接给出答案。作者将错误答案分解为“证据读取前纪律失败”和“黄金证据读取后失败”两类，发现两者在很大程度上不重叠（同时触发率仅11.2%-13.1%）。作者提出Read-Gate——一个最小运行时不变式，强制智能体在搜索后、作答前必须阅读证据，使LLM准确率提升14.9-19.9个点（原本会跳过阅读的轨迹）以及3.2-9.4个点（完整最小推理单元）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体RAG系统可能在检验证据条件推理之前就失败：智能体检索到候选片段却不查看就直接给出最终答案。这种失败发生在推理层面之前，需要将其作为轨迹层面的程序性属性来研究，而非仅关注最终答案的正确性。

Method: 使用保存的工具调用轨迹、检索到的证据、已读段落和最终答案，将错误答案分解为两类失败：预证据纪律失败（检索后未读取即作答）和黄金证据读取后失败（读取了但有误）。在HotpotQA、2WikiMultiHopQA和MuSiQue上收集12,000条成对轨迹，使用正则表达式和spaCy两种实体抽取器进行分类。随后提出Read-Gate运行时不变式，强制智能体在搜索后和最终作答前必须阅读证据。

Result: 两类失败在很大程度上非冗余，同时触发率仅11.2%-13.1%（跨正则和spaCy抽取器）。Read-Gate强制阅读使LLM准确率提升14.9-19.9个点（原会跳过阅读的轨迹）和3.2-9.4个点（完整最小推理单元）。此外，更大的隐藏思维预算并不必然增加证据检查行为。

Conclusion: 证据收集应作为轨迹层面的控制问题来独立评估，与答案侧推理分开。这一发现强调了在智能体RAG流程中，读取纪律（read discipline）是影响最终答案质量的关键程序性因素。

Abstract: Agentic retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems can fail before evidence-conditioned reasoning is tested: an agent may retrieve candidate snippets but finalize without inspecting them. We study this failure mode as a procedural property of the agent trajectory, decomposing wrong answers into pre-evidence discipline failures and post-gold-read failures using saved tool-call traces, retrieved evidence, read passages, and final answers. Across 12,000 paired trajectories on HotpotQA, 2WikiMultiHopQA, and MuSiQue, the two failure types are largely non-redundant: the both-trigger rate is in [11.2%, 13.1%] across regex and spaCy entity extractors. We then evaluate Read-Gate, a minimal runtime invariant requiring an agent to read after search and before finalization. Forced reading improves LLM-Acc by 14.9-19.9 points on trajectories that would otherwise skip reading and by 3.2-9.4 points on full minimal-reasoning cells. Additional diagnostics show that larger hidden thinking budgets do not necessarily increase evidence inspection. Together, these results indicate that evidence-gathering should be evaluated as a trajectory-level control problem, separately from answer-side reasoning.

</details>


### [113] [EduZone: A Framework for Evaluating LLM Safety for K-12 Students and Teachers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02024)
*Junyeong Park,Jieun Han,Haneul Yoo,So-Yeon Ahn,Jinsung Yoon,Alice Oh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EduZone，一个用于评估大语言模型在K-12教育场景中安全性的自动化、可扩展框架。该框架结合学生/教师使用情境、细粒度课程概念以及6类风险类别和28个子类别，生成情境化的对抗性交互，并用四种安全级别评估十个LLM，发现教育特定风险和动态多轮交互中的漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在K-12教育中被广泛使用，但现有的安全评估很少考察有害或不适当内容在LLM与学生/教师交互中的出现方式，缺乏针对教育场景的系统性安全评估框架。

Method: EduZone框架系统性地结合三要素：（1）学生和教师面向的LLM使用情境；（2）细粒度的课程概念；（3）覆盖传统和教育特定危害的6个风险类别及28个子类别，以此生成情境化对抗交互。交互分为三种设置：单轮请求、静态多轮对话和动态多轮对话。使用四种安全级别（拒绝、安全协助、带安全指导的风险协助、完全风险协助）评估十个LLM。

Result: 评估结果显示，LLM对教育特定风险和动态多轮交互表现出更大的脆弱性，而现有的安全护栏无法充分解决这些风险。

Conclusion: EduZone通过提供自动化、可扩展的评估框架，推动K-12教育中LLM安全性的发展，支持更安全的LLM在K-12教育中的开发与部署。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used across diverse tasks in K-12 education, yet existing safety evaluations rarely examine how harmful or inappropriate content appears in interactions between LLMs and students or teachers. To address this, we present EduZone, an evaluation framework for LLM safety across diverse educational scenarios. Our framework systematically combines (1) student- and teacher-facing LLM usage contexts, (2) fine-grained curriculum concepts, and (3) 6 risk categories and 28 subcategories spanning both conventional and education-specific harms to generate contextually grounded adversarial interactions. We construct these interactions in three settings: single-turn requests, static multi-turn conversations, and dynamic multi-turn conversations. Using these interactions, we evaluate ten LLMs using four safety levels: refusal, safe assistance, risky assistance with safety guidance, and fully risky assistance. Our results reveal greater vulnerability to education-specific risks and dynamic multi-turn interactions, while existing safety guardrails fail to adequately address these risks. EduZone advances LLM safety in education by providing an automated, scalable evaluation framework that supports the development and deployment of safer LLMs in K-12 education.

</details>


### [114] [HPFA: Hypergraph-Based Paired Failure Attribution for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02026)
*Runchuan Zhu,Hongbin Lai,Bowen Jiang,Junrui Zhang,Zhangheng LI,Ostap Kilbasovych,Junyuan Hong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HPFA框架，利用超图结构的成对失败归因方法，通过比较失败推理路径与参考成功路径的超边来定位根因，高效合成归因数据并训练轻量级归因模型，显著提升推理准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反思机制对LLM推理至关重要，但其效果依赖准确归因失败到具体推理步骤，而现有模型缺乏此能力。现有失败归因方法要么需要昂贵的逐步反事实测试且随轨迹长度扩展性差，要么将推理轨迹视为扁平序列而忽略其内在的非线性逻辑依赖关系。

Method: 提出基于超图的成对失败归因（HPFA）框架，通过将目标失败推理路径的超边与参考成功路径进行比较来归因失败根因。该方法通过缩小搜索空间高效定位根因，并支持可扩展的归因数据合成，用于通过监督微调和强化学习训练轻量级归因模型。

Result: 在数学推理和智能体编码任务上的实验表明，HPFA能显著提高归因准确性和效率，训练得到的归因模型在测试时持续提升推理准确率，优于缺乏图结构或成对分析的基线方法。

Conclusion: HPFA通过捕捉推理轨迹中非线性逻辑依赖关系并进行成对比较，有效解决了现有归因方法在高成本与忽略结构信息方面的问题，为提升LLM反思推理能力提供了可扩展且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Reflection is a powerful mechanism for LLM reasoning, yet its effectiveness hinges on accurately attributing failures to specific reasoning steps, a capability that current models notably lack. Existing failure attribution methods either require expensive step-by-step counterfactual testing that scales poorly with trajectory length, or treat reasoning traces as flat sequences that ignore the inherent non-linear logical dependencies. We propose a hypergraph-based paired failure attribution (HPFA) framework that attributes the failure root cause by comparing the hyperedges of the targeted failure reasoning path against a reference successful path. By reducing the search space, our method efficiently localizes root causes and enables scalable synthesis of attribution data for training a lightweight attributor model via supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning. Experiments on mathematical reasoning and agentic coding tasks demonstrate that HPFA can dramatically increase attribution accuracy and efficiency, and the trained attributor consistently improves reasoning accuracy at test time, outperforming baselines that lack graph structure or paired analysis.

</details>


### [115] [Instruction-Conditioned Exploration with Asymmetric Reinforcement Learning and Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02087)
*Jim Dilkes,Vahid Yazdanpanah,Sebastian Stein*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出指令条件化探索（ICE）方法，通过向训练提示中补充不同指令来增强LLM在强化学习训练中的行为覆盖范围，并结合非对称RL/SD训练目标将探索行为迁移到无条件的测试策略。在数学推理任务上，ICE使Qwen3-1.7B的pass@1性能在4K响应长度下相对DAPO提升5.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的强化学习后训练中，动作空间结构与传统RL不同，给探索带来了独特挑战。预训练LLM具有广泛知识和灵活性，需要新的方法来利用这些特性在训练时主动生成多样化的经验，以提高行为覆盖率。

Method: 提出指令条件化探索（ICE），在训练时向任务提示中添加多种不同指令以增加行为覆盖；同时提出Asymmetric-RL/SD目标，结合强化学习和自蒸馏训练目标，将探索到的行为迁移到无条件的测试时策略。

Result: 在数学推理任务上，ICE结合Asymmetric-RL/SD目标使Qwen3-1.7B在4K响应长度的held-out pass@1性能相对DAPO训练提升5.0%，并且在更长的8K上下文下改进仍然保持。

Conclusion: ICE方法通过指令条件化增强探索，配合非对称RL/SD目标有效提升了LLM在数学推理任务上的测试性能，并且在不同上下文长度下均表现出泛化性。

Abstract: Post-training Large Language Models (LLMs) with Reinforcement Learning (RL) has become an important tool for improving model capabilities, but the LLM action-space structure introduces challenges distinct from classical RL, with implications for inducing exploration. New methods are required that leverage the broad knowledge and flexibility of pre-trained LLMs to deliberately generate diverse experience at training time. We propose Instruction-Conditioned Exploration (ICE), which supplements task prompts during training with one of several distinct instructions, increasing the coverage of behaviours attempted. To facilitate ICE, we propose Asymmetric-RL/SD, a combined Reinforcement Learning and Self-Distillation training objective, to transfer explored behaviours to the unconditioned test-time policy. ICE with the Asymmetric-RL/SD objective improves Qwen3-1.7B held-out pass@1 performance at $4$K response length on mathematical reasoning tasks by $5.0\%$ relative to training with DAPO, with improvement persisting at a longer 8K context.

</details>


### [116] [MemArbiter: Decision-Time Memory Arbitration for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02113)
*Jiajun Dong,Yutao Hu,Fengrui Fan,Shihan Dou,Yueming Wu,Deqing Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MemArbiter是一个功能感知的记忆仲裁框架，通过将交互历史分解为原子项、组织成五个功能记忆库，并采用动态记忆显著性控制来弥合LLM智能体在长时程任务中的"记忆-行动鸿沟"（Memory-Action Gap）。在ALFWorld基准上，MemArbiter在500和750 token预算下分别达到82.8%和92.5%的成功率，超出最强基线20.9和25.4个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在长时程任务中需要跨步骤信息来保持行动连贯性。现有方法虽改善了记忆的可访问性，但即使信息可访问，由于信息组织、优先级排序和呈现方式不佳，行动相关信息仍可能无法有效指导当前决策。作者将此问题定义为"记忆-行动鸿沟"（Memory-Action Gap），并聚焦于记忆管理引发的这一鸿沟问题。

Method: MemArbiter框架包含四个核心组件：(1) 将交互历史分解为原子记忆项；(2) 按功能组织为五个记忆库（Memory Banks）；(3) 组合库级需求（bank-level demand）、项级相关性（item-level relevance）、焦点-环境表示（focal-ambient representations）；(4) 引入时间呈现门控（temporal presentation gate）动态控制记忆显著性。

Result: 在ALFWorld上，MemArbiter使用开放权重行动生成模型，在500和750 token内存预算下分别取得82.8%和92.5%的成功率，比最强基线分别高出20.9和25.4个百分点。此外，MemArbiter还改善了失败后恢复能力，减少了失败动作重复和状态-动作重复发生。

Conclusion: 结果表明，功能感知的记忆仲裁能够使可访问的信息更有效地指导行动，弥合了LLM智能体在长时程任务中的"记忆-行动鸿沟"，为智能体记忆管理提供了新的设计范式。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents must retain and use cross-step information to act coherently in long-horizon tasks. Existing methods improve memory accessibility, yet action-relevant information may still fail to guide the current decision because it is poorly formed, organized, prioritized, or presented. We call this post-access failure the Memory-Action Gap. We propose MemArbiter, a function-aware memory arbitration framework that addresses the memory-management-induced component of this gap. MemArbiter decomposes interaction histories into atomic items, organizes them into five functional Memory Banks, and combines bank-level demand, item-level relevance, focal-ambient representations, and a temporal presentation gate to dynamically control memory salience. We evaluate MemArbiter on ALFWorld against Flat Retrieval and Flat Recency under unified per-step memory budgets. With an open-weight action-generation model, MemArbiter achieves success rates of 82.8% and 92.5% under 500- and 750-token budgets, outperforming the strongest baseline by 20.9 and 25.4 percentage points, respectively. It also improves post-failure recovery and reduces failed-action repetition and state-action recurrence. These results show that function-aware memory arbitration enables accessible information to guide actions more effectively.

</details>


### [117] [Beyond Solution-Centric Search: Adaptive Inquiry and Knowledge Revision for Autonomous ML Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02143)
*Shaokang Fu,Yulong Tao,Linbo Jin,Jiarong Zhao,Qiming Shi,Tianjun Pan,Haonan Li,Chengyu Wang,Jia Wu,Chengfu Huo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出信息范式（information paradigm）替代传统解决方案为中心的搜索范式，并据此设计了一个名为Iris的"探究-修订"循环系统。Iris通过不断演化的信息状态（由可修订的、带范围和状态标注的主张构成）来指导解决方案改进，而非通过在树/图/链结构中组织候选方案的搜索。在MLE-Bench基准上，Iris在12小时预算下达到64.9%的任意奖牌率，为对比系统中最高，并在跨领域任务上展现泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM agent在解决长时程自主研究任务时多采用树、图或链等结构组织候选方案改进，即搜索过程决定了信息如何获取和管理。这种"方案中心搜索"范式在有限预算下处理需要相互依赖决策的任务时存在局限，作者认为信息获取和管理的方式应当成为核心，而非依附于方案搜索过程。

Method: Iris采用"探究-修订"循环（inquiry-revision loop），包含两个核心环节：（1）信息获取：从当前信息状态生成局部行动计划，使用认知行动（epistemic actions）探测定决策关键的不确定项，且不修改保留中的方案；（2）信息管理：跨实验综合观察结果，将其组织为任务知识——由带明确范围和状态的可修订主张构成，随新证据更新，并按所需详细程度从原始证据、结构化摘要或任务知识构建每个决策上下文。

Result: 在MLE-Bench基准上，Iris在12小时预算下达到64.9%的任意奖牌率，为所有对比系统中最高的成绩。此外，在包括时序工程（harness engineering）和模型后训练（model post-training）等四个任务上，Iris展现了跨领域的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 与传统方案中心搜索范式相比，信息范式的Iris系统通过显式建模信息的获取、管理与利用，更有效地支持长时程、多决策点且预算受限的自主研究任务，其在MLE-Bench上的领先表现验证了该范式的有效性。

Abstract: Long-horizon autonomous research tasks such as machine learning engineering require systems to make interdependent decisions under a limited budget. Existing LLM-based agents typically organize candidate-solution improvement through tree, graph, or chain structures, meaning that the search process determines how information is acquired and managed. We call this design solution-centric search and propose instead the information paradigm, in which an evolving information state represents the system's understanding of the task and guides solution improvement. We instantiate this paradigm in Iris, an inquiry-revision loop. For information acquisition, Iris generates local action plans from the current information state and uses epistemic actions to probe decision-critical unknowns without modifying the retained solution. For information management, Iris synthesizes observations across experiments into task knowledge composed of revisable claims with explicit scope and status. It updates this knowledge as new evidence arrives and constructs each decision context from raw evidence, structured summaries, or task knowledge at the required level of detail. On MLE-Bench, Iris attains a 64.9% any-medal rate under a 12-hour budget, the highest among compared systems. Across four tasks spanning harness engineering and model post-training, Iris also demonstrates cross-domain generalization.

</details>


### [118] [Beyond the Mean: Multi-Moment Policy Optimization for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02149)
*Yijun Zhang,Yule Xie,Jiaxin Ding,Xin Ding,Fan Xu,Haoxiang Zhang,Luoyi Fu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于矩（moment)的视角来审视大规模语言模型推理中的策略优化问题，将失败概率视为随机变量并通过其各阶矩刻画优化目标。作者提出MMPO（Multi-Moment Policy Optimization）框架，联合最小化失败概率分布的多个矩，并进一步开发了通用的矩变换框架以统一理解更广泛的策略优化目标族。实验在五个数学推理基准和不同规模模型上验证了MMPO的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法在提升LLM推理能力时，通常仅针对问题失败概率的单一矩（如期望）进行优化，忽略了失败概率分布的整体结构。作者认为这种单一矩优化无法充分刻画分布特性，需要引入多矩视角来更系统地设计优化目标。

Method: 本文提出MMPO框架，将随机采样问题的失败概率视为随机变量，通过联合最小化该分布的多个矩来优化策略。MMPO具有直观的操作意义：最小化获得首个成功回答所需的期望截断时间。此外，作者还提出了一个通用的矩变换框架，可以系统地诱导不同的矩轮廓，从而统一涵盖更广泛的策略优化目标族。

Result: 在五个数学推理基准测试和不同规模的模型上，MMPO一致性地优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: 矩视角为LLM推理策略优化目标的设计提供了新的洞见，MMPO及矩变换框架有望推动该领域的进一步发展。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has become a central paradigm for improving the reasoning capabilities of large language models. Existing methods generally aim to reduce the failure probabilities induced across problems. In this paper, we introduce a moment-based perspective on policy optimization for LLM reasoning by treating the failure probability of a randomly sampled problem as a random variable and characterizing optimization objectives through its moments. Under this perspective, many existing methods optimize only a single moment of the failure-probability distribution, leaving its broader distributional structure largely uncharacterized. We propose \textbf{M}ulti-\textbf{M}oment \textbf{P}olicy \textbf{O}ptimization (MMPO), a novel policy optimization framework that jointly minimizes multiple moments of the failure-probability distribution. MMPO admits a direct operational interpretation as minimizing the expected truncated time required to obtain the first successful response. Beyond MMPO, we further develop a general moment-transformation framework that systematically induces different moment profiles and provides a unified view of a broader family of policy optimization objectives. Experiments across five mathematical reasoning benchmarks and models of different scales demonstrate that MMPO consistently outperforms strong baselines. We hope this moment-based perspective offers new insights into the design of policy optimization objectives for LLM reasoning.

</details>


### [119] [Auditing Data Provenance in LLM Fine-tuning via Intrinsic Distributional Fingerprints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02154)
*Zirui Huang,Yunlong Mao,Wei Tong,Tingting Wu,Xin Ge,Sheng Zhong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DPA（Distribution Provenance Audit），一种后验的黑盒审计框架，用于检测LLM在微调过程中对专有数据的知识产权侵权，即使面对数据改写和知识蒸馏等恶意规避手段仍保持鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定制化LLM的普及使得未经授权在专有数据上微调导致的Data IP侵权风险日益严峻。现有审计技术要么需要数据准备或训练阶段的干预，要么在面对数据改写和知识蒸馏等恶意混淆手段时表现脆弱，亟需一种后验、黑盒且鲁棒的审计方法。

Method: DPA基于一个关键洞察：无论微调策略如何规避溯源，保持模型实用性的需求会约束模型保留语义内容与词汇形式的基本交集。DPA将此持久的词汇-语义交集捕获为内在的分布性指纹，将审计建模为统计假设检验，通过无偏输出采样量化这些指纹，以可靠地拒绝"未使用数据"的零假设。

Result: 在医学和法律微调任务上的大量实验表明，DPA显著优于现有基线方法，并且在面对采用改写和知识蒸馏的对抗性训练者时保持鲁棒性。此外，研究发现高保真分布指纹在实现可靠审计的同时，也可能被用于隐私攻击，揭示了双用途张力。

Conclusion: DPA提供了一种有效的后验、黑盒数据溯源审计方案，在恶意规避场景下依然稳健；但其内在的双用途张力（审计与隐私攻击共存）提示需要关注该技术的潜在负面效应。

Abstract: The proliferation of customized Large Language Models (LLMs) poses critical risks of Data Intellectual Property (Data IP) infringement via unauthorized fine-tuning on proprietary data. Existing audit techniques are limited, as they require intervention during data preparation or training and remain fragile under malicious obfuscations such as data paraphrasing and knowledge distillation.
  We propose \textit{Distribution Provenance Audit (DPA)}, a post-hoc framework for auditing data IP infringement in LLM fine-tuning under black-box and malicious settings. DPA is grounded in a critical insight: regardless of fine-tuning tactics to evade provenance, the practical necessity of maintaining utility constrains the model to preserve the fundamental intersection of semantic substance and lexical form. Accordingly, DPA captures this persistent lexical-semantic intersection as intrinsic distributional fingerprints. The framework formulates the audit as a statistical hypothesis test, effectively quantifying these fingerprints via unbiased output sampling to reliably reject the null hypothesis of non-usage.
  Extensive experiments on medical and legal fine-tuning tasks show that DPA consistently outperforms existing baselines, remaining robust against adversarial trainers employing paraphrasing and knowledge distillation. We further highlight a fundamental dual-use tension: the same high-fidelity distributional fingerprints enabling reliable auditing may also facilitate privacy attacks.

</details>


### [120] [From Profiling to Synthesis: Benchmarking Implicit Behavioral Alignment in Personalized LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02171)
*Jiajia Song,Bobo Li,Haiwen Yi,Zibo Ji,Meishan Zhang,Hao Fei,Min Zhang,Mong-Li Lee,Wynne Hsu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出IBA-Bench，一个基于纵向交互历史评估智能体隐式行为对齐的基准，并配套提出IBA-Agent框架。通过广泛检索和轨迹级对齐来解决个性化智能体中的"知识到行动"差距问题，实验表明现有LLM智能体在个性化上仍有挑战，而IBA-Agent在九个应用领域显著提升了行为对齐能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化智能体基准大多依赖静态偏好快照、固定交互日志或对预定义用户画像的问答，无法捕捉用户偏好的动态演化，也未能评估"偏好条件化任务执行"这一能力。作者将此差距称为"知识到行动"差距（knowledge-to-action gap），即智能体即便从历史中获取了用户偏好知识，也难以在实际执行任务时隐含地遵循这些约束。

Method: 1）提出IBA-Bench基准：基于纵向交互历史构建，包含噪声、隐式线索和时间不一致性，评估智能体是否能在执行任务时满足从历史交互中推断出的隐式用户约束。2）提出IBA-Agent框架：通过广泛检索（broad retrieval）获取相关历史信息，并在轨迹级别（trajectory-level）进行对齐，以调和执行任务时的冲突优先级。

Result: 在IBA-Bench上的实验表明，有效的个性化对现有最先进的LLM智能体仍是一个显著挑战；所提出的IBA-Agent在覆盖九个应用领域的复杂场景中大幅提升了行为对齐性能。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了现有个性化评测中"知识到行动"的关键差距，并通过IBA-Bench和IBA-Agent共同推动了从静态偏好理解向动态隐式行为对齐的范式转变。研究结果表明，仅让智能体"知道"用户偏好是不够的，关键在于在执行中"落实"这些偏好。

Abstract: Large Language Models have enabled increasingly capable autonomous agents, yet personalization remains critical for making such agents practically useful. Recent benchmarks have begun evaluating personalization in agents, but they largely rely on static preference snapshots, fixed interaction logs, or question answering over predefined user profiles. Such designs fail to capture the complexity of evolving user preferences and neglect preference-conditioned task execution-a discrepancy we term as the knowledge-to-action gap. To address this challenge, we introduce IBA-Bench, a benchmark for implicit behavioral alignment constructed from longitudinal interaction histories that contain noise, implicit cues, and temporal inconsistencies. Unlike prior work, IBA-Bench evaluates whether an agent can execute tasks while satisfying implicit user constraints inferred from historical interactions. We further propose IBA-Agent, an agent framework that reconciles conflicting priorities through broad retrieval and trajectory-level alignment. Experiment results on IBA-Bench show that effective personalization remains a significant challenge for state-of-the-art LLM agents, and the proposed IBA-Agent substantially improves behavioral alignment in complex scenarios across nine application domains.

</details>


### [121] [MechGeo: Autoformalizing and Proving Euclidean Geometry in Lean 4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02295)
*Hao Shen,Junyu Guo,Tian Cui,Yuxuan Xiao,Lihong Zhi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了MechGeo，一个Mathlib原生的智能体框架，同时解决欧几里得几何的忠实自动形式化和认证证明构造问题。通过GeoFormalizer将非正式问题表示为GeoIR并确定性翻译为Lean 4，GeoProver构建证明计划并经Lean内核验证，在43道IMO几何题中成功证明29道，其余14道构造反例并在专家修正后全部证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧几里得几何的自动形式化（autoformalization）和形式化证明构造是两个长期挑战。现有方法要么在形式化步骤中缺乏忠实性保障，要么在证明构造中缺乏可信的验证机制。作者旨在构建一个端到端框架，将非正式几何问题可靠地翻译为Lean 4形式化语句，同时通过Lean内核验证所有证明和反例，从而建立可信的自动形式化几何推理基础。

Method: MechGeo框架包含两个核心组件：GeoFormalizer将非正式问题表示为中间表示GeoIR，通过确定性翻译生成Lean 4语句，并利用结构诊断和语义评估迭代修复候选语句；GeoProver负责构造几何证明计划，推导中间引理，并通过库中验证过的策略选择性地对合适子目标进行代数化（algebraize）。代数证书可由Singular或SymPy生成，但所有生成的证明和反例最终都由Lean内核验证。

Result: 在7个LLM骨干网络上展示了实质性的自动形式化改进，尤其对直接翻译能力较弱的模型提升显著。在43道历史IMO几何题中，GeoFormalizer生成的正式语句有29道被GeoProver成功证明；其余14道构造了Lean验证的反例，且经专家修正后所有修复语句均获证明。连同IMO 2026 Problem 2，构成了迄今最大的经内核验证的IMO几何问题Lean证明集合。在LEAP的Lean-IMO-Bench的14道几何题中，MechGeo首次证明了12道，正式反驳了剩余2道，并证明了两个修复后的语句。

Conclusion: 反例引导的诊断、几何推理和认证符号计算相结合，构成了可信形式化几何的实用基础。MechGeo证明了在自动形式化与形式化证明之间存在可操作的协同机制——以Lean内核验证为核心，将自动化几何推理置于可信赖的基础之上。

Abstract: We present MechGeo, a Mathlib native agentic framework that jointly addresses faithful autoformalization and certified proof construction for Euclidean geometry. In this framework, GeoFormalizer represents informal problems in GeoIR, deterministically translates them into Lean 4, and iteratively repairs candidate statements using structural diagnostics and semantic evaluation. GeoProver constructs geometric proof plans, derives intermediate lemmas, and selectively algebraizes suitable subgoals through a library verified in Lean. Singular or SymPy may generate algebraic certificates, but all resulting proofs and counterexamples are checked by Lean's kernel. Experiments across seven LLM backbones show substantial improvements in autoformalization, particularly for models with weaker direct translation performance. On 43 historical IMO geometry problems, GeoFormalizer generates formal statements that GeoProver proves in 29 cases; for the remaining 14, it constructs counterexamples verified in Lean and proves all repaired statements after expert correction. Together with IMO 2026 Problem 2, this yields, to the best of our knowledge, the largest reported collection of automated, kernel-checked Lean proofs for IMO geometry problems. On the 14 geometry statements in LEAP's Lean-IMO-Bench, MechGeo proves 12 for the first time, formally refutes the remaining two, and proves both repaired statements. These results establish counterexample guided diagnosis, geometric reasoning, and certified symbolic computation as a practical foundation for trustworthy formal geometry.

</details>


### [122] [SkillTrace: Traversing a Query-Skill Graph for Composable LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02356)
*Yue Yao,Shengyuan Wang,Xin Chen,Minke Zhang,Jia He,Bingjun Luo,Tom Gedeon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillTrace是一个基于三层图结构的技能检索框架，将用户查询组织成语义层次结构，通过组合关系、相似度和依赖关系三个层面的图传播来识别完整且可执行的技能组合，在SkillsBench和ALFWorld数据集上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型代理需要通过组合技能库中可复用的技能来解决复杂任务，而关键挑战不仅在于检索单个相关技能，更在于识别完整且可执行的技能组合。现有方法无法有效处理技能组合时需要考虑的组合关系、相似度和依赖关系。

Method: SkillTrace构建一个三层图结构：(1) 组合关系层——组织用户查询中技能请求之间的组合关系；(2) 相似度层——匹配技能查询与技能库中候选技能之间的语义相似度；(3) 依赖关系层——建模所选候选技能之间的依赖关系。系统将用户查询组织成语义层次结构，匹配技能查询和候选技能，并在技能依赖关系上进行传播。

Result: 在SkillsBench上成功率达到53.17%，在ALFWorld上达到91.43%，均达到当前最优性能。同时在不同主干语言模型上取得一致性改进，展示了图基技能检索方法的通用性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 三层图结构能够有效解决技能组合检索问题，通过同时建模组合关系、相似度和依赖关系，可以识别完整且可执行的技能组成，且该方法对不同的骨干语言模型具有广泛的适用性。

Abstract: Large language model agents increasingly solve complex tasks by composing reusable skills from a library. To address this, the key challenge is not merely to retrieve individually relevant skills, but to identify a complete and executable skill composition. In this paper, we argue that this problem can be solved in a graph with three levels: compositional relations among skill queries, similarity between queries and candidates in the skill library, and the dependencies among the selected candidates. We introduce SkillTrace, which organizes the user query into a semantic hierarchy, matches skill queries and candidates, and propagates over the skill dependencies. Experiments on SkillsBench and ALFWorld demonstrate that SkillTrace achieves state-of-the-art performance, reaching a success rate of 53.17% on SkillsBench and 91.43% on ALFWorld. SkillTrace also delivers consistent improvements across different backbone language models, demonstrating the generality and robustness of graph-based skill retrieval.

</details>


### [123] [Cooperative Coevolution for Resource-Constrained Agentic LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02391)
*Zhiyuan Wang,Shengcai Liu,Jiahao Wu,Ning Lu,Hui Ouyang,Shaofeng Zhang,Haoze Lv,Ke Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Cooperative Parameter-subspace Evolution Strategy (CoPES)，一种合作协同进化方法，将LLM智能体的完整参数空间分解为低维子空间并进行协同搜索，以在资源受限环境下实现内存高效且训练时间可接受的智能体后训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具使用型大语言模型智能体产生长序列多轮轨迹，基于梯度的方法后训练内存开销巨大；进化策略（ES）虽然无需反向传播且内存高效，但GPU训练时长过高，在仅有少量GPU的资源受限场景下训练时间过长。

Method: CoPES采用合作协同进化框架，将全参数空间分解为多个低维子空间，并在各子空间上并行协同搜索，以提升优化效率、降低训练时间。

Result: 在Qwen3.5-4B工具使用智能体的数学任务后训练中，基于五个难度不同的基准，在GPU小时预算与全参数GRPO最优验证检查点相当的情况下，CoPES恢复了GRPO验证准确率增益的92%（标准ES仅67%），理论GPU内存需求不足全参数GRPO的八分之一；在五个基准的所有pass@k指标上均优于标准ES和基于LoRA的GRPO，并在问答任务上进一步验证了优势。

Conclusion: CoPES在资源受限条件下实现了内存需求与训练时间之间更好的权衡，为智能体LLM后训练提供了高效方案。

Abstract: Tool-using large language model (LLM) agents produce long, multi-turn trajectories, making gradient-based post-training memory-intensive. Evolution strategies (ES) enable memory-efficient full-parameter post-training without backpropagation and can eventually match the performance of gradient-based reinforcement learning (RL). However, resource-constrained settings typically offer only a few GPUs, so the high GPU-hour requirements of ES translate into prohibitively long training times. To address this, we introduce Cooperative Parameter-subspace Evolution Strategy (CoPES), a cooperative coevolutionary method that decomposes the full parameter space into lower-dimensional subspaces and searches over them cooperatively to improve optimization efficiency. We post-train a Qwen3.5-4B tool-using agent for the math task and evaluate it on five benchmarks of varying difficulty. Under the GPU-hour budget of full-parameter GRPO's best validation checkpoint, CoPES recovers 92% of GRPO's validation-accuracy gain, versus 67% for standard ES, while its theoretical GPU memory requirement is less than one-eighth that of full-parameter GRPO. It consistently outperforms standard ES and LoRA-based GRPO on all evaluated pass@k metrics across the five benchmarks. Additional experiments further show the advantage of CoPES on the question-answering task. These results demonstrate an improved trade-off between memory requirements and training time for agentic LLM post-training under resource constraints. The code is open-sourced in https://github.com/MetaronWang/CoPES

</details>


### [124] [Real-Time Detection and Repair of LLM Agent Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02464)
*Sunny Dubey*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM智能体失败检测：仅用步级遥测（telemetry）和微秒级监控器（one-class ESN集成+CUSUM告警），在不依赖昂贵裁判LLM的情况下检测智能体中途失败，并将检测闭环到修复（回滚重跑），以极低成本（每步约200微秒）将任务成功率从52%提升到73%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在中途执行中经常失败（循环调用、工具错误级联、目标漂移、结果伪造、吸收损坏内容等），而标准补救方案是用第二个LLM对每一步进行评判，其成本甚至超过智能体本身。作者希望探索仅凭可观测的步骤遥测数据，能否实现低成本且有效的失败检测。

Method: 提出两层次检测系统：（1）监控层：基于one-class echo-state network（ESN）集成+CUSUM告警，仅使用步级遥测（cost微秒级），仅在健康运行上训练，实现无监督异常检测；（2）验证层：确定性验证（deterministic verification），重算运行声明的总量与实际收到的工具结果是否一致，并确认所有必需调用均被执行，无任何误报且无需训练。检测后闭环到修复：标记失败运行并回滚重跑。

Result: 在2,823个智能体episode（3个框架、3个本地模型和1个商业API）上，ESN监控器在5%误报预算下检测出0.71的失败（AUROC 0.872）；确定性验证在0/63误报下捕获60%失败（含覆盖检查时96%），且在1,825个健康episode上0误报。修复环节恢复45%失败（vs 16%对照，p=0.0005），任务成功率从52%提升到73%，每步监控成本约200微秒，比裁判调用低三个数量级。

Conclusion: 仅凭步级遥测即可实现高性价比的失败检测，显著优于记忆无关基线（记忆增强随失败后时间跨度单调增长，+0.09到+0.40）；监控需要部署时健康校准（冷启动不迁移，AUROC 0.527 vs 0.885），但确定性验证层不承担此负担且零误报；整体系统可将检测闭环为修复，实现低成本高质量的任务成功率提升。

Abstract: LLM agents fail mid-episode -- they loop, cascade tool errors, drift off goal, fabricate results, or silently absorb corrupted content -- and the standard remedy, judging every step with a second LLM, costs more than the agent itself. We ask how much detection is achievable from observable step telemetry alone, using monitors costing microseconds per step and trained only on healthy runs.
  On 2,823 committed agent episodes across three frameworks, three local models (qwen2.5 7b/3b, llama3.1 8b) and a commercial API (gemini-2.5-flash), a one-class echo-state-network ensemble with CUSUM alarms detects 0.71 of failures at a 5% false-alarm budget (AUROC 0.872). Its advantage over a memoryless baseline is a monotone function of post-onset horizon (+0.09 at <=3 steps, +0.40 at >=9), predicting its own failure region out of sample on AFTraj-2K. Ranking transfers with no retraining to two corpora from other groups (AFTraj-2K 0.745, ATBench 0.779).
  Monitors carry two burdens: a per-deployment healthy null (they do not transfer -- AUROC 0.527 cold against 0.885 recalibrated) and a residual false-alarm rate. We add a layer carrying neither: deterministic verification, which recomputes a run's stated total from the tool results it actually received and confirms every required call was made. Head-to-head it catches 60% of failures (96% with the coverage check) at 0 of 63 false positives against the monitor's 54% at 17%, transfers unchanged to llama3.1:8b (110 of 110 at 0 of 10), and trips on 0 of 1825 healthy episodes.
  Detection is then closed into repair: each flagged run is rolled back and re-run live, recovering 45% of failures against a 16% resampling control (p=0.0005) and lifting task success from 52% to 73% for about one extra model call per run. The system runs at ~200 microseconds per step, three orders of magnitude below a judge call. Code, traces and results are released.

</details>


### [125] [MonitrLLM: A Community-Centered Evaluation Infrastructure for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02409)
*Victor Ojewale,Ro Encarnación,Suresh Venkatasubramanian,Danaé Metaxa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MonitrLLM，一种开源基础设施，用于将完整对话记录与用户定义的任务意图和结果评估联系起来，弥补现有LLM评估中缺少"目标驱动的用户中心评估"的空白。通过为期两周、26名大学生使用ChatGPT的试点研究，收集206份带完整对话记录的评估报告，发现高满意度(4.19/5)与23.1%的任务失败率并存，且多轮对话的失败率是单轮对话的2.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM评估存在关键空白：基准套件评估在受控任务上的能力、大规模对话语料捕捉自然使用但无用户反馈、界面内反馈机制记录满意度但无任务目的——三者都无法将交互轨迹与用户定义的结果联系起来。

Method: 开发MonitrLLM开源基础设施，将完整对话记录、用户报告的任务意图和结果评估三者作为主要评估信号统一链接；并开展两周试点研究（26名大学生，使用ChatGPT，206份评估报告含完整对话记录）验证其可行性。

Result: 试点研究发现：用户平均满意度高达4.19/5，但目标任务失败率高达23.1%；多轮对话的失败率是单轮对话的2.5倍，表明长时间交互应被视为困难信号而非参与度信号。

Conclusion: 将直接的用户反馈与观测性对话数据结合，能够提供更稳健、更全面的LLM评估；MonitrLLM展示了构建支持这一目标的基础设施的可能性，呼吁社区推动该方向的发展。

Abstract: Benchmark suites assess model capability on controlled tasks; large-scale conversation corpora capture naturalistic use without user feedback; and in-interface feedback mechanisms record satisfaction without task purpose. Together, they leave a critical gap in LLM evaluation: no existing infrastructure routinely links interaction trajectories to user-defined outcomes. We introduce MonitrLLM, open-source infrastructure for community-centered LLM evaluations that links full conversation transcripts to user-reported task intent and outcome assessments, treating all three as primary evaluative signals rather than optional metadata.
  To demonstrate the value of this approach, we conducted a two-week feasibility pilot with 26 college students using ChatGPT, collecting 206 evaluation reports with full conversation transcripts. The findings from our pilot demonstrate the value of connecting conversation trajectories with user-reported outcomes. For instance, despite reporting high average satisfaction (4.19/5) with their LLM interactions, participants also experience a substantial 23.1% failure rate on their goal tasks. We also find that multi-turn conversations are reported as failing at 2.5 times the rate of single-turn exchanges, a pattern that reframes extended interaction as a signal of difficulty rather than engagement. We conclude by discussing the value of incorporating direct user feedback with observational data for robust LLM evaluations, and the possibilities for infrastructure that enables this goal.

</details>


### [126] [Right Answer, Wrong Method: Shortcut Hacking Misleads the Evaluation of LLM Reasoning on Frontier Science Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02442)
*Xuan Ren,Weiqi Zhai,Tianle Pu,Yihua Zhu,Yihua Zhu,Hu Wei,Bing Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了大型语言模型在科学推理基准测试中的"解法黑客"（Solution Hacking）失败模式：模型通过数值搜索、枚举、猜测或"先验证后作答"等无效捷径获得正确答案，而非真正的任务推理。随着题目难度提升，该现象从2.2%上升至28.3%（奥林匹克级别）和37.4%（HLE），前沿模型中8.2%-44.1%的"正确答案"实为黑客解法。作者提出专家启发的反黑客策略（自动评判器+测试时指令），能有效抑制捷径行为并降低表面准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学推理基准仅以最终答案准确率评估LLM，但正确答案并不代表模型具备目标推理能力。作者希望揭示"解法黑客"这一被忽视的失败模式，证明仅基于答案的评估会高估前沿LLM的科学推理能力。

Method: 作者系统地跨难度级别、科学领域和前沿模型分析解法黑客现象，识别出数值搜索、枚举、猜测、答案优先验证等无效捷径。进一步设计了专家启发的反黑客策略：一个自动评判器（automatic judge）和测试时指令（test-time instruction）来抑制捷径行为。

Result: 解法黑客随难度显著上升：常见问题2.2%、奥林匹克级28.3%、HLE 37.4%。前沿模型中被判定正确的答案中8.2%-44.1%为黑客解法。反黑客策略在抑制捷径行为时显著降低报告的准确率，但对正确且非黑客解法的准确率影响较小。

Conclusion: 仅基于答案的评估会系统性地高估前沿LLM的科学推理能力，必须在基准评测中纳入解法验证机制，以区分真正的推理与捷径式"作弊"。

Abstract: Scientific reasoning benchmarks typically evaluate large language models (LLMs) using final-answer accuracy. However, a correct answer does not necessarily demonstrate the reasoning capability targeted by the problem. We identify Solution Hacking, a failure mode in which an LLM reaches the correct answer through invalid shortcuts, such as numerical search, enumeration, guessing, or answer-first verification, without providing a valid task-targeted derivation. We systematically analyze this phenomenon across difficulty levels, scientific domains, and frontier models. Solution hacking increases sharply with benchmark difficulty, from 2.2\% on common problems to 28.3\% on Olympiad-level problems and 37.4\% on HLE. Moreover, 8.2\%-44.1\% of answers credited as correct across frontier models are identified as hacked solutions. We further develop expert-inspired anti-hacking strategies, including an automatic judge and a test-time instruction. The results show that suppressing shortcut behavior substantially reduces reported accuracy while having a smaller effect on correct and non-hacked accuracy. These findings reveal that answer-only evaluation can overestimate the scientific reasoning capabilities of frontier LLMs.

</details>


### [127] [ParEvalLayer: When Partial LLM-Agent Evaluations Support a Decision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02444)
*Wei-Jung Huang,Bonan Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ParEvalLayer，一个决策层系统，用于在LLM智能体基准测试部分完成时提前判断两个智能体系统的优劣对比。通过回放已完成基准数据模拟提前终止，ParEvalLayer在部分基准上仅需15%-25%的任务结果即可与完整评估得出相同结论，论文强调应同时报告决策规则和未决比较数量，而非仅报告部分分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体评估的任务结果通常远早于整个基准运行完成时产生，研究者倾向于提前报告部分分数，但部分分数无法展示已观察任务是否支持与完整评估相同的结论：前期任务可能遗漏基准的重要部分，先跑廉价任务会扭曲样本，只判定容易对比的规则看起来准确却留下大量未解决的比较。因此需要一种方法来判断部分评估结果是否可靠。

Method: 提出ParEvalLayer决策层，读取两个智能体系统的配对结果并应用预先选择的比较策略。对于每次部分运行，它记录四种状态：被测系统在所需程度上更优、在所需程度上不更优、需要更多证据、或应当弃权。通过回放已完成的公开基准数据模拟每个评估提前停止，在每个时间点仅使用已观察结果应用策略。

Result: 在主要比较规则下，三个公开基准在仅观察15%至25%的任务结果时就与完整评估得出相同决策；其他基准需要更多任务结果。这种差异表明不同基准的提前决策可靠性差异显著，部分分数本身不足以代表最终结论。

Conclusion: 部分得分单独报告是不够的，评估报告还应说明所用的决策规则以及仍有多少比较未做出决策，以提供更完整和可解释的评估信息。

Abstract: LLM-agent evaluations often produce task outcomes long before the full benchmark run is complete. A partial score is tempting to report, but it does not show whether the observed tasks support the same conclusion as the completed evaluation. Early tasks can omit important parts of a benchmark, running cheaper tasks first can distort the observed sample, and a rule that decides only easy pairs can appear accurate while leaving many comparisons unresolved. We introduce ParEvalLayer, a decision layer that reads paired outcomes for two agent systems and a comparison policy chosen in advance. For each partial run, it records whether the tested agent system is better by the required amount, is not better by that amount, needs more evidence, or should abstain. We evaluate ParEvalLayer by replaying completed public benchmark data as if each evaluation had stopped earlier. At each point, ParEvalLayer applies the policy using only the outcomes observed so far; if it reaches one of the two comparison judgments, we check whether that judgment matches the completed data for the same system pair. With the main comparison rule, three of the public benchmarks reach the same decision as the completed evaluation after observing only 15% to 25% of task outcomes. Other benchmarks require more task outcomes. This variation shows why a partial score alone is not enough: reports should also state the decision rule and how many comparisons remain without a decision.

</details>


### [128] [AtumAI: A Principled Framework for Agentic Generation of Datacenter Control-Plane Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02569)
*Qiushi Lin,Chaojie Zhang,Íñigo Goiri,Aditya Akella,Ricardo Bianchini,Jovan Stojkovic*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AtumAI是一个基于智能体AI的数据中心控制平面策略生成框架，通过Datacenter Task Compiler将自然语言目标形式化为可搜索的规范，并利用Evolutionary Design Discovery Loop（结合扩散模型、进化算法和代理模型）扩展候选策略搜索空间，使策略生成过程变得形式化、可迁移且系统化。在负载放置、资源缩放和功耗管理三个任务上，AtumAI生成的策略均优于专家设计的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据中心效率依赖控制平面策略，但设计这些策略越来越困难：软硬件栈快速演进、设计空间庞大且相互依赖、单个策略的原型开发需要数月时间。现成的智能体AI在三个方面存在不足：非形式化（缺乏结构化、可搜索的问题陈述，硬约束无法保证）、不可迁移（每个任务从零开始求解）、非系统性（依赖LLM作为唯一候选来源，探索范围狭窄且容易陷入局部最优）。

Method: AtumAI包含两个核心组件：(1) Datacenter Task Compiler（数据中心任务编译器），将用户用自然语言表达的目标自动编译为形式化、可机器校验、可搜索的任务规范（包括目标、约束、决策变量和评估方法）；(2) Evolutionary Design Discovery Loop（进化式设计发现循环），通过扩散模型、进化算法和代理模型在该规范中进行搜索，将候选策略空间扩展到LLM输出之外。

Result: AtumAI在三个不同问题范围、设计空间和权衡的控制平面任务（工作负载放置、资源缩放、功耗管理）上进行了评估，在所有任务中，AtumAI生成的策略都一致地优于专家设计的基线策略。

Conclusion: AtumAI证明了通过智能体AI可以将新任务的上手时间从数月工程工作缩短为仅编写任务描述，同时使策略生成过程具备形式化保证、跨任务可迁移性和系统化搜索能力，为数据中心控制平面策略的自动化设计提供了可行路径。

Abstract: The efficiency of a datacenter rests on its control plane policies. Designing these policies is increasingly hard: the hardware-software stack grows fast, the design space is vast and interdependent, and prototyping a single policy takes months. Agentic AI promises to automate this search. Off the shelf, however, it falls short on three fronts. It is not formal: with no structured, searchable statement of the problem, the search has little structure to exploit and hard constraints are not guaranteed. It is not transferable: each task is solved from scratch, so nothing learned on one task carries to the next. Finally, it is not systematic: relying on the LLM as the sole source of candidates, it explores a narrow slice of the design space and settles into local optima. We introduce AtumAI, a framework that generates datacenter control-plane policies with agentic AI, making the process formal, transferable, and systematic. From a goal stated in plain language, AtumAI autonomously proposes, tests, and refines candidate policies until one satisfies the request. It does so through two components. The Datacenter Task Compiler automates problem formulation: it compiles the request into a formal, machine-checkable, and searchable specification of the task's objectives, constraints, decision variables, and evaluation methodology. The Evolutionary Design Discovery Loop then searches this specification, expanding the search beyond the LLM itself via a diffusion model, an evolutionary algorithm, and a surrogate model. Together, they reduce onboarding a new task from months of engineering to writing its description. We evaluate AtumAI on three control-plane tasks with distinct problem scopes, design spaces, and trade-offs: workload placement, resource scaling, and power management. Across all tasks, the policies generated by AtumAI consistently outperform expert-engineered baselines.

</details>
